In this paper, we propose a novel bandwidth allocation policy that exploits the heterogeneity of device computing capabilities to minimize total training time. Rather than distributing bandwidth among all selected devices simultaneously, the proposed policy partitions the participating devices into ordered subsets and sequentially grants each subset exclusive access to the full bandwidth. We formally prove that this partitioning-based policy achieves a strictly lower training time than any bandwidth allocation scheme without partitioning, irrespective of the underlying scheduling algorithm. Furthermore, by reducing per-device transmission duration, the proposed policy also minimizes uplink energy consumption, which is particularly beneficial for battery-constrained IIoT devices. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets - including GC10-Det, an industrial surface defect benchmark, and CIFAR-10, a standard image classification benchmark - demonstrate that the proposed policy consistently reduces training time and energy consumption compared to existing bandwidth allocation schemes, approaching the theoretical lower bound on round time.

</details>


### [73] [Zero Collapse: A Failure Mode of Policy Gradient Methods in Discontinuous Reward Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30896)
*Nishant Kumar,Enrique Areyan Viqueira,Amy Greenwald*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了强化学习在重复拍卖竞价中的"零坍塌"失败模式：由于拍卖回报的陡峭不连续性，随机探索和梯度更新导致策略越过最优高回报区域，陷入零回报平坦区域后因缺乏梯度信号而难以恢复。作者提出了缓解策略并建立了拍卖环境的正式RL框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重复拍卖中的竞价问题结合了连续控制与数字广告的策略复杂性，而策略梯度和基于价值的方法在拍卖回报的"悬崖状"不连续景观中表现不佳，需要理解并解决其根本性失效机制。

Method: 通过理论分析识别出"零坍塌"失效模式，解释不连续回报如何导致梯度信号消失；分析策略随机性与步长之间的相互作用；在REINFORCE和演员-评论员变体上进行实证演示，并提出初始化与架构选择的缓解策略。

Result: 发现演员-评论员方法尤其易受零坍塌影响，因为偏倚的价值估计会加速向不稳定区域的移动；实证展示了该现象在多种算法中的普遍性。

Conclusion: 提出了拍卖环境的形式化RL框架，强调了其独特的结构特性，并给出了实用的稳定性提升策略。

Abstract: Bidding in repeated auctions is a central challenge for reinforcement learning (RL), combining continuous control with the strategic complexities of digital advertising. While policy gradient and value-based methods seem well-suited for these settings, they often struggle with the discontinuous, "cliff-like" nature of auction reward landscapes. In a first-price auction, for example, a bidder receives zero reward until they cross a specific threshold, after which the reward decreases as the bid increases. This creates a landscape of flat, zero-reward regions separated by sharp boundaries.
  We identify a fundamental failure mode in this setting termed "zero collapse." We show that stochastic exploration and gradient-based updates can cause policies to overshoot optimal high-reward regions and enter flat, zero-reward regimes. Once there, the lack of an informative gradient signal makes recovery extremely sample-inefficient, effectively trapping the agent. We find that actor-critic methods are particularly susceptible, as biased value estimates can accelerate this movement toward unstable regions.
  Our contributions include: (1) a mechanistic explanation of how discontinuous rewards lead to vanishing signals and zero collapse; (2) an analysis of the interaction between policy stochasticity and step size; and (3) an empirical demonstration of this phenomenon across REINFORCE and actor-critic variants. We propose practical mitigation strategies involving initialization and architectural choices to improve stability. Finally, we introduce a formal RL framework for auction environments highlighting their unique structural properties.

</details>


### [74] [Density-Guided Robust Counterfactual Explanations on Tabular Data under Model Multiplicity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30901)
*Jun Tan,Qing Guo,Zicheng Xu,Jinglin Li,Qi Fang,Ning Gui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 DensityFlow，一种基于神经ODE和密度分数引导的生成式反事实解释框架，通过高置信度数据流形生成鲁棒的反事实解释，并在黑箱场景下引入局部代理蒸馏机制，在保证有效性的同时显著降低查询成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反事实解释在低密度区域由于分类器高方差导致可靠性不足，现有基于集成交集的方法代价高昂。

Method: 1) 将反事实生成建模为神经ODE参数化的连续时间动力学过程；2) 通过噪声对比估计学习可微密度分数，主动避开低密度区域；3) 在黑箱场景下引入局部代理蒸馏机制，沿反事实轨迹对齐轻量代理模型与目标模型。

Result: 在模型多重性下取得了更优的有效性，同时相比基于集成的基线方法大幅降低了查询成本。

Conclusion: DensityFlow通过密度引导的生成式框架有效解决了反事实解释在低密度区域的不稳定问题，实现了鲁棒且高效的反事实生成。

Abstract: Counterfactual explanations (CEs) are essential for actionable recourse, yet their reliability is often compromised in low-density regions, where classifiers exhibit high variance. Unlike existing methods that rely on expensive ensemble intersections to define stability, we propose \textit{DensityFlow}, a generative framework that constructs robust CEs by adhering to the high-confidence data manifold. Specifically, we model the counterfactual generation as continuous-time dynamics parameterized by Neural ODE, guided by a differentiable density score to actively avoid uncertain, low-density areas. This density score is learned via Noise Contrastive Estimation, effectively leveraging a $(K{+}1)$-way discriminator to estimate density ratios. For black-box settings, we introduce a local proxy distillation mechanism that aligns a lightweight surrogate with the target model strictly within the trajectory of CE generation, enabling efficient gradient-based optimization with minimal queries. Experiments demonstrate that \textit{DensityFlow} achieves superior validity under model multiplicity while significantly reducing query costs compared to ensemble-based baselines. Our implementation is available at https://github.com/G-AILab/DensityFlow.

</details>


### [75] [De-attribute to Forget for LLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30919)
*Xinyang Lu,Jiabao Pan,Rachael Hwee Ling Sim,See-Kiong Ng,Anthony Kum Hoe Tung,Bryan Kian Hsiang Low*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于数据归因奖励的大语言模型遗忘框架DareU，通过强化学习降低模型生成内容对遗忘数据的归因分数，以平衡遗忘效果与模型效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM遗忘方法依赖优化预测损失（如最大化遗忘集损失），易导致过度遗忘和模型效用下降，亟需更优的遗忘框架。

Method: 将LLM遗忘的优化目标重新定义为清零数据归因，提出DareU框架：使用强化学习更新LLM，降低其生成响应相对于遗忘数据所有者的归因分数（即去归因），并用LLM分类器高效近似归因计算。

Result: 实验表明DareU在实现有效遗忘的同时，能够平衡遗忘质量与模型效用，优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 基于数据归因奖励的强化学习遗忘框架可有效解决传统方法的过度遗忘问题，为LLM遗忘提供了新思路。

Abstract: The rapid development of large language models (LLMs) has raised concerns on the use of inappropriate data for training, which has led to a growing interest in LLM unlearning. Many existing LLM unlearning approaches rely on optimizing prediction loss(es), such as maximizing the loss on the forget set, but often face critical issues like over-forgetting and poor model utility. To address them, this paper novelly frames the optimization objective for LLM unlearning as one of zeroing out data attribution instead. In particular, we propose the first LLM unlearning framework based on data attribution rewards called DareU that performs reinforcement learning to update the LLM by reducing the attribution score of its generated responses (i.e., de-attributing) to the forget data owners. Empirical evaluation using an LLM classifier as an efficient approximation of attribution shows that DareU outperforms existing baselines by achieving effective unlearning while balancing forget quality and model utility well.

</details>


### [76] [Spectral Anatomy of Quantum Gaussian Process Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30952)
*Jian Xu,Chao Li,Guang Lin,Yuning Qiu,Delu Zeng,John Paisley,Qibin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出归一化谱熵 $S(K)/\log n$ 是统一解释量子高斯过程（QGP）中HHL回归去量子化与量子核后验病理学两个看似独立现象的核心诊断量。通过理论证明（Cauchy-Schwarz Nyström误差界、有限样本方差-收缩恒等式、目标依赖最优熵）和大规模实验（模拟器与IBM Heron硬件，无误差缓解，中位误差3.2%），验证了该诊断量对多种核族（硬件高效、matchgate、IQP、RBF等）具有跨平台可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两个独立的最新结果分别否定了HHL-based QGP回归的指数加速和揭示量子核后验病理学打破贝叶斯优化，表面看似无关。本文旨在揭示这两个现象受同一核心量（归一化谱熵）支配，从而建立统一的理论理解框架。

Method: 理论层面：证明了Cauchy-Schwarz形式的Nyström近似误差上界、用Bach自由度$d_σ(K)$表达的有限样本方差-收缩恒等式、以及通过核本征基中目标内在维度刻画目标依赖最优熵。实验层面：在模拟器和IBM Heron量子硬件（$n_q=4$和$n_q=6$，24种配置，无误差缓解）上验证了多种核族（HE、matchgate、IQP、RBF/Matérn/RFF/深度核）的谱熵曲线的普适性和可迁移性。

Result: 模拟器上所有核族在去量子化、ECE和方差-收缩面板上均坍缩到相同的$S/\log n$曲线。NLL最优工作点：平滑目标对应高熵，带限量子数据目标对应低熵。硬件迁移：IBM Heron上中位绝对误差3.2%，均值5.2%（$n_q=4$）；第二个后端均值误差2.7%；$n_q=6$扩展均值误差1.7%。仅一个HE配置出现30%异常值，重跑后降至0.5%（归因于校准漂移）。

Conclusion: 归一化谱熵是QGP中核可表达性与可训练性的普适诊断量，统一解释了HHL回归去量子化与量子核后验病理学，且对多种核族和量子硬件平台具有稳定可迁移性，无需误差缓解即可在实际量子设备上有效工作。

Abstract: Two recent results have reshaped quantum Gaussian processes (QGPs). On the one hand, \citet{lowe2025assessing} rule out the exponential speedups claimed by HHL-based QGP regression in the typical, well-conditioned regime; on the other, an independent line of work shows that highly expressive quantum kernels suffer posterior pathologies that break Bayesian optimization. We show that these seemingly unrelated phenomena are governed by the same quantity: the normalized spectral entropy $S(K)/\log n$ of the kernel Gram matrix. We prove a Cauchy--Schwarz tail bound on Nyström approximation error, a finite-sample variance-contraction identity in terms of Bach's degrees of freedom $d_σ(K)$, and a characterization of the \emph{target-dependent} optimal entropy via the intrinsic dimension of the target in the kernel eigenbasis. Empirically, the diagnostic is kernel-agnostic: hardware-efficient, matchgate, IQP \emph{and} RBF/Matérn/RFF/deep-kernel families all collapse onto identical $S/\log n$ curves on dequantization, ECE, and variance-contraction panels. The NLL sweet spot lives at high entropy for smooth targets and at low entropy for band-limited quantum-data targets. The diagnostic transfers from simulator to IBM Heron hardware with median absolute error $3.2\%$ and mean $5.2\%$ in $S/\log n$ across $24$ configurations at $n_q = 4$, with matchgate and IQP within $5\%$ mean and a single HE configuration returning a $30\%$ outlier that drops to $0.5\%$ on rerun (attributed to calibration drift); the same diagnostic transfers to a second Heron backend (mean error $2.7\%$) and to a $n_q = 6$ scale-up on the original backend (mean error $1.7\%$). No error mitigation is applied throughout.

</details>


### [77] [Parallel Tempering Initial Sampling in Inference-Time Reward Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30991)
*Myeongjun Oh,Gwangho Kim,Sungyoon Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PATHS是一种基于并行回火的初始采样方法，通过耦合多条采样链并周期执行Metropolis交换，有效解决了扩散/流模型在复杂奖励景观中因多峰和稀缺性导致的模式捕获问题，显著提升推理时奖励对齐质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准SMC方法从标准先验初始化粒子，在复杂奖励景观中高奖励区域极为稀少，导致性能不佳；且现有奖励感知初始采样方法仍易陷入局部模式，因为复杂奖励景观往往多峰。需要一种能高效探索稀有高奖励区域的初始化方法。

Method: 提出PATHS方法，构建奖励温度阶梯的多条采样链，通过并行回火机制周期性地在链间执行Metropolis交换，从而在平坦化后的奖励景观中高效探索，缓解模式捕获问题。

Result: 在布局到图像生成和数量感知生成任务上，PATHS在复杂提示词下持续获得更好的对齐质量。

Conclusion: 并行回火初始采样能有效增强有限预算下对稀有高奖励区域的探索，是一种优于现有SMC及奖励感知初始化的推理时奖励对齐方法。

Abstract: Inference-time reward alignment steers pretrained diffusion and flow-based generative models to satisfy user-specified rewards without retraining. Recently, Sequential Monte Carlo (SMC) has emerged as a powerful framework for this task by iteratively filtering and propagating multiple particles. However, we show that standard SMC-based methods often suffer from poor performance because they initialize particles from a standard prior, whereas high-reward regions in complex reward landscapes are extremely rare. Further, we show that even recent reward-aware initial sampling approaches remain vulnerable to getting trapped in local modes, as complex reward landscapes are often multi-modal. To overcome these limitations, we propose PATHS (PArallel Tempering for High-complexity reward Sampling), a novel initialization method that couples multiple sampling chains through parallel tempering. PATHS maintains a ladder of reward-tempered chains and periodically performs Metropolis swaps, enabling efficient exploration across flattened reward landscapes, thereby mitigating the mode-trapping issues. Our analysis reveals that this mechanism substantially enhances the finite-budget exploration of rare, high-reward regions that are typically challenging to sample. Experiments on layout-to-image and quantity-aware generation show that PATHS achieves consistent gains in alignment quality, particularly on complex prompts.

</details>


### [78] [DEM: A Distilled Explanation Model for Interpretable Anomaly Detection in Physiological Sensor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31007)
*Jyotirmoy Singh,Anushka Roy,Shreea Bose,Chittaranjan Hota*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Distilled Explanation Model (DEM)，一种三阶段玻璃盒框架，通过将梯度提升专家模型的知识蒸馏到基于线性残差的决策树中，在生理传感异常检测任务上实现了AUC 0.9964，同时提供可读的if-then规则，推理速度比SHAP快1235倍，并引入蒸馏保真度指标量化解释可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生理传感器异常检测需要同时具备高预测精度和临床可解释性，现有黑盒模型虽性能强但缺乏透明性，而SHAP/LIME等事后解释方法本质上是近似而非模型自身的推理过程，且计算开销大，不适用于实时监控场景。

Method: DEM采用三阶段玻璃盒框架：1) 训练XGBoost梯度提升专家模型；2) 构建线性基线模型；3) 在残差(专家模型预测与线性基线之差)上训练可解释决策树，将非线性知识蒸馏为透明规则。同时提出蒸馏保真度指标，量化解释树捕捉专家模型非线性贡献的忠实程度。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV、WESAD、eICU及SmartNet WBAN四个数据集上，临床上下文异常检测AUC达0.9964，可穿戴压力检测AUC达0.9047；推理速度0.17ms/千样本，比SHAP快1235倍；消融实验验证了XGBoost蒸馏阶段相对于朴素残差拟合的优势，深度敏感性分析展示了可控的精度-可解释性权衡。

Conclusion: DEM在生理传感异常检测中同时实现了高精度、原生可解释性和实时推理能力，蒸馏保真度指标为解释可信度提供了量化评估，模型支持用户通过控制树深度显式地调节精度与可解释性之间的权衡，适合临床实时监测场景。

Abstract: Anomaly detection in physiological sensor data from Wireless Body Area Networks (WBANs) can be caused by sensor faults, network disruptions, or missing data, leading to false alarms. Hence, it demands both high predictive accuracy and clinically interpretable explanations. Existing approaches rely either on black-box models that achieve strong performance but offer no transparency, or on post-prediction explanation methods such as SHAP and LIME. In this paper, we propose the Distilled Explanation Model (DEM), a three-stage glass-box framework that distills the non-linear knowledge of a gradient boosting expert into an interpretable decision tree operating on residuals relative to a linear baseline, so that the explanation is not an approximation but the prediction itself. DEM introduces a novel distillation fidelity metric that quantifies how faithfully the explanation tree captures the expert model's non-linear contribution, providing a principled measure of explanation trustworthiness absent from prior interpretable models. Evaluated across four physiological datasets, including MIMIC-IV, WESAD, eICU, and an in-house SmartNet WBAN corpus, DEM achieves an AUC of 0.9964 on clinical contextual anomaly detection and 0.9047 on wearable stress detection while producing human-readable if-then rules at a controllable depth. Inference requires 0.17ms per 1000 samples, rendering DEM 1235x faster than SHAP-based post-hoc explanation and suitable for real-time physiological monitoring. Ablation studies confirm that the XGBoost distillation step provides measurable gains over naive residual fitting, and depth-sensitivity analysis demonstrates an explicit, user-controlled accuracy-interpretability trade-off unique to DEM among existing intrinsically interpretable models.

</details>


### [79] [Augmented Lagrangian Predictive Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31022)
*Jeffrey Seely,Julian Gould*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出增强拉格朗日预测编码（PC-ALM），通过每层局部拉格朗日乘子累积约束误差，使预测编码的局部更新梯度与反向传播对齐，在深度达128层非线性网络中匹配BP性能，尤其在PC表现不佳的深窄网络中效果显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测编码（PC）作为反向传播（BP）的局部学习替代方案，在深度网络中性能仍不及BP，尤其是在深窄网络中存在梯度传播缓慢、信用分配低效的问题。

Method: 提出PC-ALM算法，在每层引入局部拉格朗日乘子来累积约束误差，通过增广拉格朗日框架中的对偶上升驱动激活值的循环动态，替代传统PC的标量能量热流动态。

Result: 在线性PC网络中，PC-ALM收敛到精确BP梯度均衡；在非线性网络中（深度达128层），PC-ALM在所有宽度-深度组合下匹配BP性能，尤其在深窄网络中优于PC。信用信号在深度网络中呈现"弹道式"均匀传播，而非PC的缓慢扩散式传播。

Conclusion: PC-ALM弥合了局部学习与BP之间的性能差距，增广拉格朗日框架为理解分布式系统如何通过纯局部动力学计算和传播类BP信用信号提供了新视角。

Abstract: Predictive coding (PC) is a local-learning alternative to backpropagation (BP), training deep networks via local energy-minimization dynamics rather than a global backward pass. We introduce Augmented Lagrangian Predictive Coding (PC-ALM), which maintains PC's inference budget but aligns each weight update toward BP by accumulating per-layer constraint errors into a layer-local Lagrange multiplier. In linear PC networks, PC-ALM converges to an equilibrium with exact BP gradients distributed across the network via only layer-local updates. We analyze PC-ALM in nonlinear PC networks up to depth 128 and show that it matches BP performance across all width-depth regimes, notably in deep narrow networks where PC underperforms. PC-ALM introduces recurrent dynamics in each layer's activations. Compared to PC's heat flow on a scalar energy, PC-ALM dynamics are driven by dual ascent on the augmented Lagrangian. We observe "ballistic" credit propagation across very deep networks, with credit signals evenly distributed across layers, compared to PC's slow, diffusive credit propagation. Beyond the algorithm itself, the augmented Lagrangian framework offers a generalization of PC, and may yield insights into how distributed systems could compute and propagate BP-like credit signals through purely local dynamics.

</details>


### [80] [Multi-Scale Separable Fourier Neural Networks for Solving High-Frequency PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31027)
*Qihong Yang,Qiaolin He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种多尺度可分离傅里叶神经网络（MS-SFNN），通过独立子网络与固定随机权重、余弦激活实现高精度求解高频偏微分方程，并采用解析导数与批量QR分解解决内存瓶颈，实验表明该方法显著优于PINN和SV-SNN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于物理信息的神经网络（如PINN）在处理高频PDE时面临精度不足和训练困难的问题，且自动微分在大规模高维问题中造成严重的内存瓶颈，亟需一种既高效又精确的替代方案。

Method: MS-SFNN采用可分离架构：d维输入对应d个独立子网络，每个子网络仅作用于单一坐标，通过逐元素乘法构造基函数，PDE解表示为这些基函数的线性组合，系数由最小二乘确定。所有网络权重和偏置从均匀分布随机初始化一次后固定不变；引入可调缩放因子调节基函数频率内容；使用余弦激活函数嵌入傅里叶特征；用解析推导的基函数导数替代自动微分，并开发了内存高效的批量QR分解算法求解大规模最小二乘系统。

Result: 在多个具有挑战性的线性与非线性高频PDE数值实验中，MS-SFNN取得了前所未有的精度，显著超越了PINN和SV-SNN等现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: MS-SFNN通过可分离架构、固定随机权重与解析导数的结合，为高频PDE求解提供了一种高精度、内存高效的新范式，在计算效率和求解精度之间取得了优良平衡。

Abstract: We propose a novel neural network architecture, termed Multi-Scale Separable Fourier Neural Networks (MS-SFNN), for the accurate and efficient solution of linear and nonlinear high-frequency partial differential equations (PDEs). MS-SFNN exploits a separable representation: given a $d$-dimensional input, it employs $d$ independent subnetworks -- each acting on a single coordinate -- and constructs basis functions via element-wise multiplication of their outputs. The PDE solution is approximated as a linear combination of these basis functions, with coefficients determined by least squares. Critically, all network weights and biases are randomly initialized once, from a uniform distribution with unit variance, and remain fixed thereafter. To enhance expressivity, a tunable scaling factor is introduced in each subnetwork to modulate the frequency content of the resulting basis functions. Fourier features are explicitly embedded through cosine activations, endowing the method with strong spectral approximation capabilities. To mitigate the memory bottleneck associated with dense collocation in high-frequency or three-dimensional problems, we replace automatic differentiation with analytically derived basis function derivatives and develop a memory-efficient batched QR decomposition algorithm for solving large-scale least-squares systems. Numerical experiments demonstrate that MS-SFNN achieves unprecedented accuracy across a range of challenging PDEs, significantly outperforming state-of-the-art methods such as Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINN) and Separated-Variable Spectral Neural Networks (SV-SNN).

</details>


### [81] [Annealed Softmax Greedy in Many-Armed Bayesian Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31034)
*William Overman,Mohsen Bayati*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明，在贝叶斯多臂伯努利强盗问题中，若先验满足β-正则性（β=1，即存在大量近最优臂），退火softmax贪心策略（无显式不确定性追踪）可实现近最优的贝叶斯遗憾率 Õ(√T)，为无不确定性机制的RLVR/GRPO类策略的有效性提供了一种理论解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释为何RLVR和GRPO这类基于可验证奖励的强化学习方法虽然不显式追踪 epistemic 不确定性，却依然能有效工作的原因，为这类算法的有效性提供理论支撑。

Method: 在贝叶斯伯努利多臂强盗问题中，对退火softmax（Boltzmann）贪心策略进行理论分析。具体设定为：策略以softmax形式根据经验平均奖励选择动作，温度参数退火；先验满足β-正则性（β=1，即线性上尾条件，意味着存在大量近最优臂）。推导该策略的贝叶斯遗憾界。

Result: 当臂数 m = Θ(√T) 时，退火softmax贪心达到 Õ(√T) 的贝叶斯遗憾，这是该设定下的近最优率，与经验均值贪心策略相当。在β-正则性下，大量臂的经验均值始终接近最优值，因此softmax采样次优臂时倾向于近最优臂而非明显劣质臂。反之，臂数量少时软max策略可能遭受线性遗憾。

Conclusion: 退火softmax贪心在大量近最优臂环境下无需显式不确定性追踪即可达到近最优性能。这为RLVR提供了结构类比：基础策略以不可忽略的概率生成正确回答（扮演β-正则性的角色），使得采样其他完成时更可能接近正确，从而避免陷入明显劣质动作。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) and group-based policy optimization methods such as GRPO update a stochastic policy by sampling multiple completions per prompt and increasing the policy's probability on those with higher reward, regularized by a KL penalty toward a reference policy. These updates do not include explicit mechanisms that track epistemic uncertainty. This paper studies a stylized explanation for why such uncertainty-agnostic updates can nevertheless be effective. We analyze an annealed softmax (Boltzmann) policy that selects actions according to a softmax of empirical mean rewards in a many-armed Bayesian Bernoulli bandit. Under a linear upper-tail condition on the prior (the $β=1$ case of $β$-regularity), which implies an abundance of near-optimal arms, we prove that annealed softmax greedy achieves Bayes regret $\tilde{O}(m + T/m)$, and in particular $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{T})$ when the number of arms scales as $m = Θ(\sqrt{T})$. This is the near-optimal Bayes regret rate in this regime, attained also by empirical-mean greedy. Under $β$-regularity, many arms maintain empirical means close to the optimum throughout learning, so when softmax samples an arm other than the empirically best, that arm tends to be another near-optimal one rather than a clearly inferior one. By contrast, with a small number of arms, the same kind of softmax policy can suffer linear regret. The result also provides a structural analogy to RLVR, where a base policy with a non-negligible probability of producing a correct completion plays the role of $β$-regularity.

</details>


### [82] [Best-Arm Identification-Based Trust Region Selection for Bayesian Optimization on Multimodal Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31050)
*Nobuo Namura,Sho Takemori*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种轨迹感知框架，将最佳臂识别（BAI）与信任区域贝叶斯优化相结合，通过外推多个局部优化器的轨迹并逐步淘汰次优候选者，加速多模态优化问题的全局收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准高斯过程贝叶斯优化在复杂多模态或高维问题上性能下降，信任区域方法虽能缓解但区域选择效率仍有提升空间。

Method: 将多个局部初始化的优化器的优化轨迹进行外推以预测其最终性能，并利用最佳臂识别（BAI）逐步淘汰次优候选者，形成轨迹感知的信任区域BO框架。

Result: 理论上证明了所提方法在温和假设下比传统BO更快收敛到全局最优，实验上在合成和真实基准上验证了有效性。

Conclusion: BAI引导的信任区域BO能有效解决多模态优化问题，收敛速度优于传统BO方法。

Abstract: Gaussian process-based Bayesian optimization (BO) is a popular approach for expensive black-box optimization, but its performance often degrades on complex multimodal or high-dimensional problems. Trust region-based BO mitigates this issue by focusing on local regions, and recent studies suggest that selecting an effective region can be formulated as a multi-armed bandit problem. We propose a trajectory-aware framework that integrates best-arm identification (BAI) with trust region-based BO to efficiently solve multimodal optimization problems. Our method extrapolates the optimization trajectories of multiple locally initialized optimizers to predict their final performance and progressively eliminates suboptimal candidates via BAI. We theoretically show that the proposed BAI-guided BO converges faster to the global optimum than conventional BO under mild assumptions, and demonstrate its effectiveness through extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world benchmarks.

</details>


### [83] [STEP: Learning STructured Embeddings for Progressive Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31061)
*Lucas Thil,Jesse Read,Rim Kaddah,Guillaume Doquet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种自监督对比学习方法，通过学习低维潜在空间来建模渐进式时间序列（如退化或任务完成过程），该空间的几何结构本身即为解释：使用两个固定的正交原型向量锚定流形，潜在向量的极坐标(θ, r)分别追踪状态进展和活跃模式，无需代理标签。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列模型（尤其是黑箱深度模型）缺乏可解释性，无法揭示数据背后的状态演化机制，而渐进式时间序列（如工业退化、机器人任务、神经活动）需要透明的表征来理解不可逆的状态转换过程。

Method: 自监督对比学习框架；学习低维潜在空间，其几何结构被设计为可解释形式：每个观测对应流形上一点，流形锚定在两个固定正交原型向量之间；潜在向量的极坐标(θ, r)中，θ追踪状态进展（如健康到故障），r标识活跃模式（如运行条件）；无需代理标签。

Result: 在工业退化、机器人任务和神经活动等多个领域验证了三项能力：端状态预测、多步预测和可解释的相位分离；方法在各项任务上匹配或超越黑箱基线；基于潜在极坐标的简单线性回归器可与深度架构竞争，证明状态以几何可访问的形式编码。

Conclusion: 该方法成功将不可逆状态演化编码为几何可解释的潜在表示，为渐进式时间序列提供了透明且高性能的分析框架，同时验证了极坐标表征在捕捉状态进展和模式识别方面的有效性。

Abstract: We present a novel method for learning interpretable representations of progressive time series, that is, data capturing irreversible state transitions such as degradation or task completion. Our approach uses a self-supervised contrastive objective to learn a low-dimensional latent space whose geometry is itself the interpretation: each observation becomes a point on a manifold anchored between two fixed orthogonal prototype vectors, and a trajectory becomes a path across that manifold. From this structure we read a latent compass, the polar coordinates (θ, r) of the latent vector, in which θ tracks the progression of the underlying state (e.g., from healthy to failed) and r identifies the active mode (e.g., the operating condition), without any proxy labels. We evaluate the approach against the state of the art on diverse domains, including industrial degradation, robotic tasks, and neural activity, validating three key capabilities: (1) end-state prediction, (2) multi-step forecasting, and (3) interpretable phase separation. Our method matches or improves over black-box counterparts on all of these while providing transparency about the underlying mechanisms. A simple linear regressor on top of the latent compass coordinates is competitive with deep architectures, direct quantitative evidence that the underlying state is encoded in a geometrically accessible form.

</details>


### [84] [Scalable Bayesian Inference for Nonlinear Conservation Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31127)
*Tim Weiland,Philipp Hennig*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于高斯过程先验的数值保守方法，用于非线性守恒律的不确定性感知模拟，结合稀疏近似技术实现大规模正反问题求解，在前向模拟中提供结构化不确定性量化，在反问题中数秒内恢复非参数源场的后验分布，优于需要数分钟的神经网络基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性守恒律在实际应用中常受测量稀疏或噪声等不确定性来源影响，经典数值方法和现代深度学习方法均难以妥善处理这类不适定问题。

Method: 将经典数值方法框架为高斯过程先验下的贝叶斯推断，结合稀疏近似技术实现大规模扩展，开发数值保守的不确定性感知模拟方法。

Result: 前向模拟继承了经典求解器的精度并提供结构化不确定性量化；反问题中可在数秒内恢复非参数源场的后验分布，优于需要数分钟且仅能提供点估计的神经网络基线方法。

Conclusion: 所提方法在保持数值保守性的同时，有效实现了对非线性守恒律中不确定性的量化，在效率和精度上均优于现有基线方法。

Abstract: Nonlinear conservation laws are at the heart of many of the most important dynamical systems in science and engineering. In practical applications, such systems are often subject to various sources of uncertainty, e.g. due to sparse or noisy measurements. Inferring physical quantities and fields of interest then becomes an ill-posed problem which both classical numerical methods and modern deep learning-based methods struggle to treat appropriately. Recent work has framed classical numerical methods as Bayesian inference under Gaussian process priors, resulting in a physics-aware treatment of uncertainties. Following this line of work, we develop a novel numerically conservative method for uncertainty-aware simulations of nonlinear conservation laws. We use recent sparse approximation techniques to scale up to large-scale forward and inverse problems. For forward simulation, we inherit the accuracy of classical solvers while providing structured uncertainty quantification. On inverse problems, we recover posteriors over nonparametric source fields in seconds -- outperforming neural baselines that take minutes to produce a less accurate point estimate.

</details>


### [85] [Generalizing Multi-Scale Time-Series Modeling with a Single Operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31129)
*Cheonwoo Lee,Dooho Lee,Doyun Choi,Jaemin Yoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SiGMA，一种基于可学习离散高斯核的统一多尺度时间序列预测架构，解决了现有方法依赖固定离散缩放的局限性，在长短期预测中达到SOTA，训练速度快5.3倍，内存节省3.8倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列多尺度建模方法缺乏统一的理论基础，且依赖固定离散的缩放方式，无法捕捉距离感知的连续时间动态。

Method: 将现有缩放方法统一为缩放算子族，提出基于尺度空间理论的可学习离散高斯（LDG）核来实现距离感知缩放，构建单一广义多尺度架构（SiGMA）。

Result: 在长短期预测基准上超越所有SOTA多尺度基线，16组长短期设置中13组最优；训练速度最高提升5.3倍，内存消耗降低最多3.8倍。

Conclusion: SiGMA通过可学习的高斯核实现了统一且高效的多尺度时间序列预测，既提升了预测精度，又显著改善了计算效率。

Abstract: Multi-scale modeling has emerged as an effective design principle for time-series forecasting by capturing temporal dynamics at multiple resolutions. As no principled foundation has been established in the literature, we unify existing scaling methods into a scaling operator family, revealing a fundamental limitation of existing approaches: reliance on fixed and discrete scaling. To address this limitation, we propose SiGMA (Single Generalized Multi-scale Architecture), which enables distance-aware scaling via the learnable discrete Gaussian (LDG) kernel grounded in scale-space theory. We evaluate SiGMA comprehensively on long- and short-term forecasting benchmarks against state-of-the-art multi-scale baselines. SiGMA outperforms all competitors on both tasks, especially achieving the best performance in 13 out of 16 long-term evaluation settings. Beyond accuracy, SiGMA significantly improves training speed by up to 5.3 times and reduces memory consumption by up to 3.8 times over the strongest competitors. Code is available at https://github.com/cheonwoolee/SiGMA.

</details>


### [86] [TabCausal: Pretraining Across Causal Environments for Tabular Causal Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31156)
*Zi-Rong Li,Si-Yang Liu,Tian-Zuo Wang,Han-Jia Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TabCausal是一种因果发现基础模型（CDFM），通过广泛的因果预训练策略，在多样化的因果环境（包括图先验、结构机制、噪声模型、维度、样本量和干预机制）上进行动态任务构建，实现了从观测数据和混合干预数据中可迁移的因果结构学习，在大型合成基准和语义因果环境基准上均优于多种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果发现基础模型（CDFMs）性能有限，常无法一致地匹配强经典方法，关键瓶颈在于因果预训练任务的构建方式不够有效。

Method: 提出TabCausal，采用数据驱动的动态任务构建策略，在多样化的图先验、结构机制、噪声模型、维度、样本量和干预机制上进行广泛因果预训练；同时引入协议引导且经LLM审计的语义因果环境基准，用于评估分布外泛化能力。

Result: 在大规模合成基准上，TabCausal的宏平均性能优于多种因果发现基线；在语义因果环境基准上，尤其在干预证据下，展现出稳健的结构恢复能力。

Conclusion: 广泛的因果预训练是实现可迁移的摊销式因果发现的关键因素，TabCausal验证了该策略的有效性。

Abstract: Causal discovery aims to recover directed causal relations from observational and interventional data, providing a basis for mechanistic understanding and reliable decision-making. Causal discovery foundation models (CDFMs) seek to amortize this problem by mapping a dataset directly to a causal graph in a single forward pass, avoiding per-dataset testing, search, or optimization. However, existing CDFMs remain limited, often failing to consistently match strong classical methods, and we find that a key bottleneck is how causal pretraining tasks are constructed. Based on this observation, we propose TabCausal, a data-driven CDFM trained with broad causal pretraining over diverse graph priors, structural mechanisms, noise models, dimensions, sample sizes, and intervention regimes. A dynamic task construction strategy composes these causal environments into varied discovery tasks, enabling more transferable structural learning from observational and mixed-interventional data. On large-scale synthetic benchmarks, TabCausal achieves better macro-averaged performance than a diverse set of causal discovery baselines. To further bridge abstract synthetic generators and realistic causal reasoning scenarios, we introduce a protocol-guided and LLM-audited semantic causal environment benchmark, where domain-grounded SCMs generate interpretable observational and interventional datasets for out-of-distribution analysis. Across both synthetic and semantic environments, TabCausal demonstrates robust structure recovery, especially under interventional evidence, highlighting broad causal pretraining as a key ingredient for transferable amortized causal discovery.

</details>


### [87] [Convergence of Two-Timescale Markovian Stochastic Approximations with Applications in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31172)
*Vagul Mahadevan,Claire Chen,Shuze Daniel Liu,Shangtong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了双时间尺度随机逼近算法在马尔可夫噪声下的稳定性与收敛性，并首次证明了带资格迹的TDC算法在离策略线性函数逼近下的几乎必然收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的双时间尺度SA算法的稳定性与收敛性分析仅适用于i.i.d.噪声场景，而强化学习中更常见的是马尔可夫噪声。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 核心创新在于使用慢时间尺度参数的运行最大值（running max）来控制快时间尺度参数，而非传统方法中使用当前慢时间尺度参数。无需使用投影算子，也无需噪声定义在紧致空间上。

Result: 建立了双时间尺度SA算法在马尔可夫噪声下的稳定性与收敛性；首次证明了带资格迹的TDC算法在离策略线性函数逼近下的几乎必然收敛。

Conclusion: 该工作为更实际的RL场景（马尔可夫噪声）下的双时间尺度算法提供了严格的理论保障，推动了TDC等算法的理论分析进展。

Abstract: This work studies the convergence of two-timescale stochastic approximations (SA), a class of iterative algorithms that update two sets of parameters in fast and slow timescales respectively. Notable examples of two-timescale SA in reinforcement learning (RL) include temporal difference learning with gradient correction (TDC) and actor-critic methods. Previously, the stability (i.e., boundedness) and convergence of two-timescale SA were only established under i.i.d. noise. This work instead establishes the stability and convergence of two-timescale SA under Markovian noise, a setup that is more realistic in RL. Notably, we do not need to use any projection operator and the noise does not need to live in a compact space. Our key technical novelty is to control the fast timescale parameter with the running max of the slow timescale parameter, instead of with the current slow timescale parameter, as most prior works do. As a key application, we establish the first almost sure convergence of TDC with eligibility traces under off-policy learning with linear function approximation.

</details>


### [88] [Student Capacity Moderates Knowledge Distillation Effectiveness: A Systematic Study Across ResNet Teacher-Student Pairs on CIFAR-10](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31191)
*Umut Onur Yasar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了教师-学生模型容量关系对知识蒸馏（KD）效果的影响，在CIFAR-10上使用ResNet系列进行了三类师生配对实验，发现：学生容量是调节蒸馏增益的关键因素；实现正确性（如梯度裁剪bug）会严重影响Feature-KD的性能；输入分辨率匹配的架构是有效蒸馏的前提条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究教师-学生网络容量关系如何调节知识蒸馏的有效性，澄清在何种条件下KD能带来显著增益，并揭示实现细节和架构设计对蒸馏效果的潜在影响。

Method: 在CIFAR-10上使用ResNet系列（R50→R18、R34→R18、R50→R34）进行三类师生配对实验，对比Logit-KD和Feature-KD两种蒸馏方法，采用3个随机种子并报告均值±标准差以确保可重复性。

Result: 学生容量是蒸馏增益的关键调节因素：R34学生受益显著高于R18学生；梯度裁剪bug（排除投影层）会压制Feature-KD性能，修正后Feature-KD在三分之二的配对中达到或超越Logit-KD（R50→R34达95.55%，基线95.25%）；修正32x32输入的ResNet stem使教师准确率提升超过5pp，远超任何KD增益。

Conclusion: 知识蒸馏的有效性受学生容量、实现正确性和架构匹配度等多重因素调节，研究者需严格控制这些因素以获得可靠的KD比较结果。

Abstract: We investigate how teacher-student capacity relationships modulate knowledge distillation (KD) effectiveness in ResNet-based image classification on CIFAR-10. Across three teacher-student pairs -- R50->R18, R34->R18, and R50->R34 -- we compare Logit-KD and Feature-KD under controlled, reproducible conditions (3 seeds, mean+/-std reported throughout). We report three main findings. First, student capacity is a key moderating factor in distillation gain: R34 students benefit substantially more from KD than R18 students even when teacher-student accuracy gaps are comparable, with the strongest gain of +0.30pp observed for R50->R34 Feature-KD versus +0.18pp for R34->R18 Feature-KD and +0.00pp for R34->R18 Logit-KD. Second, implementation correctness critically affects Feature-KD: a gradient clipping bug that excluded projection layers suppressed Feature-KD performance and produced misleading comparisons with Logit-KD. After correction, Feature-KD matches or outperforms Logit-KD in two of three pairs, reaching 95.55% on R50->R34 against a baseline of 95.25%. Third, input-resolution-aware architecture is a prerequisite for effective distillation: correcting the ResNet stem for 32x32 inputs raises teacher accuracy by over 5pp -- an order of magnitude larger than any KD gain. All code and results are available at github.com/umutonuryasar/kd-capacity-gap.

</details>


### [89] [Geometry-based Schrödinger Bridges for Trustworthy Multimodal Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31193)
*Jiayu Xiong,Jing Wang,Qi Zhang,Wanlong Wang,Jun Xue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于几何的多模态融合方法GMF，利用扩散施罗丁格桥传输的平方初速度作为独立于模型预测的可靠性信号，有效检测低质量数据（噪声、不完整、冲突输入），打破传统置信度方法的循环依赖问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可信融合方法依赖模型自身预测置信度来判断数据质量，形成循环依赖：当模型自信但错误时，这些方法无法检测错误。需要一种独立于模型预测的可靠性评估机制。

Method: 提出Geometry-based Multimodal Fusion (GMF)，通过计算输入在潜空间中所需的传输校正量来评估可靠性。采用扩散施罗丁格桥传输（Diffusion Schrödinger Bridge）结合Rectified Flow实现，将平方初速度作为高效学习的校正分数：有效数据平方速度幅值低，噪声/不完整/冲突数据需要更强的传输校正。

Result: 在严重传感器噪声和语义冲突场景下，GMF显著优于基于置信度的基线方法，有效标记不可靠输入即使分类器被误导。

Conclusion: 基于几何的可靠性信号可作为独立判别器，打破置信度方法的循环依赖，提升多模态系统对低质量数据的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Real-world multimodal systems must be robust against low-quality data, such as sensor noise, incomplete multimodal data and conflicting inputs. However, existing trustworthy fusion methods rely on the model's own prediction confidence to judge data quality. This creates a circular dependency: when a model is confident but wrong, these methods fail to detect the error. To break this loop, we propose Geometry-based Multimodal Fusion (GMF). Instead of relying on predictions, we evaluate reliability by measuring how much transport correction the input needs in latent space. We implement Diffusion Schrödinger Bridge transport with Rectified Flow, where the squared initial velocity gives an efficient learned correction score. Valid data has low squared velocity magnitude, while noisy, incomplete data or conflicting data requires stronger transport correction. This geometry-based reliability signal acts as an independent judge, effectively flagging unreliable inputs even when the classifier is fooled. Extensive experiments demonstrate that GMF significantly improves robustness against severe sensor noise and semantic conflicts compared to confidence-based baselines.

</details>


### [90] [Beyond Additive Decompositions: Interpretability Through Separability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31200)
*Jinyang Liu,Munir Eberhardt Hiabu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出张量分离学习（TSL），一种通过贪婪分阶段与正交重构学习秩-1乘积和回归模型的方法，避免加性模型在强交互下产生信号抵消和外推问题，并提供理论保障与可视化忠实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释性方法（如GAM、SHAP、功能ANOVA）依赖加性表示，在存在强交互作用时会出现信号抵消和支撑外推问题，导致信息丢失，亟需一种既能保持可解释性又能忠实捕捉交互结构的回归模型。

Method: 提出张量分离学习（TSL），通过分阶段贪心过程结合正交重构，学习单变量逐特征函数的秩-1乘积之和作为回归模型。模型通过可分离性约束避免加性投影带来的信息损失，且模型可完全由一阶偏依赖函数重构（除常数因子外）。

Result: 建立了对具有有界混合p阶偏导数的函数的逼近率保证，并在回归基准测试中证明TSL能与黑箱模型竞争。

Conclusion: TSL通过可分离张量结构在保持可解释性的同时有效建模强交互作用，避免了加性模型的固有问题，且理论上有逼近保证，实践中达到黑箱模型性能。

Abstract: Interpretable machine learning requires models that are accurate and structurally faithful to the data. Existing explainability methods rely heavily on additive representations (e.g., Generalized Additive Models (GAMs), SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP), functional ANOVA), which can suffer from signal cancellation and off-support extrapolation in the presence of strong interactions. We propose Tensor Separation Learning (TSL), a regression model that learns a sum of rank-1 products of univariate per-feature functions via a stagewise greedy procedure with orthogonal refitting. By enforcing separability, TSL avoids the information loss inherent in additive projections caused by marginalizing higher-order interactions. The learned TSL model can be fully reconstructed from first-order partial dependence functions, up to constant factors. This stage-wise correspondence ensures that the resulting visualizations are faithful to the fitted components. We establish approximation-rate guarantees for functions with bounded mixed $p$-th order partial derivatives and demonstrate that TSL competes with black-box models on regression benchmarks.

</details>


### [91] [Fixed-Point Masked Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31215)
*Andrea Miele,Yiming Qin,Alba Carballo-Castro,Justin Deschenaux,Pascal Frossard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CoFRe框架，通过固定点求解器替代部分去噪器，结合跨步一致性损失和三态重用的热身策略，实现自适应深度和更少的参数，在OpenWebText和ImageNette上显著降低了训练成本并提升了低预算下的生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码生成模型（MGMs）虽支持并行解码且性能优异，但每一步都需要全序列双向Transformer，导致训练成本高昂且在低采样预算下质量退化。现有方法通过更好的采样器或固定深度去噪器提升效率，但每个精炼步骤仍分配固定量的去噪计算，缺乏自适应深度机制。

Method: 提出固定点掩码生成模型（FP-MGM），用共享注意力层上的固定点求解器替代部分去噪器以实现自适应深度；引入跨步一致性损失对齐相邻去噪步的隐藏表示；提出三态重用（3SR）策略，对未变化、仍掩码和新揭示的token分别处理，用前一步解热启动求解器。预训练MGMs可通过短微调转换为FP-MGM。

Result: 在OpenWebText上，参数量减少38.8%，训练时间减少11.5%，显存减少16.9%，在96次Transformer前向传播预算下生成困惑度从830.8降至101.8。在ImageNette上，训练时间减少48.6%，显存减少50.7%，所有采样预算下FID均有改善。

Conclusion: CoFRe提供了一个实用的框架，实现了更廉价的训练和更强的低预算掩码生成能力。

Abstract: Masked Generative Models (MGMs) enable parallel decoding and achieve strong performance across modalities, but require full-sequence bidirectional transformers at every step, making training costly and degrading quality under low sampling budgets. Existing work improves efficiency via better samplers or cheaper fixed-depth denoisers, but they still allocate a fixed amount of denoiser computation to each refinement step. We introduce Fixed-Point Masked Generative Models (FP-MGMs), which replace part of the denoiser with a fixed-point solver over shared attention layers to enable adaptive depth with fewer parameters. To make it more effective for masked generation, we first introduce a cross-step consistency loss, which aligns hidden representations at neighboring denoising steps and, second, three-state reuse (3SR) which warm-starts the solver using the previous solution by treating differently unchanged, still-masked, and newly revealed tokens respectively. Together, these components define our complete training-to-inference framework for fixed-point masked generation, \emph{CoFRe}. We also show that pre-trained MGMs can be converted into FP-MGMs with short fine-tuning, avoiding full retraining. Across modalities, CoFRe improves the quality and cost trade-off. On OpenWebText, CoFRe reduces parameters by 38.8\%, training time by 11.5\%, and VRAM by 16.9\%, while improving generative perplexity from 830.8 to 101.8 at a budget of $96$ transformer-block forward passes, compared to MDLM. In ImageNette, CoFRe reduces training time by 48.6\% and VRAM by 50.7\%, while improving FID in all sample budgets tested. Overall, CoFRe offers a practical framework for cheaper training and stronger low-budget masked generation.

</details>


### [92] [Multivariate Distributional Reinforcement Learning Using Sliced Divergences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31222)
*Baptiste Debes,Tinne Tuytelaars*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出切片分布强化学习（SDRL），通过将一维散度投影到多维返回分布上，解决了多变量分布强化学习中度量泛化困难、计算复杂及矩阵折扣下的收缩问题，并验证了其在多个环境中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布强化学习（DRL）在多变量场景下存在两个核心挑战：1）许多常用度量无法自然地泛化到一维以上或在泛化后失去计算可处理性；2）多变量情况下引入通用矩阵折扣，尚无收缩性结果可用。

Method: 通过投影（slicing）将可处理的一维散度提升到多变量返回分布，设计了两种变体：均匀切片（uniform slicing）在共享标量折扣下证明贝尔曼收缩，最大切片（maximum-slicing）在稠密折扣矩阵下实现收缩。支持Wasserstein、Cramér和MMD等基散度，并分析了哪些变体适用于标准单样本贝尔曼更新。

Result: 在玩具链问题、基于图像的网格世界环境以及部分Atari游戏中进行了实验评估。

Conclusion: SDRL提供了一种将一维分布散度有效推广到多变量DRL的通用框架，在保证贝尔曼收缩的同时保持了计算可处理性，且支持多种散度选择和灵活的折扣结构。

Abstract: Distributional reinforcement learning (DRL) models the full return distribution rather than expectations, but extending it to multivariate settings remains challenging. Many common metrics do not naturally generalize beyond one dimension or lose computational tractability, and the multivariate case introduces additional difficulties such as general matrix discounting, for which no contraction results are available. We introduce Sliced Distributional Reinforcement Learning (SDRL), which lifts tractable one-dimensional divergences to multivariate return distributions via projections. We prove Bellman contraction for uniform slicing under shared scalar discounting, and introduce a maximum-slicing variant with contraction under general dense discount matrices. SDRL supports a broad class of base divergences; we analyze Wasserstein, Cramér, and Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD), and characterize which SDRL variants suit the standard single-sample Bellman update used in distributional RL. We evaluate SDRL on a toy chain problem and a gridworld image-based environment as well as a subset of Atari games.

</details>


### [93] [What changes after deployment? A survey on On-device Learning in TinyML](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31226)
*Massimo Pavan,Luca Pezzarossa,Fabrizio Pittorino,Manuel Roveri,Xenofon Fafoutis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了约70篇TinyML设备端学习（ODL）研究，从分布变化视角出发，分析了不同类型分布变化对应用、硬件和解决方案的影响，并指出了方法论基准与真实部署场景之间的持续差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TinyML设备面临部署后分布变化导致静态模型失效的根本挑战，而现有文献缺乏对分布变化类型及其所需不同解决方案的系统性刻画。

Method: 以"分布变化机制"为统一原则，对约70篇设备端学习（ODL）工作进行综述性分析，考察不同分布变化类型对可寻址应用、硬件选择及解决方案结构的影响。

Result: 识别出不同类型分布变化对应用场景、硬件需求和解决方案结构的差异化影响，同时揭示了方法论基准测试与真实部署场景之间的持续差距。

Conclusion: 分布变化是TinyML设备端学习的核心挑战，不同变化类型需要针对性解决方案，当前研究存在方法论与真实部署之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Machine learning models on microcontroller-class devices (TinyML) face a fundamental challenge: post-deployment distribution change undermines static models. On-device learning (ODL) addresses this by running the learning process directly on the device. The existing literature has not characterized how distribution change occurs or how different change types require different solutions. Approximately 70 ODL works are surveyed under one principle: the distribution change regime. The survey analyzes how different types of distribution change influence the applications addressable on-device, the hardware employed, and the structure of the solutions. A persistent gap between methodological benchmarks and real-world deployment scenarios is also identified.

</details>


### [94] [EchoRL: Reinforcement Learning via Rollout Echoing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31228)
*Jinhe Bi,Aniri,Minglai Yang,Xingcheng Zhou,Wenke Huang,Sikuan Yan,Yujun Wang,Zixuan Cao,Michael Färber,Xun Xiao,Volker Tresp,Yunpu Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对RLVR训练中出现的优势退化问题（所有自生成 rollout 均验证成功导致策略梯度消失），提出EchoRL模块，通过从成功rollout中基于熵值识别EchoClip作为辅助监督信号，持续提升后训练性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR方法在训练后期，大量prompt的rollout出现优势退化——所有自生成rollout均为验证成功，导致其奖励标准差为零，进而优势为零，策略梯度消失，训练收益边际化；而这些rollout中仍包含有价值的学习信号却被忽略。

Method: 提出EchoRL轻量模块：首先分析外部专家模型生成的金色轨迹的熵模式，然后从验证成功的rollout中基于逐层熵值识别出EchoClip，将其作为辅助监督信号加入到RL目标函数中。

Result: 在10个基准、5种LLM骨干网络、4种主流RLVR后训练方法上的实验表明，EchoRL能以最小开销持续提升RLVR后训练性能。

Conclusion: EchoRL有效缓解了RLVR训练中的优势退化问题，通过重新利用被忽略的rollout信号进一步提升了模型训练效果，且通用性强、开销低。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards is an effective route for post-training to strengthen the reasoning capability of large language models. However, as training proceeds, the learning signal can collapse thus makes the training gain become marginal and ineffective. Specifically, a growing fraction of prompts' rollouts become advantage-degenerated: all the self-generated rollouts show verified-success, making the standard deviation over their rewards be zero; accordingly each rollout's advantage becomes degenerated (zero) as well. Given such rollouts' advantages, the policy-gradient for model optimization eventually vanishes, capping the training performance. We argue that some of these rollouts still contain valuable learning signals but unfortunately omitted with the existing RLVR methods. In this paper, inspired through analyzing the entropy pattern behind golden trajectories produced by external expert models, we propose EchoRL for better exploiting the advantage-degenerated rollouts to further improve the training performance. EchoRL is a lightweight module that first identifies an EchoClip from verified-success rollouts based on their step-level entropy values, and then feeds this clip back as an auxiliary supervision signal in the RL objective. Extensive experiments across 10 benchmarks, 5 LLM backbones, and 4 popular RLVR post-training methods demonstrate that EchoRL consistently improves RLVR post-training with minimal overhead.

</details>


### [95] [Bifurcated Remaining Useful Life Prediction: A Hybrid Approach for Realistic Uncertainty Characterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31241)
*Xabier Belaunzaran,Antonio Nappa,Arkaitz Artetxe,Basilio Sierra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于涡扇发动机剩余使用寿命预测的混合预后框架，通过状态感知策略区分健康与退化阶段，结合LSTM自编码器、条件威布尔生存分析和概率神经网络，生成具有物理一致性的不确定性区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RUL预测方法通常忽略发动机运行状态的分阶段特性，且难以同时量化偶然不确定性和认知不确定性，导致预测置信度不足，无法有效支持风险导向的维护决策。

Method: 采用状态感知策略将寿命分为健康期和退化期；LSTM自编码器基于正常数据训练，通过重构误差进行状态分类；健康期使用条件威布尔生存分析估计平均剩余寿命；退化期使用带蒙特卡洛丢弃的概率神经网络捕捉双重不确定性；通过校准的Sigmoid函数将自编码器输出转换为连续状态概率，动态加权集成预测。

Result: 生成的物理一致的不确定性区间在寿命末期具有高置信度预测，同时准确反映早期运行的内在方差。

Conclusion: 该框架为风险知情维护提供了稳健工具，通过动态加权集成和不确定性量化提升了RUL预测的可靠性与实用性。

Abstract: This study presents a novel hybrid prognostic framework for uncertainty-aware Remaining Useful Life (RUL) estimation in turbofan engines using the NASA C-MAPSS dataset. The framework employs a state-aware strategy that bifurcates the engines operational lifespan into "healthy" and "degraded" regimes. An LSTM-based autoencoder, trained strictly on nominal data (RUL > 150 cycles), monitors reconstruction error to act as a robust state classifier. For the healthy regime, a Conditional Weibull Survival Analysis is used for Mean Residual Life estimation. For the degraded regime, a Probabilistic Neural Network with Monte Carlo Dropout captures both aleatoric and epistemic uncertainties. Rather than using rigid binary labels, a calibrated sigmoid function converts the autoencoders output into continuous state probabilities, dynamically weighting the final ensemble prediction. The primary strength of this framework is its generation of physically consistent uncertainty bands, yielding high-confidence predictions near end-of-life while accurately reflecting the inherent variance of early operation, providing a robust tool for risk-informed maintenance.

</details>


### [96] [Spectral Reach: Understanding Neural Scaling as Progress into the Spectral Tail](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31244)
*Konstantin Nikolaou,Jonas Scheunemann,Sven Krippendorf,Samuel Tovey,Christian Holm*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"谱位置"（spectral position）作为可扩展的度量指标，用于衡量经验神经正切核（eNTK）中哪些特征值当前驱动损失下降。通过尺度实验发现：训练过程中谱位置下降，学习从主导特征模式转向谱尾；更大模型具有更深的"谱到达"（spectral reach）能力，能够利用弱谱信号；特征学习通过自适应放大梯度幅度来支撑谱到达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经缩放定律描述了模型规模、数据集大小、计算量与性能之间的幂律关系，但支撑这些定律的机制仍不清晰，主要原因在于缺乏可扩展的分析工具。

Method: 提出"谱位置"（spectral position）这一可扩展度量，用于跟踪训练过程中eNTK特征值对损失下降的贡献变化；通过缩放实验分析不同规模模型的行为差异。

Result: 训练过程中谱位置下降，学习从主导特征模式向谱尾转移；更大模型具有更大的"谱到达"能力，能利用更深的谱尾弱信号；特征学习通过自适应放大梯度幅度维持谱到达。

Conclusion: 解释了为什么更大模型能达到更低损失：它们能持续学习对较小模型不可及的弱谱信号。特征学习是谱到达的关键使能因素。这为通过架构和优化器设计进行具体干预提供了方向。

Abstract: Neural scaling laws describe predictable power-law relationships between model size, dataset size, compute, and performance. While these laws guide the development of modern foundation models, the mechanisms underpinning them remain poorly understood, in part due to the absence of scalable analysis tools. To close this gap, we introduce "spectral position": a scalable measure of which eigenvalues of the empirical neural tangent kernel (eNTK) currently drive loss reduction. Applying this measure to scaling experiments, we find that spectral position decreases throughout training: learning shifts from dominant eigenmodes into the spectral tail. Larger models reach further into the tail than smaller models, revealing a size-dependent capacity we call "spectral reach". This suggests why larger models achieve lower losses: they sustain learning on weak spectral signals inaccessible to smaller models. We further identify feature learning as a key enabler of spectral reach. It adaptively amplifies gradient magnitudes as learning advances, sustaining progress where frozen representations stall. This points to concrete interventions through architecture and optimizer design.

</details>


### [97] [Toward Identifiable Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31245)
*Walter Nelson,Theofanis Karaletsos,Francesco Locatello*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了稀疏自编码器(SAE)训练结果高度不稳定，不同运行会产生不同概念字典和稀疏编码；通过分析模型属性导致不稳定的原因，提出最小化架构和训练过程改动，得到两种可识别SAE(iSAE)变体，在降低重构误差的同时提升稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAE在解释和交互神经网络表示方面具有重要价值，但存在训练结果不稳定的问题，即不同训练运行倾向于产生不同的概念字典和稀疏编码。作者希望从理论和实践两方面解决这一稳定性问题。

Method: 1）理论上证明SAE高度不稳定；2）刻画导致现实SAE不稳定的模型属性；3）对架构和训练过程进行最小化改动，提出iSAE（可识别SAE）；4）将SAE与传统字典学习方法建立联系，证明实践中学习的字典满足近似受限等距条件(RIP)，从而保证稀疏编码近可识别性。

Result: 提出的iSAE（两个版本）相比标准TopK SAE，在重构误差更低的同时显著提升了稳定性。字典学习满足近似受限等距条件，使得稀疏编码具有近可识别性。

Conclusion: 通过理论分析和架构改进，SAE的不稳定性问题可以通过最小化改动得到有效缓解，iSAE在保持可解释性的同时实现了更稳定和更精准的表示学习。

Abstract: Recently, sparse autoencoders (SAEs) have emerged as an attractive tool for interpreting and interacting with representations in practical neural networks. While it is common empirical folklore, we also show theoretically that SAEs are highly unstable: different training runs are likely to produce different concept dictionaries and sparse codes. We characterize the model properties that hinder the stability of real-world SAEs, and address each of these problems through minimal changes to the architecture and training procedure. Together, these changes yield two versions of an \textbf{i}dentifiable SAE (iSAE), a variant of the standard TopK SAE with lower reconstruction error and improved stability. We explain this improvement theoretically by connecting SAEs with traditional dictionary learning approaches, and show that the dictionaries learned in practice satisfy an approximate restricted isometry condition, rendering the corresponding sparse codes in those models near-identifiable.

</details>


### [98] [Learning Cardiac Latent Representations in Vectorcardiogram Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31249)
*Bosong Huang,Panzhen Zhao,Zengxiang Li,Patricia Lee,Wei Jin,Alan Wee-Chung Liew,Ming Jin,Shirui Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LVCG，第一个在物理基础的向量心电图（VCG）空间中进行自监督表示学习的框架，通过学习视角不变的潜在VCG表示替代传统ECG信号空间表示，有效降低冗余、提高泛化能力，在域迁移场景下表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准十二导联ECG是同一心电活动在不同空间方向的投影，在ECG信号空间进行表示学习存在大量冗余，会导致虚假相关性和过拟合风险。

Method: 受Frank VCG模型启发，提出LVCG框架，直接在VCG空间中学习统一的潜在心电活动表示，通过自监督学习方式实现视角不变性。

Result: LVCG在多项任务上优于基于ECG空间的基线方法，尤其在域迁移场景下展现出更强的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 在物理基础的VCG空间进行表示学习能有效减少冗余、提升泛化性能，是ECG表示学习的一个有前景的新方向。

Abstract: Electrocardiography (ECG) is a cornerstone of cardiac assessment, making the learning of informative ECG representations fundamental to tasks ranging from disease diagnosis to clinical report generation. However, existing methods operate almost exclusively in the observable ECG signal space. In practice, the standard twelve-lead ECG represents multiple projections of the same underlying cardiac electrical activity from different spatial orientations. Therefore, representation learning in the ECG space inevitably introduces substantial redundancy, which may lead to spurious correlations and increased risk of overfitting. To address this and motivated by the Frank vectorcardiogram (VCG) model, we propose learning a unified latent representation of cardiac electrical activity directly in the VCG space. We introduce LVCG, the first general self-supervised representation learning framework designed to operate in this physically grounded latent space. By learning view-invariant latent VCG representations rather than lead-specific artifacts, VCG minimizes redundancy and improves generalization. LVCG generally outperforms ECG-space baselines across tasks, demonstrating enhanced robustness and generalization, especially in domain shift settings.

</details>


### [99] [Fraud Type Decomposition and the Observation-Mechanism Taxonomy:Class-Specific Detection Limits in Payment Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31257)
*Gaurav Dhama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明支付网络欺诈检测中，将欺诈视为同质二元变量在结构上是错误的，并提出将欺诈分为五类，每类具有不同的审查和标注流程。通过分类估计并聚合，效率优于统一估计，效率差距由异质观测率导致的Jensen惩罚刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有欺诈检测方法将欺诈视为同质二元变量，忽略了通过异质且不完美的观测过程生成的标签结构，导致可证明的低效。

Method: 提出观测机制分类法，将欺诈分为五类，每类定义不同的审查和标注流程；证明分类估计后聚合严格优于统一估计；推导每类检测的理论约束边界。

Result: 分类估计的聚合效率严格优于统一估计，效率差距由异质观测率导致的Jensen惩罚表征；识别出每类欺诈的检测极限包括内生标签污染、结构不可观测性和特征非信息性。

Conclusion: 欺诈检测本质上是多个不同的估计问题集合，每个问题由其自身的观测结构和检测极限所支配。

Abstract: Fraud detection in payment networks relies on labels generated through heterogeneous and imperfect observation processes, yet existing approaches treat fraud as a homogeneous binary variable. We show that this assumption is structurally incorrect and leads to provable inefficiency. We introduce an observation-mechanism taxonomy that partitions fraud into five classes, each defined by a distinct censorship and labeling pipeline. We prove that estimating fraud rates separately by class and aggregating strictly dominates pooled estimation, with the efficiency gap characterized as a Jensen penalty arising from heterogeneous observation rates. For each class, we derive the binding theoretical constraint on detection, including endogenous label corruption, structural non-observability, and feature non-informativeness. These results establish that fraud detection is fundamentally a collection of distinct estimation problems, each governed by its own observation structure and detection limit.

</details>


### [100] [Lightweight CNN-Based Anomaly Detection for High Voltage Converter Modulators in the Spallation Neutron Source](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31259)
*Alberto D. Cencillo,Leonardo Concepción,Julián Luengo,Isaac Triguero*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对大型加速器设施中高压脉冲变换器非计划停机问题，研究了深度学习中时序滤波与跨通道混合的操作顺序这一归纳偏置对故障检测性能的影响。在SNS的HVCM数据集上，最佳变体实现了AUC-PR 0.816和AUC-ROC 0.934，超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高压脉冲变换器非计划停机是大型加速器设施停机的主要原因，现有深度学习方法在多通道信号处理中从第一层就纠缠时序与跨通道操作，缺乏显式建模通道独立性和结构化跨通道交互的机制。

Method: 通过改变时序滤波与跨通道混合两种操作的应用顺序，并引入每脉冲自适应通道重加权机制，探索架构归纳偏置对故障检测性能的影响。

Result: 在SNS四个子系统（RFQ、DTL、CCL、SCL）上，最佳变体达到pooled AUC-PR 0.816和AUC-ROC 0.934，在大部分子系统和五个故障族上超越现有最优方法。

Conclusion: 时序与跨通道操作顺序这一架构选择对故障检测至关重要；消融实验识别出三个主导输入通道，并将各故障族的性能差异归因于故障前兆表现为单通道幅度偏移还是需要联合通道表征才能显现的细微模式。

Abstract: Unscheduled trips of high-power pulsed converters are a leading source of downtime at large accelerator facilities. At the Spallation Neutron Source (SNS), the High Voltage Converter Modulators (HVCMs) are consistently the second-largest contributor to lost beam time. Each HVCM pulse is recorded across sensor channels spanning currents, voltages, and magnetic fluxes, whose mutual interactions encode the operating state of the system. Fault precursors do not manifest uniformly across these channels: depending on fault type, they may alter the temporal structure of individual signals, change the statistical dependencies among channels, or both. Existing deep-learning approaches typically process multi-channel signals with standard convolutional pipelines that entangle temporal and cross-channel operations from the first layer, giving the model no explicit mechanism to represent channel independence or structured inter-channel interaction. We hypothesise that architectural inductive bias, specifically the ordering of temporal filtering and cross-channel mixing, plays a central role in detection performance on this class of data. To test this, we vary the order in which these two operations are applied, and examine whether per-pulse adaptive channel reweighting further improves sensitivity. Evaluated on the public HVCM dataset across all four SNS subsystems (RFQ, DTL, CCL, SCL), our best variant achieves a pooled AUC-PR of 0.816 and AUC-ROC of 0.934, outperforming the state of the art on most subsystems and five of the six fault families. Ablations identify three dominant input channels and link per-fault-family performance to whether precursors manifest as amplitude shifts in individual channels or as subtler patterns requiring joint channel representations to surface.

</details>


### [101] [Algorithmic Recourse of In-Context Learning for Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31272)
*Wenshuo Dong,Jiaming Zhang,Shaopneg Fu,Hongbin Lin,Di Wang,Lijie Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次针对上下文学习（ICL）下的表格数据提出算法追索（algorithmic recourse）研究，从理论上证明了追索的有界性和收敛性，并提出了高效的自适应子空间追索框架（ASR-ICL），实验表明该方法能以更少查询达到与现有方法相当的追索质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高风险决策场景（如信贷审批）中，被影响的个体需要可解释的追索手段来改变模型决策。尽管大语言模型通过上下文学习实现了表格数据预测，但算法追索在ICL场景下尚未被探索。

Method: 理论分析证明ICL下追索的有界性和随上下文大小增加向经典解收敛的行为；实践上提出ASR-ICL框架，一种零阶追索方法，通过自适应子空间搜索高效生成可操作且稀疏的追索方案，并扩展到多类任务。

Result: 在多个真实数据集和模型上的实验表明，ASR-ICL能以更少的查询次数达到与现有方法相当的追索质量，并实证验证了理论预测的收敛行为。

Conclusion: ASR-ICL为ICL下的表格数据提供了高效、可操作的算法追索方案，理论与实验共同验证了其有效性，为高风险决策场景下的LLM可解释性提供了新途径。

Abstract: As predictive models are increasingly deployed in high-stakes settings such as credit approval, there is a growing need for post-hoc methods that provide recourse to affected individuals. Many such models operate on tabular data, where features correspond to real-world attributes. Recently, in-context learning (ICL) has enabled large language models to perform tabular prediction by conditioning on labeled examples at inference time, without explicit training. However, algorithmic recourse for tabular decision-making under ICL remains largely unexplored. In this work, we present the first study of algorithmic recourse for tabular data under ICL. We carry out a theoretical analysis, showing that recourse remains well-defined and bounded, and we characterize how recourse converges toward classical solutions as the context size increases. In practice, we propose a novel zeroth-order recourse framework, Adaptive Subspace Recourse for In-Context Learning (ASR-ICL), that efficiently generates actionable and sparse recourse for black-box ICL models. The proposed framework naturally extends to multi-class tabular tasks. Experiments across multiple real-world datasets and models demonstrate that ASR-ICL achieves recourse quality comparable to existing methods with fewer queries and empirically confirm the predicted convergence behavior, supporting our theoretical analysis.

</details>


### [102] [Survival Reinforcement Learning: Toward Scalable Self-Supervised RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31273)
*Franki Nguimatsia-Tiofack,Fabian Schramm,Théotime Le Hellard,Justin Carpentier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出生存强化学习（SRL），一种基于在线分类的替代方法，通过最大化智能体在目标处的停留时间，解决了对比强化学习（CRL）在长时序目标条件规划中面临的均匀性-容忍性困境，在机器人操控任务上达到SOTA水平，并在长时序 locomotion 任务上实现2-8倍性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督对比强化学习（CRL）虽然展现了深度扩展能力（成功使用超过64层网络），但在长时序目标条件规划中存在对比损失固有的均匀性-容忍性困境（uniformity-tolerance dilemma），限制了其性能。

Method: 提出生存强化学习（Survival Reinforcement Learning, SRL），这是一种基于在线分类的替代方法，通过最大化智能体在目标状态处的停留时间（dwell time）来扩展生存价值学习框架。SRL绕过了CRL的结构性约束，同时缓解了生存框架中固有的"bang-bang"控制问题（即产生剧烈切换的不连续控制信号）。

Result: 在多种机器人基准任务上，SRL在操控任务中达到了与SOTA CRL匹配的性能，在稳定的长时序 locomotion 任务上实现了2倍到8倍的性能提升。

Conclusion: 结果强有力地证明，基于分类的方法可能成为扩展强化学习的关键基础原语（primitive）。

Abstract: While self-supervised Contrastive Reinforcement Learning (CRL) has shown remarkable depth-scaling capabilities, successfully using networks over 64 layers, scaled CRL still struggles with long-horizon goal-conditioned planning due to the uniformity-tolerance dilemma inherent in contrastive losses. We introduce Survival Reinforcement Learning (SRL), an online classification-based alternative that extends the survival value learning framework by maximizing the agent's dwell time at target goals. SRL bypasses the structural constraints of CRL and mitigates the "bang-bang" control solutions inherent to survival frameworks, which often induce undesirable behavior in complex dynamical systems. Evaluated across diverse robotic benchmarks, scaled SRL matches state-of-the-art CRL on manipulation tasks and outperforms it by 2x to 8x on stable, long-horizon locomotion tasks. Our results provide strong additional evidence that classification-based methods may serve as a key primitive in the broader effort to scale reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [103] [Learning Parametric Nitrogen Fertilizer Response Curves Using Neuro Symbolic Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31276)
*Giorgio Morales,John Sheppard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种神经符号回归方法，无需预设函数形式即可从数据中学习氮响应曲线，在合成数据和真实冬小麦数据上均优于传统模型，能够发现特定田块管理分区的参数化函数关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精准农业中准确建模作物对氮肥的响应至关重要，但现有方法要么依赖预设参数形式，要么使用不透明的机器学习模型，难以解释或发现数据中的场地特异性功能关系。

Method: 采用基于Transformer的多集合符号骨架预测策略，通过构建多样化输入子集并强制一致性约束来发现共享的函数骨架，再利用遗传算法拟合观测数据。

Result: 在合成一维问题上，该方法即使在数据稀缺场景下也能恢复正确表达式；在真实冬小麦数据上，发现的表达式不仅拟合误差低于二次平台和指数函数等传统模型，还能捕捉不同空间区域的多样化功能行为。

Conclusion: 神经符号回归方法能够发现场地特异性的农艺关系，为精准农业中的知情决策提供支持，展示了其在发现可解释、参数化作物响应函数方面的潜力。

Abstract: Accurately modeling crop response to Nitrogen (N) fertilization is a fundamental challenge in precision agriculture, as it impacts both economic returns and environmental sustainability. Existing approaches either rely on predefined parametric forms or opaque machine learning models, limiting their ability to interpret or discover site-specific functional relationships from data. In this work, we propose a neuro symbolic regression (SR) approach to learn parametric N-response curves without assuming a predefined functional form. Our approach integrates a transformer-based Multi-Set Symbolic Skeleton Prediction strategy, enabling the discovery of shared functional structures across multiple subdomains or management zones (MZs). By constructing diverse input subsets and enforcing consistency across them, the method recovers robust symbolic skeletons that are subsequently fitted to observed data using a genetic algorithm. This framework was first evaluated on synthetic one-dimensional problems to assess its robustness under varying levels of epistemic uncertainty. The results demonstrate the ability of the proposed SR approach to recover correct expressions even in data-scarce regimes. In this work, we present the results of applying our method to real-world winter wheat data, learning distinct parametric N-response curves for different MZs within a field. The results show that the discovered expressions not only achieve lower fitting errors than traditional models such as quadratic-plateau and exponential functions, but also capture diverse functional behaviors across spatial regions. This demonstrates the potential that neuro SR has to enable the discovery of site-specific agronomic relationships and support informed decision-making in precision agriculture.

</details>


### [104] [Interpretability Without Tradeoffs: Disentangling Polysemanticity At Equal Predictive Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31304)
*Doğukan Bağcı,Bernt Schiele,Simone Schaub-Meyer,Jonas Fischer,Robin Hesse*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ELUDe是一种无损、无监督的解缠方法，通过重新组织层间信息流，将DNN的潜在表示分解为可解释的单义子单元，在保证模型输出完全不变的同时提升可解释性，无需重新训练或标签，适用于多种视觉模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DNN的单个神经元常编码多个不相关概念，导致模型决策过程难以理解。现有方法（如稀疏自编码器）虽能分离混合信号，但会改变模型结构进而损害下游性能。作者希望在不牺牲性能的前提下提升可解释性。

Method: ELUDe通过重新组织层间信息流，将潜在表示分解为清晰、可检查的子单元，这些子单元行为类似可解释的单义特征。该方法保证模型输出完全不变（功能等价），无需显式训练、无需标签，可直接应用于预训练模型。

Result: 在DINOv2和有监督ViT-B/16等多个视觉模型上，ELUDe提升了可解释性，下游准确率保持不变，运行高效，并支持模型表示操控等实用功能。

Conclusion: ELUDe提供了一种几乎无权衡的可解释性方案：在不损失性能的前提下，实现更清晰、可扩展且可操作的模型洞察。

Abstract: Deep neural networks (DNNs) are widely used, but interpreting what they actually learn remains difficult. A major obstacle is that individual neurons often encode multiple unrelated concepts, obscuring the decision process of the network. While prior work, such as sparse autoencoders, can separate these mixed signals into more meaningful, "monosemantic" features, this typically requires altering the model in ways that can degrade downstream performance. To overcome this, we introduce ELUDe (explicit, lossless, unsupervised disentanglement), a method for improving the interpretability of DNNs while preserving their functional equivalence. ELUDe breaks latent representations into clear, inspectable sub-units that behave like interpretable features, while guaranteeing that the model's outputs remain exactly the same. It requires no explicit training, no labels, and can be applied to pretrained models. ELUDe works by reorganizing how information flows between layers, re-routing concept-specific contributions while preserving the original computation by construction. Across several vision models, including DINOv2 and supervised ViT-B/16, ELUDe improves interpretability, keeps downstream accuracy unchanged, runs efficiently, and supports practical uses such as steering model representations. In short, ELUDe offers interpretability (almost) without a tradeoff: clearer, scalable, and actionable model insights with no loss in performance.

</details>


### [105] [Non-Asymptotic Convergence of Stochastic Iterative Algorithms: A Lyapunov Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31309)
*Zaiwei Chen,Siva Theja Maguluri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了基于李雅普诺夫方法对随机迭代算法（随机逼近算法）进行有限时间分析的技术，重点介绍了广义Moreau包络作为通用李雅普诺夫函数，并将其应用于随机梯度下降、线性随机逼近和强化学习算法（如Q学习和时序差分学习），同时讨论了马尔可夫噪声、半范数压缩算子、耗散算子及高概率界等扩展问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为随机逼近算法的有限时间分析提供一个统一、自洽的理论框架，特别是将其应用于强化学习领域，解决现有分析方法在不同场景下缺乏统一性的问题。

Method: 利用广义Moreau包络构造通用李雅普诺夫函数，对压缩算子（相对于某种范数）在独立同分布噪声下进行分析，并推广到马尔可夫噪声、半范数压缩算子、耗散算子等复杂场景，同时推导均方收敛保证和高概率界。

Result: 展示了该框架能够统一地给出随机梯度下降、线性随机逼近、Q学习和时序差分学习等算法的均方收敛保证，并扩展到更一般的算子和噪声类型。

Conclusion: 提供了一个系统性的有限时间分析路线图，揭示了李雅普诺夫方法在随机逼近算法中的普适性，并指出了若干开放问题。

Abstract: We survey Lyapunov-based techniques for the finite-time analysis of stochastic iterative algorithms, also known as stochastic approximation (SA) algorithms, for solving fixed-point equations $\bar{F}(x)=x$, where the operator $\bar{F}(\cdot)$ can only be accessed through a noisy oracle. We first focus on the standard setting in which $\bar{F}(\cdot)$ is contractive with respect to some norm and the noise is i.i.d., and explain how generalized Moreau envelopes serve as universal Lyapunov functions, regardless of the underlying norm. We then show how this framework yields mean-square convergence guarantees and applies to stochastic gradient descent, linear SA, and value-based reinforcement learning algorithms such as Q-learning and temporal-difference learning. Finally, we discuss extensions to Markovian noise, seminorm-contractive operators, dissipative operators, and high-probability bounds, and conclude with open problems. The goal is to present a unified and self-contained roadmap for the finite-time analysis of SA and its applications, especially in reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [106] [Graph Neural Networks Are Not Continuous Across Graph Resolutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31315)
*Christian Koke,Yuesong Shen,Abhishek Saroha,Marvin Eisenberger,Bastian Rieck,Michael Bronstein,Daniel Cremers*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现图神经网络（GNNs）并非在所有自然图收敛模式下都是连续的，这导致相似图（尤其是不同分辨率下的同一物体）会产生截然不同的潜在表示。作者追溯该问题至信息传播方案的结构性障碍，并提出一种原则性修改，使模型具备跨尺度的连续性，从而实现不同分辨率的稳定整合与可靠泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战社区中关于GNNs连续性的传统认知，揭示GNNs在跨尺度图表示中的不连续性缺陷及其对模型泛化能力的限制。

Method: 首先理论分析标准GNN信息传播方案导致不连续性的结构性障碍，然后提出一种原则性的架构修改，使GNN具备跨尺度连续性。

Result: 所提修改使模型能够在不同分辨率下生成一致的潜在表示，实现了跨尺度的可靠泛化，并通过大量数值实验验证了理论发现。

Conclusion: 标准GNN存在跨尺度不连续的根本性问题，但通过原则性架构修改可以解决，从而提升模型在层级或多尺度图数据上的表现。

Abstract: We show that contrary to conventional wisdom in the community, graph neural networks (GNNs) are not continuous with respect to all natural modes of graph convergence. As a result, GNNs may generate substantially different latent representations for graphs that are very similar. In particular they assign vastly different latent embeddings to graphs that represent the same underlying object at different resolution scales. We trace this failure of continuity back to a structural obstruction arising from commonly used information-propagation schemes. Building on this insight we then derive a principled modification to standard GNN architectures which equips models with continuity across scales. The proposed modification enables consistent integration of distinct resolutions and reliable generalization between them. We systematically validate our theoretical findings in a wide range of numerical experiments.

</details>


### [107] [Forgetting Has Neighbors: Localized Collateral Forgetting in Machine Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31317)
*Polina Dolgova,Sebastian U. Stich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发现机器遗忘方法在逐样本预测上与重训练模型存在高度非均匀差异，提出“局部连带遗忘”现象并分析其机制，进而提出局部教师蒸馏策略来缓解该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准评估使用聚合指标（如准确率、遗忘分数）可能掩盖局部失败模式，作者希望揭示并理解在逐样本层面遗忘模型与重训练模型的不一致性及其空间非均匀性。

Method: 1) 在逐样本层面比较遗忘模型与重训练模型的预测差异，分析差异随几何邻近性的变化；2) 识别机制：替代目标与局部预测结构不一致，通过共享表征传播到邻近样本；3) 提出局部教师蒸馏，用仅在保留集中遗忘集邻域训练的教师产生软标签替代随机目标。

Result: 梯度上升和随机标签方法（有/无保留集微调）的逐样本差异随与遗忘集的几何邻近性增大；局部教师蒸馏在CIFAR-100部分类别删除任务上显著缩小了与重训练模型的差距，尤其在遗忘集附近，同时保持竞争性的聚合指标。

Conclusion: 局部连带遗忘是机器遗忘中存在的系统性现象，其根源在于替代目标与局部预测结构不匹配；局部教师蒸馏是一种有效且简单的缓解策略。

Abstract: Machine unlearning aims to remove the influence of selected training examples without full retraining. Standard evaluations often summarize unlearning quality with aggregate metrics, such as accuracy- and forgetting-based scores, which can hide localized failures. We study this failure mode at the example level by comparing the predictions of an unlearned model to those of the model retrained after deletion. We show that this pointwise discrepancy can be highly non-uniform: for gradient-ascent and random-labeling methods, with and without retain-set fine-tuning, it grows with geometric proximity to the forget set. We call this phenomenon localized collateral forgetting. Our analysis identifies a mechanism behind the effect: surrogate targets used during unlearning can be inconsistent with the local prediction structure induced by retraining, and this inconsistency propagates through shared representations to nearby examples. Motivated by this mechanism, we propose Local Teacher Distillation, a simple mitigation strategy that replaces random targets with soft labels from a small teacher trained only on retained neighbors of the forget set. On CIFAR-100 partial-class deletion, this local teacher brings the unlearned model substantially closer to retraining, especially near the forget set, while maintaining competitive aggregate unlearning metrics.

</details>


### [108] [Generalized Intention Modeling in Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31318)
*Mateusz Odrowaz-Sypniewski,Jasmine Bayrooti,Ajay Shankar,Amanda Prorok*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种任务自适应的对手建模框架，通过学习多种意图表示的绩效驱动混合，并引入一种与自我智能体未来回报互信息最大化的新意图表示，在多种任务中匹配或超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对手建模方法使用先验选择的片段信息（如对手下一动作或未来环境状态）编码意图，假设这些信息普遍代表意图，但实验表明意图具有任务和环境依赖性，需要更灵活的方法。

Method: 提出任务自适应对手建模框架：1）学习绩效驱动的多种意图表示的混合；2）引入新意图表示，最大化与自我智能体未来回报的互信息，捕获与性能最直接相关的对手信息。

Result: 在多样化任务中一致匹配或超越最先进基线方法的表现，并揭示了不同对手建模策略成功或失败的时机与原因。

Conclusion: 任务自适应的意图混合表示比单一先验意图表示更有效，且最大化与未来回报互信息的表示方式能更好地捕捉与性能相关的对手信息。

Abstract: Modeling an opponent's intent is critical for effective decision-making in non-cooperative, competitive, and general-sum multi-agent reinforcement learning. Existing opponent modeling methods encode intent using an embedding derived from episode information chosen a priori, such as the opponent's next action or a future environment state, and use this to guide the ego-agent's behavior. These approaches assume that the chosen information is universally representative of intent; however, we show empirically that this is not the case as intentions are often task- and environment-dependent. To address this, we introduce a task-adaptive opponent modeling framework that learns a performance-driven mixture of multiple intent representations. We further introduce a new intention representation that maximizes mutual information with the ego-agent's future returns, thereby capturing opponent information that is most directly relevant to performance. Our approach consistently matches or exceeds the performance of state-of-the-art baselines across diverse tasks and yields insights into when and why different opponent modeling strategies succeed.

</details>


### [109] [dashi: A Python library for Dataset Shift Characterization to Support Trustworthy AI Development and Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31360)
*David Fernández-Narro,Pablo Ferri,Ángel Sánchez-García,Juan M. García-Gómez,Carlos Sáez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个名为 dashi 的开源 Python 库，用于探索、量化和表征数据集漂移（dataset shifts），包括无监督方法（基于信息几何和非参数统计流形）和有监督方法（量化模型性能退化），并在三个健康 AI 案例研究中验证了其实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据集漂移（包括时间漂移和多源漂移）会严重降低模型性能并影响数据质量，尤其在健康 AI 领域可能危及患者安全。尽管协变量漂移、先验漂移和概念漂移的理论基础已很成熟，但缺乏易于使用且全面的软件工具来进行相关分析。

Method: dashi 提供双轨方法：1）无监督方法，利用信息几何和非参数统计流形进行数据变异性表征（如信息几何时间图、全局概率偏差和源概率异常度等多源变异性指标）；2）有监督方法，量化和表征模型性能退化。两种方法均支持用户定义的时间批次和领域/源批次。

Result: 在三个健康 AI 案例研究（妊娠期糖尿病、COVID-19 和紧急医疗调度）中展示了 dashi 的实用性，通过交互式视觉分析和变异性指标，支持 AI 生命周期各阶段的可信度评估。

Conclusion: dashi 通过数据一致性和 AI 性能评估，支持构建稳健、安全的机器学习管道，增强 AI 生命周期各阶段的信任度。

Abstract: The Artificial Intelligence (AI) life cycle requires a thorough understanding of the underlying data dynamics for robust, safe and cost-effective AI development and use. Dataset shifts are defined as changes between train and test data distributions. Whether occurring over time (temporal) or across different sites (multi-source), they can severely degrade model performance and compromise data quality. This is particularly important in health AI, where the safety and fundamental rights of patients can be severely affected by uncontrolled shifts both at training and operational stages. While the theoretical foundations of covariate, prior, and concept shifts are well established, there is a lack of accessible and comprehensive software tools to perform their analysis. We introduce dashi, an open-source Python library designed for the exploration, quantification, and characterization of dataset shifts. dashi provides a dual approach: an unsupervised approach that leverages information geometry and non-parametric statistical manifolds to data variability characterization and analysis (e.g., Information Geometric Temporal plots and Multi-Source Variability metrics like Global Probabilistic Deviation and Source Probabilistic Outlyingness), and a supervised approach that quantifies and characterizes model performance degradation. Both unsupervised and supervised approaches work across user-defined temporal and domain/source batches. We demonstrate the utility of dashi on three simulated and real-world health AI case studies on gestational diabetes mellitus, COVID-19 and emergency medical dispatch. By providing interactive visual analytics and variability metrics, dashi supports trustworthiness of AI life cycle stages enabling robust and safe machine learning pipelines through the assessment of data coherence and AI performance.

</details>


### [110] [Trading Complexity for Expressivity Through Structured Generalized Linear Token Mixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31367)
*Erwan Fagnou,Paul Caillon,Blaise Delattre,Alexandre Allauzen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一框架来分离token混合层中直接影响和循环传播两个关键特性，设计新的循环模式以在运行时间和表达能力之间进行原则性权衡，并在合成任务和语言建模上进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有token混合层（如注意力和状态空间模型）在解码速度、内存需求和缓存大小之间存在必要的权衡，需要一个统一框架来理解并探索新的权衡方案。

Method: 提出了一个统一框架，分离了生成步骤中输入的直接影响（direct influence）和通过过去输出的循环信息传播（recurrent propagation），允许每个状态依赖多个过去状态而非仅前一个，设计新的循环模式以实现期望的复杂度。

Result: 在合成任务和语言建模上进行了实证验证，新的循环模式在运行时间和表达能力之间实现了原则性权衡。

Conclusion: 提供了一个跨模型家族的理解和设计高效且富有表现力的token混合器的统一工具包。

Abstract: Token mixing layers play a key role in how language models can learn and generate long-range dependencies. Their efficiency relies on the necessary trade-off between decoding speed and the memory requirements, along with the cache size. Considering causal generation, this paper explores new trade-offs thanks to a unified framework which separates two crucial features: (i) the direct influence of inputs on outputs in one generation step; (ii) the recurrent propagation of information through past outputs. This framework encompasses major architectures such as attention and state-space models, but also generalizes the recurrence equations by allowing each state to depend on multiple past states rather than only the immediate predecessor.
