By introducing structure, we design new recurrence patterns that provably achieve the desired complexity, while providing theoretical insights on their expressivity -- trading runtime for expressivity in a principled way. Empirical validation is performed on synthetic tasks, along with language modeling. Together, these results provide a unified toolkit for the understanding and design of efficient and expressive token mixers across model families.

</details>


### [111] [Softsign: Smooth Sign in Your Optimizer For Better Parameter Heterogeneity Handling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31371)
*Dmitrii Feoktistov,Timofey Belinsky,Andrey Veprikov,Amir Zainullin,Aleksandr Beznosikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SoftSignum和SoftMuon优化器，通过温度控制的软符号变换替代硬符号映射，结合自适应分位数温度调度和强凸正则化的几何松弛框架，在非凸随机优化中证明收敛性，实验表明在多种深度学习任务（包括大语言模型预训练）中优于传统符号优化器和AdamW。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于符号和LMO启发的优化器在深度学习中性能优秀且内存占用低，但其固定幅度更新机制与梯度幅度解耦，未能考虑参数异质性，导致终端收敛时振荡而非收敛。

Method: 1) 提出SoftSignum，用温度控制的soft-sign映射替代硬符号函数，实现从符号更新到SGD-like幅度敏感更新的参数级平滑过渡；2) 设计自适应分位数温度调度策略；3) 将相同原理扩展到矩阵值优化器得到SoftMuon；4) 基于强凸正则化器和Fenchel共轭建立广义几何松弛框架，证明随机非凸环境下的收敛性。

Result: 在多种深度学习任务（包括大语言模型预训练）上，SoftSignum和SoftMuon相比基于硬符号的对应优化器及标准AdamW均有持续改进。

Conclusion: 软符号优化器通过平滑过渡机制有效解决了符号优化器终端收敛振荡的问题，且具有理论收敛保证和实证有效性。

Abstract: Sign-based and LMO-inspired optimizers have recently attracted substantial attention in deep learning due to their strong performance and low memory footprint. However, their fixed-magnitude updates can hurt terminal convergence: they decouple update mechanisms from gradient magnitudes and fail to account for parameter heterogeneity, often leading to oscillation rather than convergence. We propose SoftSignum, a smooth relaxation of sign-based optimization that replaces the hard sign map with a temperature-controlled soft-sign transformation, enabling a parameter-wise transition from sign-like updates to magnitude-sensitive SGD-like steps. We complement it with an adaptive quantile-based temperature schedule and extend the same principle to matrix-valued optimizers, obtaining SoftMuon. We also develop a generalized geometry-relaxation framework based on strongly convex regularizers and Fenchel conjugates, proving convergence in stochastic non-convex setting. Experiments on diverse deep learning tasks, including LLM pretraining, show that SoftSignum and SoftMuon consistently improve over their hard sign-based counterparts and standard AdamW.

</details>


### [112] [Scaling Higher-Order Graph Learning with Maximal Clique Complexes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31373)
*Antoine Vialle,Aref Einizade,Fragkiskos D. Malliaros,Jhony H. Giraldo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对图神经网络（GNN）仅限于建模成对交互的局限性，提出基于胞腔复形（cell complexes）的高阶模型，通过引入简化与分解的胞腔Weisfeiler-Lehman测试（sCWL和fCWL）以及最大团复形（maximal clique complex），在保持表达力的同时提升计算效率；进一步提出线性时间复杂度的CliqueWalk随机游走算法，避免显式团枚举，最终构建了一个可扩展的拓扑学习框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图神经网络（GNN）受限于只能建模成对交互关系，而基于胞腔复形的高阶模型虽然表达力更强，但可扩展性差（计算复杂度高），亟需一种既能保持高阶表达力又具备良好可扩展性的方法。

Method: 1) 提出简化胞腔Weisfeiler-Lehman测试（sCWL）和分解胞腔Weisfeiler-Lehman测试（fCWL），在保留CWL测试表达力的同时提升计算效率；2) 引入最大团复形（maximal clique complex），在降低时间和内存复杂度的同时保持强经验性能；3) 提出CliqueWalk算法，一种有偏随机游走方法，用于采样最大团，其复杂度与图大小呈线性关系。

Result: sCWL和fCWL保留了CWL测试的表达力，但计算效率显著提升；基于最大团复形的CWNs在降低时间和内存复杂度的同时，仍保持强大的经验性能；CliqueWalk实现了线性时间复杂度的最大团采样。

Conclusion: 本文构建了一个可扩展的拓扑学习框架，能够高效地进行高阶图表示学习，在保持强表达力的同时解决了高阶模型的可扩展性瓶颈。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) are limited to modeling pairwise interactions, while higher-order models based on cell complexes achieve greater expressivity but often suffer from poor scalability. We introduce simplified and factored cellular Weisfeiler Leman tests (sCWL and fCWL), which preserve the expressivity of the CWL test while improving computational efficiency. We further introduce the maximal clique complex, enabling scalable CWNs with reduced time and memory complexity while retaining strong empirical performance. To avoid explicit clique enumeration, we propose CliqueWalk, a biased random walk that samples maximal cliques and scales linearly with graph size. These contributions yield a scalable topological learning framework for higher-order graph representation.

</details>


### [113] [Constrained Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning with Max-Min Criterion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31388)
*Giseung Park,Hyunyoung Nam,Woohyeon Byeon,Amir Leshem,Youngchul Sung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种融合最大最小准则与显式约束满足的多目标强化学习框架，通过理论分析、收敛性证明以及在建筑热控制、多目标运动控制和温室气体排放感知交通管理三个领域的实验，验证了该方法在平衡公平性与约束满足方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最大最小MORL方法在促进公平性方面有效，但缺乏对约束条件的支持，限制了其应用范围。

Method: 提出一个将最大最小准则与显式约束满足相结合的多目标强化学习框架，并建立了理论基础，在表格环境下进行了收敛性分析。

Result: 在三个实际领域（建筑热控制、多目标运动控制、温室气体排放感知交通管理）中，该方法有效平衡了公平性与约束满足。

Conclusion: 该框架扩展了最大最小MORL的适用性，使其能够同时处理公平性目标和显式约束，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning (MORL) extends standard RL by optimizing policies with respect to multiple, often conflicting, objectives. While max-min MORL has emerged as an effective approach for promoting fairness, its applicability remains limited, particularly when constraints must be incorporated. In this paper, we propose a MORL framework that integrates the max-min criterion with explicit constraint satisfaction. We establish a theoretical foundation for the proposed framework and validate the resulting algorithm through convergence analysis and experiments in tabular settings. We further demonstrate the practical relevance of our approach in simulated building thermal control, multi-objective locomotion control, and greenhouse-gas-emission-aware traffic management. Across these domains, our method effectively balances fairness and constraint satisfaction in multi-objective decision-making.

</details>


### [114] [DG-CoLearn: An Efficient Collaborative Learning Framework for Dynamic Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31427)
*Ashley Hoi-Ting Au,Zikun Zhang,Ligang He,Qiang Ni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DG-CoLearn是一种基于增量快照处理的客户端无关协作动态图学习框架，通过服务器中介的嵌入交换机制实现跨客户端消息传递，在不暴露原始图结构的前提下，实现了33.8倍训练加速、27.4倍通信开销降低，并在节点分类和链接预测任务上分别提升了13.36%的F1和8.27%的MAP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态图学习方法因重复全快照重训练导致计算开销巨大，且不适合数据分区的协作场景；跨分区边不可避免，但直接共享图结构违反隐私约束。

Method: 提出DG-CoLearn框架，采用增量图快照处理，仅关注时间更新影响的图区域，并通过时序建模保留历史信息；引入服务器中介的嵌入交换机制，在不暴露原始跨客户端结构信息的情况下实现准确的多跳消息传递。

Result: 训练时间加速最高33.8倍，通信开销降低最高27.4倍；节点分类F1提升13.36%，链接预测MAP提升8.27%。

Conclusion: DG-CoLearn在协作动态图学习中有效桥接了效率、可扩展性和客户端间结构化隐私保护。

Abstract: Dynamic graph learning (DGL) is essential for modelling evolving graph data, but existing methods suffer from significant computational overhead due to repeated full-snapshot retraining and are not well-suited for collaborative settings with partitioned data. In realistic graph systems, cross-partition edges are unavoidable, but direct sharing of graph structure between clients may violate privacy constraints. We propose DG-CoLearn, a client-oblivious collaborative dynamic graph learning framework built on incremental graph snapshot processing, which focuses computation on graph regions affected by temporal updates while preserving historical information through temporal modelling. This incremental design is consistently applied across the entire graph processing pipeline, including a server-mediated embedding exchange mechanism to enable accurate multi-hop message passing without exposing raw cross-client structural information. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DG-CoLearn achieves up to 33.8$\times$ speedup in training time and 27.4$\times$ reduction in communication overhead, while consistently improving predictive performance on both node classification (up to 13.36% F1 improvement) and link prediction (up to 8.27% MAP improvement) tasks. These results highlight the effectiveness of DG-CoLearn in bridging efficiency, scalability, and client-to-client structural privacy in collaborative dynamic graph learning.

</details>


### [115] [DRIFT: Decoupled Rollouts and Importance-Weighted Fine-Tuning for Efficient Multi-Turn Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31455)
*Jian Mu,Tianyi Lin,Chengwei Qin,Zhongxiang Dai,Yao Shu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DRIFT将KL正则化RL目标重写为重要性加权监督学习，通过从固定参考策略采样轨迹并计算回报权重，实现与在线RL相当的多轮交互性能，同时保持SFT的训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮交互场景中，在线RL虽能有效处理动态性但成本过高（需每轮生成完整修正轨迹），离线SFT虽高效但存在分布偏移和行为崩溃问题，亟需一种兼顾效率与效果的方法。

Method: DRIFT将KL正则化RL目标等价转化为重要性加权监督学习：从固定参考策略离线采样交互轨迹，计算基于回报的重要性权重，然后通过加权SFT优化策略。

Result: DRIFT在性能上匹配或超越多轮RL基线，同时保持标准SFT的训练效率和简洁性。

Conclusion: DRIFT通过解耦采样与优化，有效弥合了在线RL与离线SFT之间的差距，为多轮交互场景下的LLM优化提供了高效实用的新框架。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed in multi-turn interactive settings where users or environments can iteratively provide lightweight feedback. Unfortunately, optimizing such behavior presents a sharp dilemma in practice: online reinforcement learning is able to effectively address multi-turn dynamics but is prohibitively expensive due to the cost of generating full correction trajectories at every update, whereas offline supervised fine-tuning (SFT) is efficient but suffers from distribution shift and behavioral collapse. To this end, we novelly propose DRIFT (Decoupled Rollouts and Importance-Weighted Fine-Tuning), a framework that operationalizes the theoretical insight that the KL-regularized RL objective is equivalent to importance-weighted supervised learning. DRIFT decouples rollout from optimization by sampling offline interaction trajectories from a fixed reference policy, deriving return-based importance weights, and optimizing the policy via weighted SFT on the resulting dataset. Empirically, we demonstrate that DRIFT matches or exceeds the performance of multi-turn reinforcement learning baselines while maintaining the training efficiency and simplicity of standard supervised fine-tuning. Code is available at https://github.com/2020-qqtcg/DRIFT.

</details>


### [116] [PithTrain: A Compact and Agent-Native MoE Training System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31463)
*Ruihang Lai,Hao Kang,Haozhan Tang,Akaash R. Parthasarathy,Zichun Yu,Junru Shao,Todd C. Mowry,Chenyan Xiong,Tianqi Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Agent任务效率（ATE）指标及PithTrain框架，在保持与生产框架同等吞吐量的同时，显著提升AI编码Agent在MoE训练框架上的理解、操作和扩展效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE训练框架经过多年工程优化，但难以适应新架构和系统优化；AI编码Agent应用于这些框架时存在隐性成本，而现有评估仅关注吞吐量，忽略了Agent与框架交互的效率问题。

Method: 提出Agent任务效率（ATE）指标；基于四个面向Agent的原生设计原则构建紧凑型框架PithTrain；引入ATE-Bench基准测试覆盖真实训练框架任务。

Result: PithTrain在吞吐量上与生产框架持平；在ATE-Bench上，Agent任务效率显著提升，Agent Turns减少62%，Active GPU Time降低64%。

Conclusion: 面向Agent原生的框架设计能大幅降低AI编码Agent理解和扩展MoE训练框架的隐性成本，为未来训练框架的发展提供了新方向。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) has become the dominant architecture for frontier language models. To meet this demand, production frameworks have built optimized MoE training stacks over years of engineering effort. Yet evolving these stacks for new architectures and system optimizations remains expensive. With the rise of AI coding agents, they could automate parts of training-framework development and accelerate this evolution. But applying them to these existing frameworks carries hidden costs, invisible to today's throughput-only evaluations. We name this missing dimension agent-task efficiency (ATE): the cost of using coding agents to understand, operate, and extend a framework. Grounded in four agent-native design principles, we build PithTrain, a compact, agent-native MoE training framework. We further introduce ATE-Bench, covering real-world training-framework tasks. Our evaluation shows PithTrain matches the throughput of production frameworks, and on ATE-Bench, PithTrain enables higher agent-task efficiency, with up to 62% fewer Agent Turns and 64% less Active GPU Time.

</details>


### [117] [GPU Forecasters: Language Models as Selective Surrogates for Kernel Runtime Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31464)
*Zaid Khan,Justin Chih-Yao Chen,Jaemin Cho,Elias Stengel-Eskin,Mohit Bansal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究如何利用LLM作为GPU的替代代理来预测GPU内核性能，从而减少昂贵的硬件评估成本。实验表明，LLM能准确预测相对内核性能，通过强化学习可进一步提升精度和校准度，在内核搜索中使同等GPU预算下评估的候选数量提升数倍，并找到更快的kernel。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU内核优化（如进化搜索或编码智能体）需要反复在目标硬件上测量性能，每次评估涉及编译和重复执行，成本高昂。随着LLM驱动的搜索规模扩大，这种on-device评估成为瓶颈，因此需要研究如何用LLM作为选择性GPU替代代理来预测内核性能。

Method: 提出LLM作为GPU选择性替代代理的方法，要求替代模型既准确又具选择性（在可能出错时主动 defer 给GPU）。使用强化学习提升预测准确性和置信度校准。评估指标包括预测准确性、校准度，以及在实际有限GPU预算下恢复快速内核的实用性。

Result: LLM能够准确预测相对内核性能；强化学习可进一步提升预测准确性和置信度校准；在相同GPU评估预算下，使用替代模型的内核搜索可评估数倍于基线的候选数量，并找到更快的内核。

Conclusion: LLM可以不仅作为内核生成器，还能作为GPU的虚拟模型，在内核优化中发挥更广泛的作用——通过作为性能预测代理来缓解on-device评估的瓶颈，从而提升内核搜索效率。

Abstract: GPU kernels are the workhorse of modern deep learning, and optimizing them (via evolutionary search or coding agents) usually requires repeated measurement on target hardware. While these measurements provide the ground-truth signal necessary for kernel search, they are costly, because each evaluation of a kernel requires compilation and repeated execution on a GPU. As improvements in LLM inference reduce the cost of writing novel kernels and LLM-driven searches scale to large search budgets, on-device evaluation becomes a bottleneck. To address this, we study how LLMs can serve as selective GPU surrogates for kernel evaluation, by forecasting the performance of proposed kernels. A useful surrogate should be accurate, and it should be selective, by knowing when it could be wrong, and deferring to the GPU. To evaluate surrogates, we measure whether their forecasts are accurate, calibrated, and practically useful for recovering fast kernels under limited GPU-measurement budgets. Next, we study whether reinforcement learning can improve forecast accuracy and confidence calibration. Our experiments demonstrate that LLMs can accurately forecast relative kernel performance, that their utility can be improved through reinforcement learning. Used inside a kernel search, the surrogate lets the search consider several times as many candidates under the same GPU evaluation budget, and that leads to finding faster kernels than an equal-budget baseline. These results suggest that LLMs can play a broader role in kernel optimization, by acting as virtual models of a GPU rather than solely as kernel generators for search.

</details>


### [118] [Graphical einops: bridging tensor networks and computation graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31485)
*Vincent Wang-Maścianica,Nikhil Khatri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种形式化的图形演算系统，用于einops支持的张量编程结构片段，通过嵌套分级管表示张量轴，使架构图具有证明能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习中的架构图通常仅具表示性，其暗示的张量程序恒等式仍需通过文字和张量轴操作来证明，缺乏形式化的图形证明工具。

Method: 将张量轴表示为基类型周围的嵌套分级管（graded tubes），管边界恢复无向张量网络视图，有向内部保留计算图的操作语义。核心重写规则是分级自然性（grade-naturality），即"滑动的眼镜"操作。

Result: 标准等变性证明可简化为短小的图推导；可将注意力掩码转换为预处理操作，恢复稀疏注意力块的高效实现。

Conclusion: 该图形演算使架构图从纯表示性工具升级为可证明的形式化系统，为张量编程提供了严格的图形化推理框架。

Abstract: Architecture diagrams are ubiquitous in deep learning, but they are usually only representational: the tensor-program identities they suggest are still proved by prose and tensor-axis manipulation. We introduce a formal graphical calculus for the structural fragment of tensor programming underlying einops, making such diagrams proof-enabling. Our calculus represents tensor axes as nested graded tubes around a base type. The tube boundary recovers the undirected tensor-network view of axes, while the directed interior retains the operational reading of computation graphs. The key rewrite is grade-naturality: sliding spectacles over tubes. Standard equivariance proofs become short diagrammatic derivations. We additionally demonstrate how our rewrite system may be applied to convert attention masks into pre-processing operations, recovering efficient implementations of sparse attention blocks.

</details>


### [119] [Assign and Add: A Mechanistic Study of Compositional Arithmetic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31497)
*Brady Exoo,Alberto Bietti,John Sous*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Transformer中组合泛化的机制，通过在变量赋值和模加法这一受控设置下进行分析，发现模型使用相同的MLP模块处理直接输入和间接变量赋值，训练动态呈现三个阶段：模加法学习、变量赋值结构学习、精炼泛化阶段，并从理论上解释了组合性如何从训练动态中涌现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Transformer如何实现组合泛化（compositional generalization），即模型能够组合技能执行训练中未见过的新任务组合的机制。

Method: 在变量赋值（variable assignment）和模加法（modular addition）的受控设置下，将训练数据划分为不相交集合，观察小型Transformer对未见过的变量-数字组合的泛化能力；通过机制分析（mechanistic analysis）揭示模型内部模块的使用方式；从经验角度分析训练动态，并结合理论框架解释组合性的涌现。

Result: 1) 小型Transformer能泛化到训练中未见过的新变量和数字组合；2) 同一个"模加法"MLP模块既用于直接输入也用于通过变量赋值机制的间接输入；3) 训练动态呈现三阶段：先学习模加法，再学习变量赋值所需的结构，最后精炼阶段泛化到未见过的困难序列。

Conclusion: 组合泛化可以自然地从Transformer内部机制的组合性中涌现，而非需要显式的特殊设计。

Abstract: Large language models are able to compose skills in order to perform complex tasks, many of which might not have been seen during training. The details of how exactly this composition occurs remain elusive. In this paper, we study a mechanism for compositional generalization in transformers by considering a simple controlled setting involving variable assignment and modular addition. By partitioning our training data into disjoint sets, we observe that small transformers are able to generalize to previously unseen combinations of variables and numbers. Our mechanistic analysis shows that the same ``modular addition'' MLP module is used whether the inputs are given directly or indirectly through a separate variable assignment mechanism. We also analyze the training dynamics from an empirical lens, which reveals three phases of learning: first, modular addition is learned, then the structure required for variable assignment, and finally a refinement phase where the model generalizes to some hard sequences not seen in training. Finally, we provide a theoretical framework to explain how compositionality emerges from training dynamics. These results suggest that compositional generalization can be a natural consequence of the compositionality of internal mechanisms in~transformers.

</details>


### [120] [On Efficient Scaling of GNNs via IO-Aware Layers Implementations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31500)
*Daria Fomina,Daniil Krasylnikov,Alexey Boykov,Andrey Dolgovyazov,Vyacheslav Zhdanovskiy,Fedor Velikonivtsev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对GNN中稀疏、不规则内存访问导致的瓶颈问题，将常用层分为三种内核族（SpMM卷积、归约聚合、注意力层），为每族开发了减少数据移动、提升局部性的GPU内核，在多种图上实现了显著加速（最高8.5倍）和内存减少（最高76倍），并发布了即插即用的实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNN框架（如DGL、PyTorch Geometric）支持通用消息传递，但复杂层常物化边级中间结果，增加内存流量，限制了大规模图上的可扩展性。

Method: 从I/O和算术强度视角出发，将常用GNN层分为SpMM卷积、归约聚合和注意力层三类；针对每类开发专用GPU内核减少数据移动、改善局部性；研究图重排序对不同内核映射的影响。

Result: 融合注意力内核：Graph Transformer最高3.9倍加速（中位数1.6倍），Tensor Core变体最高7.3倍；GATv2最高8.5倍加速（中位数2.0倍），峰值内存最高减少76倍（中位数6倍）；度感知归约内核最高10倍加速（中位数2.6倍）；SpMM层使用适当缓存的cuSPARSE最高8倍加速。

Conclusion: 通过内核族分类和定制优化可显著提升GNN性能，图重排序效果取决于内核映射方式；发布即插即用实现以支持可复现的硬件感知GNN加速。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) are bottlenecked by sparse, irregular memory access. Popular frameworks such as DGL and PyTorch Geometric support general message passing, but complex layers often materialize edge-wise intermediates, increasing memory traffic and limiting scalability on large graphs. We take an I/O- and arithmetic-intensity--centric view and show that widely used layers fall into three kernel families: SpMM-based convolutions, reduction-based aggregations, and attention-based layers (GATv2/Graph Transformer). For each family, we develop GPU kernels that reduce data movement, improve locality, and remain robust across realistic graphs. We also study graph reordering and find that its impact depends on the kernel mapping: it benefits neighbor-parallel (gather-dominated) kernels more consistently than feature-parallel designs. Empirically, our fused attention kernels reach up to $\textbf{3.9}\times$ speedup for Graph Transformer (median $\textbf{1.6}\times$), with Tensor Core (block-sparse) variants up to $\textbf{7.3}\times$ on locally dense graphs; for GATv2 we reach up to $\textbf{8.5}\times$ speedup (median $\textbf{2.0}\times$) while reducing peak memory by up to $\textbf{76}\times$ (median $\textbf{6}\times$). Our degree-aware reduction kernels achieve up to $\textbf{10}\times$ speedup (median $\textbf{2.6}\times$). For SpMM-based layers, properly cached cuSPARSE achieves up to $\textbf{8}\times$ speedup over DGL and outperforms evaluated custom baselines in the majority of evaluations. We release our implementations as drop-in replacements to support reproducible, hardware-aware GNN acceleration.

</details>


### [121] [Skill Reuse as Compression in Agentic RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31509)
*Zhikun Xu,Yu Feng,Jacob Dineen,Taiwei Shi,Jieyu Zhao,Ben Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ReuseRL方法，基于最小描述长度(MDL)原则，通过从成功轨迹中提取可复用的技能字典并添加分段代价惩罚，提升强化学习智能体的泛化能力。在ALFWorld、TextWorld-Cooking和Countdown-Stepwise任务上，ReuseRL在分布内和分布外均优于GRPO等基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RL训练的LLM智能体容易学习脆弱、任务特定的捷径行为，缺乏泛化能力。作者假设成功轨迹在结构上可压缩（分解为少量可复用抽象模式）时，智能体泛化能力更强。

Method: 1) 基于MDL原则，从成功轨迹中提取共享技能字典；2) 在RL目标函数中增加分段代价（segmentation cost），显式惩罚编码效率低下的特殊行为；3) 提供了该压缩惩罚的PAC-Bayes泛化界证明。

Result: 在ALFWorld、TextWorld-Cooking和Countdown-Stepwise三个环境中，ReuseRL在分布内和分布外任务上的成功率均优于vanilla GRPO和强round-length基线方法。

Conclusion: 结构可压缩性是提升RL智能体泛化能力的关键，基于MDL原则的压缩正则化方法有效抑制了过拟合于任务特定捷径的行为。

Abstract: Large language model agents trained with reinforcement learning (RL) often learn brittle, task-specific shortcuts. We hypothesize that agents generalize better when their successful trajectories are structurally compressible, decomposed into a small set of reusable abstract patterns. To formalize this, we introduce ReuseRL, which grounds agentic RL in the Minimum Description Length (MDL) principle. ReuseRL extracts a shared skill dictionary from successful trajectories and augments the RL objective with a segmentation cost, explicitly penalizing idiosyncratic behaviors that encode poorly. We prove a PAC-Bayes generalization bound for this compression penalty. Across ALFWorld, TextWorld-Cooking, and Countdown-Stepwise, ReuseRL improves in- and out-of-distribution success over vanilla GRPO and strong round-length baselines.

</details>


### [122] [On the Relationship Between Activation Outliers and Feature Death in Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31518)
*Elana Simon,Etowah Adams,James Zou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Sparse autoencoders（SAEs）在分解神经网络激活时存在特征死亡问题，即许多学到的特征从未激活。研究发现，激活中的维度级离群值（维度均值远大于逐token变化）是导致该问题的关键原因。离群严重度γ=‖μ‖/‖σ‖能有效预测初始死亡比率。去均值化（减去激活均值）可以完全消除离群导致的特征死亡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAEs在分解神经网络激活时，存在大量学习到的特征从未激活（特征死亡）的问题，这浪费了字典容量并可能重新引入叠加现象。不同模型间特征死亡率差异巨大（GPT-2接近0%，AlphaFold3超过70%），其背后的机制尚不明确，需要解释这一现象并提供解决方案。

Method: 1. 发现维度级激活离群值（维度均值远大于逐token变化）是特征死亡的原因；2. 定义离群严重度指标γ=‖μ‖/‖σ‖；3. 在454个模型-层组合（涵盖语言、视觉、蛋白质、基因组模型）上验证γ与初始死亡比率的相关性（Spearman ρ=0.89 for dead-by-TopK, 0.82 for dead-by-ReLU）；4. 分析训练过程中死特征复活机制，发现需要SAE偏置学习激活均值，该过程在高γ时极慢；5. 提出去均值化预处理来消除离群导致的特征死亡。

Result: γ=‖μ‖/‖σ‖可有效预测初始特征死亡比率（Spearman ρ最高达0.89）。去均值化（减去激活均值）能消除所有测试模型中离群导致的特征死亡，验证了该机制的正确性。

Conclusion: 激活中的维度级离群值是SAE特征死亡的根本原因，去均值化是一种简单有效的预处理手段，为何时及为何需要此预处理步骤提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) decompose neural network activations into interpretable features, but many learned features never activate, a problem called feature death that wastes dictionary capacity and can reintroduce superposition. Death rates vary dramatically between models: near-zero on GPT-2, over 70% on AlphaFold3 with identical configurations. We find that dimension-level activation outliers (dimensions whose mean magnitude is large relative to per-token variation) cause this by shifting pre-activations at initialization based on each feature's alignment with the activation mean. Features anti-aligned with the mean receive permanently negative pre-activations and never fire. We formalize outlier severity as $γ= \|μ\|/\|σ\|$; it predicts initial death rates (Spearman $ρ= 0.89$ for dead-by-TopK, $0.82$ for dead-by-ReLU) across 454 model-layer combinations spanning language, vision, protein, and genomic models. Dead features can revive during training, but recovery requires the SAE bias to learn the activation mean, a process that is prohibitively slow at high $γ$. Mean-centering (subtracting the activation mean) sidesteps this and eliminates outlier-induced death across all tested models, confirming the mechanism and providing a principled basis for when and why this preprocessing step is necessary.

</details>


### [123] [The Dynamic-Probabilistic Consistency Gap in Chaotic Surrogate Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31547)
*Andre Herz,Matthijs Pals,Daniel Durstewitz,Georgia Koppe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了动力系统重建（DSR）中存在的动态-概率一致性差距（DPC gap）：有限时域概率目标会损害动力学保真度或使预测不确定性与局部切线动力学脱钩。作者提出KAFFEE框架，基于可微扩展卡尔曼滤波，通过局部新息似然和Jacobian协方差传播来弥合这一差距，在随机超混沌Lorenz-96系统和13个混沌系统上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DSR方法在追求有限时域概率目标时，会导致动力学退化或预测不确定性与系统固有动力学（如混沌系统的Jacobian生成协方差增长）脱钩，缺乏一种能同时保证动力学保真度和概率一致性的训练框架。

Method: KAFFEE框架采用可微扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）进行训练，通过计算局部预测残差（新息）的似然作为优化目标，同时利用学习的局部Jacobian矩阵传输协方差，避免开放循环高斯展开目标对混沌系统膨胀动力学的惩罚。

Result: 在随机超混沌Lorenz-96系统上，KAFFEE减少了核坍塌、噪声掩蔽和盲不确定性三种失败模式，改善了动力学不变量重构，保持了竞争性预测分数。在13个混沌系统的基础模型概率适应任务中，KAFFEE实现了上下文贝叶斯滤波，同时基本保持了零样本动力学。

Conclusion: DPC gap是DSR中概率建模与动力学保真度之间的固有冲突，KAFFEE通过EKF框架有效弥合了这一差距，为动力系统重建中的不确定性量化提供了新的方法论基础。

Abstract: Dynamical systems reconstruction (DSR) aims to learn surrogate models that capture the dynamics underlying time-series data. Reliably deploying these surrogates requires uncertainty estimates consistent with the learned dynamics. We expose a dynamic-probabilistic consistency (DPC) gap: the pursuit of finite-horizon probabilistic objectives can degrade dynamics or decouple predictive uncertainty from the local tangent dynamics it ought to reflect. We isolate three mechanisms behind this gap: core collapse, noise masking, and blind uncertainty. Specifically, we show that open-loop Gaussian rollout objectives can penalize Jacobian-generated covariance growth in chaotic systems, encouraging optimization shortcuts that weaken physical expansion or decouple uncertainty from it. To mitigate this gap, we propose KAFFEE (Kalman-Aware Framework For Ergodic Emulation), a differentiable extended Kalman filter-based training framework that evaluates likelihood on local predictive residuals (innovations) while transporting covariance through learned local Jacobians. On stochastic hyperchaotic Lorenz-96, KAFFEE reduces the identified failure modes, improves reconstruction of dynamical invariants relative to open-loop objectives, and maintains competitive predictive scores. We further show that the DPC gap appears when probabilistically adapting a DSR foundation model across 13 chaotic systems, where KAFFEE enables in-context Bayesian filtering while largely preserving zero-shot dynamics.

</details>


### [124] [Giving Sensors a Voice: Multimodal JEPA for Semantic Time-Series Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31580)
*Utsav Dutta,Gerardo Pastrana,Sina Khoshfetrat Pakazad,Henrik Ohlsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CHARM框架，通过引入通道级文本描述和JEPA训练目标，在异构多元时间序列上学习通用表示，仅用线性探针即可在多项任务上取得强性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用表示学习在异构多元时间序列领域尚未得到充分探索，现有方法缺乏对通道级语义信息的利用和跨数据集的泛化能力。

Method: 采用通道顺序等变的Transformer编码器，融入通道级文本描述；使用联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）训练，设计新型损失函数促进信息丰富且时间稳定的嵌入；通过描述感知门控机制实现可解释的通道间关系学习。

Result: 在异常检测、分类、短期和长期预测任务中，学到的嵌入仅使用线性探针即取得强性能。

Conclusion: 性能主要来自JEPA目标和条件架构，文本描述作为跨数据集泛化的通道标识符，而非性能提升的核心因素。

Abstract: Transformer-based architectures have advanced sequence modeling in language and vision, yet general-purpose representation learning for heterogeneous multivariate time series remains underexplored. We introduce CHARM (Channel-Aware Representation Model), which incorporates channel-level textual descriptions into a Transformer encoder equivariant to channel order. CHARM is trained with a Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) and a novel loss promoting informative, temporally stable embeddings; latent-space prediction encourages robustness to sensor noise while description-aware gating provides interpretability through learned inter-channel relationships. Across anomaly detection, classification, and short- and long-term forecasting, the learned embeddings achieve strong performance using only a linear probe. Performance is driven primarily by the JEPA objective and conditioning architecture, with text descriptions serving as channel identifiers for cross-dataset generalization.

</details>


### [125] [A Tight Theory of Error Feedback Algorithms in Distributed Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31594)
*Daniel Berg Thomsen,Adrien Taylor,Aymeric Dieuleveut*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对分布式学习中的梯度压缩误差反馈算法EF和EF21进行了严格收敛分析，通过识别最优步长选择和构造最优Lyapunov函数，分别给出了两者紧的收敛保证，结果与智能体数量无关且恢复单智能体场景下的已知最优保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通信开销是分布式学习的主要瓶颈，梯度压缩虽可缓解但会损害收敛性。误差反馈机制可修复该问题，但现有变体众多且相对性能尚不明确。

Method: 通过识别最优步长选择并构造针对每种方法定制的Lyapunov函数，对EF和EF21两种主流误差反馈算法进行紧的收敛分析。

Result: 得到了与智能体数量无关的紧收敛保证，并恢复了单智能体场景下的已知最优结果。

Conclusion: EF和EF21在最优参数配置下具有理论保证的收敛性能，且分析统一了多智能体与单智能体场景下的收敛结果。

Abstract: Communication costs are a major bottleneck in distributed learning and first-order optimization. A common approach to alleviate this issue is to compress the gradient information exchanged between agents. However, such compression typically degrades the convergence guarantees of gradient-based methods. Error feedback mechanisms provide a simple and computationally cheap remedy for this issue, but numerous variants have been proposed, and their relative performance remains poorly understood. This paper provides tight convergence analyses for two of the main error-feedback algorithms from the literature, the classic Error Feedback method (EF) and Error Feedback 21 (EF21), by identifying optimal step-size choices and constructing optimal Lyapunov functions tailored to each method. The results hold independently of the number of agents and recover the known best guarantees possible in the single-agent regime.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [126] [ZAPS-DA: Zero-Phase Action Policy Smoothing with Decoupled Actor for Continuous Control in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30612)
*Faiq Shamass*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ZAPS-DA框架通过解耦演员网络和监督学习零相位滤波目标，在不修改基础RL训练流程、不引入后处理滤波和相位延迟的前提下，显著降低连续控制策略的动作抖动（MetaDrive中转向抖动减少14-21倍，Webots中8-45倍），同时保持或改善任务完成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关策略（off-policy RL）训练的连续控制策略常产生高频动作抖动，无法直接部署于物理执行器；事后滤波会引入相位延迟，而在演员损失中嵌入平滑惩罚会耦合RL梯度并混淆奖励回归与过度平滑。

Method: ZAPS-DA将主演员（由基础RL损失训练）与一个解耦演员分离，后者通过监督学习模仿存储在回放缓冲区中的零相位滤波目标进行训练。部署时使用解耦演员，其是从当前观测到平滑动作的前馈映射，无需推理时滤波或动作历史输入——称为"非因果滤波器的因果蒸馏"。采用幅度匹配的MSE损失实现跨优化器类的零超参数可移植性。

Result: 在MetaDrive中，转向抖动减少14-21倍，油门抖动减少3-5倍（所有p<10⁻⁴，Bonferroni校正），任务完成率匹配（成功率p=0.28，碰撞率p=0.31），奖励成本6.3%；在Webots自适应巡航控制环境中，转向抖动减少8-45倍，总任务失败率从2.0%降至0.7%，奖励持平（p=0.121）。

Conclusion: ZAPS-DA通过因果蒸馏非因果滤波器的方式，在不引入相位延迟和推理时滤波的前提下有效抑制动作抖动，在多个仿真环境中实现了帕累托改进（抖动大幅降低而任务性能不降甚至提升），为RL策略平滑部署提供了一种轻量且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Continuous control policies trained with off-policy reinforcement learning frequently exhibit high-frequency action jitter, rendering direct deployment on physical actuators impractical. Post-hoc filtering attenuates jitter but introduces phase lag; embedding smoothness penalties in the actor's loss couples them with the RL gradient and conflates reward regression with over-aggressive smoothing. We present ZAPS-DA, a framework that reduces action jitter at deployment with negligible phase lag and no post-processing. ZAPS-DA pairs an unmodified main actor (trained by the base RL loss) with a separate decoupled actor trained via supervised imitation of zero-phase filtered targets stored in the replay buffer. The deployed policy is the decoupled actor: a feed-forward map from the current observation to a smooth action, with no inference-time filter and no action-history input -- a mechanism we term causal distillation of a non-causal filter. A magnitude-matched MSE loss provides zero-hyperparameter portability across optimizer classes. Validated with Soft Actor-Critic and a Savitzky--Golay filter in two driving simulators using paired n=150 evaluation protocols: on MetaDrive, ZAPS-DA reduces steering jitter by 14--21x and throttle jitter by 3--5x (all $p < 10^{-4}$, Bonferroni-corrected) while matching task-completion (p=0.28 success, p=0.31 crash) at a 6.3% reward cost; on a custom Webots adaptive cruise control environment, the same SG configuration produces a Pareto improvement -- reward parity (p=0.121), 8--45x steering jitter reduction, and total task-failure rate reduced from 2.0% to 0.7%.

</details>


### [127] [ELAN4D: Embodiment-Centric 4D Supervision for Vision-Language-Action Models via Plug-and-Play Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30484)
*Zeyuan He,Bowen Yang,Zhirui Fang,Keru Zhou,Lei Jiang,Jingjing Qian,Fan Mo,Junchi Yan,Philip Torr,Xiu Li,Li Jiang,Jialin Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ELAN4D是一个以具身为中心的4D感知训练框架，通过预测机器人关键点未来轨迹作为时空监督信号，增强VLA策略的鲁棒性和泛化能力，在分布外扰动下表现显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型多为反应式策略，直接从当前观测回归动作，缺乏对未来动力学的显式建模，限制了其在分布外扰动下的泛化能力。

Method: 利用本体感知状态的前向运动学推导机器人关键点（如关节和末端执行器）的3D位移轨迹作为紧凑的度量监督；设计即插即用的辅助分支，包含轻量级轨迹解码器，通过梯度隔离将4D信号注入动作专家，推理时丢弃该解码器不改变基础策略接口。

Result: 在LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus、RoboTwin2.0及真实世界任务上，ELAN4D一致优于强VLA基线，在相机、背景和布局偏移等分布外扰动下取得最大增益。

Conclusion: 以具身为中心的4D时空监督能有效构建更鲁棒和泛化的操作策略。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown promise for robotic manipulation, yet most existing policies operate reactively by directly regressing actions from current observations, without explicitly modeling future dynamics. This limits their ability to generalize under out-of-distribution perturbations. To address this issue, we propose ELAN4D, an embodiment-centric, 4D-aware training framework that enhances VLA policies with future robot keypoint tracks as predictive spatio-temporal supervision. Using only forward kinematics from proprioceptive states, we derive 3D displacement tracks of robot keypoints, such as joints and the end-effector, with negligible preprocess cost. These tracks provide metric and compact supervision without requiring external trackers or reconstruction. A plug-and-play auxiliary branch with a lightweight track decoder injects this 4D signal into the action expert while preserving the pretrained vision-language backbone through gradient isolation. The track decoder is discarded during inference, leaving the base policy interface unchanged. Extensive experiments on LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, RoboTwin2.0 and real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate that ELAN4D consistently improves over strong VLA baselines, achieving the best overall performance and substantial gains under out-of-distribution perturbations, including camera, background, and layout shifts. These results highlight the effectiveness of embodiment-centric 4D supervision for building more robust and generalizable manipulation policies.

</details>


### [128] [Geometry-Aware Control Barrier Functions for Collision Avoidance via Bernstein Polynomial Approximations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30696)
*Siwon Jo,Yanze Zhang,Yupeng Yang,Wenhao Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于伯恩斯坦多项式符号距离场（BP-SDF）的几何感知控制障碍函数（CBF），统一表示机器人与障碍物形状，利用伯恩斯坦多项式的可微性在闭环中施加控制约束，实现高效安全的导航与多机器人避碰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CBF方法使用球体或超椭球等形状替代物处理不规则几何体时存在保守性过高或需要大量局部基元导致约束数量膨胀、实时性能下降的问题。

Method: 提出基于伯恩斯坦多项式符号距离场（BP-SDF）的几何感知CBF，统一表示障碍物与机器人形状，以统一最小距离表示障碍函数，利用伯恩斯坦多项式的可微性在闭环中施加控制约束。

Result: 在不同环境下的仿真中验证了该方法在单机器人导航和异构多机器人碰撞避免中的安全保证效率与性能。

Conclusion: BP-SDF CBF为不规则几何体提供了一种统一、高效且可微的安全约束表示方法，有效解决了传统方法的保守性与实时性权衡问题。

Abstract: Safe navigation often relies on well-defined conditions based on the shape of robots and obstacles, and can be challenging when they have irregular geometries. While Control Barrier Functions (CBFs) offer an efficient mechanism to enforce safe set forward invariance, common shape surrogates (e.g., spheres or super-ellipsoids) either are overly conservative in unstructured scenes or require many local primitives, which inflates constraint counts and degrades real-time performance. In this paper, we introduce a novel geometry-aware Control Barrier Function (CBF) based on Bernstein-Polynomial Signed Distance Fields (BP-SDFs). It provides a unified way to represent the obstacles and robots, so as to represent the barrier function with a unified minimum distance. Benefiting from the differentiability of the Bernstein polynomials, one can easily enforce the control constraints in a closed loop. We validate the method's efficiency and performance to guarantee safety in single-robot navigation and heterogeneous multi-robot collision avoidance via simulations under different environments.

</details>


### [129] [CoMo3R-SLAM: Collaborative Monocular Dense SLAM with Learned 3D Reconstruction Priors for Outdoor Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30488)
*Zhihao Cao,Qi Shao,Shuhao Zhai,Feng Tian,Anh Nguyen,Hesheng Wang,Baoru Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CoMo3R-SLAM是首个协作式单目稠密RGB SLAM系统，利用学习到的前馈3D重建先验实现多机器人户外建图，无需深度传感器，在Tanks and Temples和Waymo数据集上达到与RGB-D方法相当的性能，在线运行速度8 FPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协作式稠密SLAM系统依赖深度传感器，存在有效载荷、功耗和标定成本高等问题。单目相机虽轻量，但协作式单目稠密SLAM面临尺度歧义和不可靠的跨智能体数据关联挑战，尤其在低重叠和重复结构的户外场景中，传统特征匹配不可靠，亟需鲁棒的几何信息。

Method: 每个智能体运行先验引导前端实现实时跟踪和局部稠密融合；协调器执行稠密点图匹配进行跨智能体验证、封闭式Sim(3)规范同步以及GPU加速的全局BA（含分段级深度优化）。无需深度传感器和参数化内参，仅依赖单目RGB输入。

Result: 在Tanks and Temples和Waymo序列上，CoMo3R-SLAM在四个Tanks and Temples场景中的三个取得最佳ATE，在Waymo上达到与RGB-D方法相当或更优的精度，在线运行速度8 FPS。

Conclusion: CoMo3R-SLAM证明了仅凭单目RGB即可实现鲁棒的跨智能体约束和全局一致的度量建图，为协作式稠密SLAM提供了无需深度传感器的轻量化解决方案。

Abstract: Collaborative dense SLAM is essential for multi-robot teams to achieve scalable and consistent 3D perception across large-scale outdoor environments. Existing systems typically depend on depth sensors, incurring significant payload, power, and calibration costs. Monocular RGB cameras are a lightweight alternative, but collaborative monocular dense SLAM remains difficult due to scale ambiguity, unreliable inter-agent data association, especially in outdoor scenes where low overlap and repetitive structures make traditional feature matching unreliable, motivating robust geometric information. We propose CoMo3R-SLAM, the first collaborative monocular dense RGB SLAM system that leverages robust learned feed-forward 3D reconstruction priors for outdoor multi-agent mapping. Each agent runs a prior-guided front-end for real-time tracking and local dense fusion, while a coordinator performs dense pointmap matching for cross-agent verification, closed-form Sim(3) gauge synchronization, and GPU-accelerated global bundle adjustment with segment-level depth optimization. Requiring neither depth sensors nor parametric intrinsics, our system produces robust cross-agent constraints and globally consistent metric maps from monocular RGB alone. On Tanks and Temples and Waymo sequences, CoMo3R-SLAM achieves the best ATE on three of four Tanks and Temples scenes and competitive Waymo accuracy, matching or exceeding state-of-the-art RGB-D methods while running online at 8 FPS.

</details>


### [130] [Trajectory Planning for Non-Communicating Mobile Robots using Inverse Optimal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30906)
*Nina Majer,Yannick Epple,Xin Ye,Stefan Schwab,Sören Hohmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种结合逆最优控制与自预测的联合轨迹规划与预测算法，使非通信移动机器人在避碰场景中高效交互。相比基于恒定加速度估计的规划方法，中位到达时间加快9.8%，且求解器始终能收敛到可行解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非通信移动机器人在避碰场景中缺乏直接信息交换，现有方法在目标估计和预测准确性上存在不足，导致规划效率低下或求解失败。

Method: 采用逆最优控制从观测轨迹中估计所有机器人的未知目标状态；每台机器人从其他机器人视角进行自预测，并利用估计的目标状态求解联合预测问题；将预测结果用于轨迹规划。

Result: 在2-8台机器人的仿真中，中位到达时间比基于恒定加速度估计的方法快9.8%，且求解器在所有场景中均能成功求解规划和预测问题。

Conclusion: 所提出的联合预测与规划方法能有效提升非通信多机器人避碰场景的交互效率与求解鲁棒性。

Abstract: To enable an efficient interaction of non-communicating mobile robots in collision avoidance scenarios, we present a novel combined trajectory planning and prediction algorithm. Inverse optimal control is used to estimate unknown goal states of all robots based on observed past trajectories. Each robot also takes the perspective of other robots in considering self-prediction and solves a joint prediction problem using the estimated goal states. The resulting predictions are then considered for planning. Simulation results of scenarios with 2-8 robots show that the median of the durations until all vehicles reach their goals is 9.8 % faster compared to planning with constant acceleration based estimated goal states. Moreover, the proposed approach never leads to the solver being unable to find a solution to the planning or prediction problem.

</details>


### [131] [Physics-informed Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning under Hybrid Contact Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30503)
*Vittorio Giammarino,Anastasios Manganaris,Ahmed H. Qureshi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文分析了物理信息驱动的目标条件强化学习（Pi-GCRL）在接触式操作任务中的失效原因，并提出了一种接触感知的分层公式，以选择性地应用物理归纳偏差，从而将Pi-GCRL扩展至接触丰富的操作领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Pi-GCRL方法在导航和无物体目标到达域中有效，但在接触丰富任务（如操作）中，接触交互会导致混合动力学、模式依赖的可控性和非光滑价值景观，使得现有方法性能下降。因此需要分析和改进Pi-GCRL以适用于接触式操作。

Method: 首先分析接触丰富操作中混合动力学、模式依赖可控性和非光滑价值景观等结构特性导致现有Pi-GCRL方法失效的原因；然后提出接触感知和分层公式，选择性地在整个操作问题中应用物理信息归纳偏差。

Result: 揭示了现有Pi-GCRL方法在接触丰富操作中性能下降的结构性原因，并提出了能够扩展Pi-GCRL至接触式操作的接触感知分层方法。

Conclusion: 本文为将Pi-GCRL推广至接触丰富操作领域提供了原则性步骤，通过接触感知的分层公式解决了接触交互带来的混合动力学和价值景观非光滑性问题。

Abstract: Learning to reach arbitrary goals from sparse feedback requires agents to infer a rich notion of reachability across state--goal pairs. Goal-conditioned reinforcement learning (GCRL) tackles this challenge by learning policies that generalize across goals, but this generalization becomes increasingly difficult as the underlying dynamics become high-dimensional, hybrid, or contact-dependent. To address this issue, physics-informed GCRL (Pi-GCRL) introduces optimal-control-inspired inductive biases into goal-conditioned value learning. While Pi-GCRL methods have proven effective in navigation and object-free goal-reaching domains, their reliability in contact-rich tasks remains unclear, where contact interactions induce hybrid dynamics, mode-dependent controllability, and nonsmooth value landscapes. In this work, we show that these structural properties can cause existing Pi-GCRL methods to degrade when applied naively to contact-rich manipulation. Motivated by this analysis, we introduce contact-aware and hierarchical formulations that apply physics-informed inductive biases selectively across the manipulation problem. Our results provide a principled step toward extending Pi-GCRL to contact-rich manipulation.

</details>


### [132] [VLM-GLoc: Vision-Language Model Enhanced Monte Carlo Localization for Robust Semantic Global Localization in Cluttered Quasi-Static Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30506)
*Shivendra Agrawal,Bradley Hayes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VLM-GLoc方法，利用开放词汇视觉-语言模型（VLM）作为统一语义观测前端，实现层次化语义蒙特卡洛定位（MCL），在几何混淆的准静态环境（如杂货店、实验室）中，全局定位成功率分别达到70%和74%，显著优于传统几何方法和领域专用基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法在几何混淆环境（如平行货架杂货店、重复家具的办公室）中缺乏区分性几何特征，而领域专用视觉管道难以处理长尾语义分布和动态视觉杂乱，亟需一种统一、鲁棒的语义定位方法。

Method: 提出层次化语义MCL框架VLM-GLoc：利用开放词汇VLM作为语义观测前端，从三个方面获益——提取高区分性文本特征、隐式过滤模糊/动态对象、对持久性物体进行针对性数据增强；引入逆语义提案机制，通过文本到地图检索进行粒子初始化。

Result: 在两个真实环境（3,500 sq. ft.杂货店+手机平台、3,700 sq. ft.实验室+四足机器人）中，VLM-GLoc全局定位成功率达70%和74%，大幅超越传统纯几何方法和领域专用基线。

Conclusion: VLM能作为统一语义观测前端，有效解决几何混淆环境中的全局定位难题，兼具区分性、鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Global localization in geometrically aliased, quasi-static environments such as grocery stores, offices, schools, and hospitals poses a significant challenge for mobile robots. Grocery stores with parallel aisles and a long tailed distribution of products, as well as offices and labs with repetitive furniture such as chairs, desks, monitors, and doors, exemplify common indoor environments that present geometric and even semantic ambiguity. Traditional approaches rely either on distinct geometric features or on domain-specific vision pipelines that struggle with long-tail semantic distributions and transient visual clutter. We present VLM-GLoc, a method for hierarchical semantic Monte Carlo Localization (MCL) that leverages open-vocabulary Vision-Language Models (VLMs) as a unified semantic observation front-end. We hypothesize a three-fold benefit from VLMs: (1) extracting highly discriminative rich text features, (2) implicit quality filtering of blurry or dynamic objects, and (3) permanence reasoning for targeted data augmentation. We introduce an inverse semantic proposal mechanism that seeds particles via text-to-map retrieval. Evaluated across two real-world environments with different characteristics and two different platforms: a 3,500 sq. ft. grocery store with a cellphone and a 3,700 sq. ft. lab space with a quadruped, VLM-GLoc achieves 70% and 74% global localization success respectively, substantially outperforming traditional geometry-only and domain-specific baselines.

</details>


### [133] [On-Device Robotic Planning: Eliminating Inference Redundancy for Efficient Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31460)
*Joonhee Lee,Hyunseung Shin,Hyunmi Kim,Pei Zhang,Jeonggil Ko*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: REIS是一个受人类认知启发的机器人决策框架，通过利用机器人推理工作负载中的时间冗余（连续观测常产生相同动作和子目标），结合轻量级场景门控、KV引导的affordance路由和深思熟虑推理，在保持语义适应性的同时大幅减少不必要的推理，从而降低推理延迟，实现实时部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于大语言模型和视觉-语言模型的机器人推理策略虽然具备强大的语义规划能力，但推理延迟过高，限制了实际实时部署。

Method: 提出REIS框架，包含三个核心组件：轻量级场景门控（lightweight scene gating）、KV-steered affordance路由（KV-steered affordance routing）和深思熟虑推理（deliberative reasoning），通过利用时间冗余性来减少不必要的推理步骤。

Result: 在ALFRED和真实机器人任务上，REIS显著抑制了推理开销，同时保持了有竞争力的任务性能。

Conclusion: REIS通过模拟人类认知中避免重复推理的特性，有效解决了机器人推理中的延迟瓶颈，为实时机器人控制提供了可行的加速方案。

Abstract: Reasoning-based robotic policies using large language and vision-language models achieve strong semantic planning capabilities but mostly suffer from a high inference latency that limits practical real-time deployment. In this work, we observe that robotic reasoning workloads contain substantial temporal redundancy, where consecutive observations frequently produce identical actions and subgoals. Based on this insight, we present REIS, a human cognition inspired robotic decision-making framework that minimizes unnecessary reasoning while preserving semantic adaptability. REIS combines lightweight scene gating, KV-steered affordance routing, and deliberative reasoning to accelerate robotic control under embodied constraints. Experiments on ALFRED, and real-world robotic tasks demonstrate that REIS significantly suppresses reasoning overhead while maintaining competitive task performance.

</details>


### [134] [ARISTO Hand: Sensing-Driven Distal Hyperextension for Fine-Grained Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30508)
*Aaron Kim,Dong Ho Kang,Mark Helwig,Mingyo Seo,Kazuto Yokoyama,Tetsuya Narita,Luis Sentis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ARISTO Hand，一种肌腱驱动机械手，通过主动远端过伸机制和混合指尖传感架构（刚性指甲安装力-扭矩传感器+软性电容触觉阵列），实现了对薄物体的精确操作，将1-20mm厚度物体的拔出力提升2.76倍，并在SD卡插拔任务中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仿人机械手在处理薄物体时缺乏精确的接触几何和可靠的力感知能力，传统屈曲运动学在边缘接触场景下接近奇异点，导致本体感应力估计退化。

Method: 采用主动远端过伸机制超越标准屈曲的运动学限制；结合刚性指甲安装力-扭矩传感器和软性电容触觉阵列的混合传感架构。

Result: 对于1-20mm厚度物体，拔出力提升2.76倍；刚性传感器在边缘接触中提供可靠的力测量；在多阶段SD卡插拔任务中完成验证。

Conclusion: 主动过伸与混合指尖传感的协同设计显著增强了机械手对薄物体的操控能力，同时保持了对标准物体的抓取性能。

Abstract: Manipulating thin objects requires precise contact geometry and reliable force perception, yet many anthropomorphic robotic hands lack the mechanical and sensing capabilities needed for such interactions. We present the ARISTO Hand, a tendon-driven robotic hand that integrates active distal hyperextension with a hybrid fingertip-sensing architecture that combines a rigid, nail-mounted force-torque sensor and a soft capacitive tactile array. Active hyperextension enables controlled fingertip engagement beyond the kinematic limits of standard flexion, increasing pull-out force by 2.76x for object thicknesses of 1-20 mm while preserving the nominal grasp capability. The rigid nail-mounted sensor provides reliable force measurements during edge contacts, where the sensitivity of proprioceptive force estimation degrades as the contact geometry approaches kinematic singularities. We validate the proposed architecture through quantitative force characterization and a multi-stage SD card extraction and insertion task. Video and supplementary materials are available at: https://aristohand.github.io

</details>


### [135] [Any-ttach: Quick End-effector Swapping Enables Manipulation Dexterity with Simplicity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30569)
*Weizhe Ni,Jinzhou Li,Haoyu Li,Cody Andres Alessio-Bunnell,Wenjing Pan,Xianyi Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Any-ttach提出了一种以工具为中心的操控框架，通过快速更换末端执行器实现灵巧操作，而非依赖高自由度多指手。系统包括低成本自动更换机构、手持数据采集设备和任务规划框架，支持多种工具和末端模块。实验表明，该方法提高了工具更换可靠性、演示效率，降低了工具位姿变异性，并在长时任务（制作三明治、准备黄瓜）中成功执行六种工具技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，机器人灵巧操作追求构建复杂的高自由度多指手，但人手的功能表明其复杂性可能很大程度上是为了使用和制造工具。这启发作者探索一种更简单的工具为中心的灵巧操作范式。

Method: Any-ttach包含三个核心组件：(1) 低成本的自动末端执行器快速更换机构，兼容简单的开合接口；(2) 手持设备用于采集人类演示数据；(3) 任务规划框架，组合学习、参数化和规划的工具使用技能。系统支持日常工具、铰接工具（如剪刀）、Fin Ray手指和低成本拟人手等多种末端模块。

Result: 实验表明，Any-ttach提高了工具更换可靠性、演示效率，降低了工具位姿变异性，支持多样化的工具使用技能。在两个长时任务（制作三明治、准备黄瓜）中，通过末端执行器切换和执行监控，成功执行了六种工具使用子技能。

Conclusion: 机器人的操作能力不仅可以通过更复杂的末端执行器来扩展，也可以通过快速更换的工具和末端模块来扩展。这为机器人灵巧操作提供了一种新的思路：以工具为中心而非以手为中心。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation dexterity is often pursued by building increasingly complex high-DoF multifingered hands. While many robotic hands are designed to replicate human morphology, the functional role of human hands suggests a different perspective: much of their complexity may exist to enable tool use and tool making. This observation motivates Any-ttach, a tool-centric manipulation framework that treats quick end-effector swapping as a mechanism for dexterity with simplicity. Any-ttach combines a low-cost automatic swapping mechanism for an open-close robot interface, a handheld device for collecting human demonstrations, and a task planning framework that composes learned, parameterized, and planned tool-use skills. The system supports diverse tools and end-effector modules, including daily tools, articulated tools such as scissors, Fin Ray fingers, and a low-cost anthropomorphic hand, through the same shared interface. Our experiments show that Any-ttach improves tool-swapping reliability, increases demonstration efficiency, reduces tool-pose variability, and supports diverse tool-use skills. In two long-horizon tasks, making a sandwich and preparing a cucumber, Any-ttach executes six tool-use subskills through end-effector switching and execution monitoring. These results suggest that robots can expand manipulation capability not only through more complex end-effectors, but also through rapidly exchangeable tools and end-effector modules. More details and videos are available at https://any-ttach.github.io/.

</details>


### [136] [Caspar: CUDA Accelerator for Symbolic Programming with Adaptive Reordering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30583)
*Emil Martens,Aaron Miller,Matias Varnum,Annette Stahl*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Caspar是一个利用GPU加速机器人领域非线性优化的库，通过从符号表达式自动生成优化的CUDA核心，在BAL数据集上的光束法平差任务中比现有最优方案快5-20倍，且内存占用更少、精度相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 让现代GPU的计算能力在机器人领域更易获取，弥合Python符号编程与C++高性能GPU运行时之间的鸿沟。

Method: 基于SymForce库，用户定义符号残差函数（含李群运算），Caspar通过符号微分自动生成优化的CUDA核心和接口，执行非线性优化。

Result: 在BAL数据集的光束法平差任务中，比现有最优方案快5-20倍，内存需求更低，精度相当。

Conclusion: 符号式GPU编程方法在机器人优化问题中具有显著性能优势，Caspar作为SymForce的一部分已开源。

Abstract: We present Caspar, a library that makes the power of modern GPUs more accessible in robotics and provides a state-of-the-art nonlinear GPU solver that can be applied to a wide range of different optimization problems. Caspar bridges the gap between expressive symbolic programming in Python and high-performance GPU runtimes in C++ by automatically generating optimized CUDA kernels from symbolic expressions. Building on the SymForce library, users can easily define and combine symbolic expressions, including Lie group operations, to generate custom CUDA kernels. To use Caspar as a solver, users need only define the symbolic residual functions; Caspar then uses symbolic differentiation to generate the necessary GPU kernels and interfaces to perform nonlinear optimization.
  In this paper, we present the core components of Caspar and showcase its performance by performing bundle adjustment on the Bundle Adjustment in the Large (BAL) dataset. We benchmark Caspar against other state-of-the-art bundle adjusters and show that it is 5 to 20 times faster than the best alternative, requires less memory, and achieves similar accuracy. This illustrates the benefit of our symbolic GPU programming approach. Caspar is released as part of SymForce and is freely available at https://github.com/symforce-org/symforce

</details>


### [137] [Exploiting Chordal Sparsity for Globally Optimal Estimation with Factor Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30617)
*Avinash Subramanian,Connor Holmes,Timothy D. Barfoot,Frank Dellaert,Frederike Dümbgen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文在GTSAM框架中实现了自动构建凸半定规划(SDP)松弛的方法，并通过利用贝叶斯树结构分解SDP问题，显著加速了弦稀疏问题的全局求解，同时保持了与标准局部求解器相当的可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于因子图的鲁棒状态估计对机器人感知、导航和控制至关重要，但标准局部求解器可能收敛到较差的局部最小值，带来安全隐患。而凸松弛技术虽能全局求解或认证，但需要大量人工构建且求解大规模SDP计算成本高。

Method: 1) 在GTSAM框架中创建自动构建凸SDP松弛的过程，支持常见因子和变量类型；2) 利用GTSAM原生的贝叶斯树结构对SDP问题进行分解，加速弦稀疏问题的求解。

Result: 在3D位姿图SLAM（环形因子图）和2D定位问题（链式因子图）两个案例中，该结构感知的全局估计器相比标准局部求解器展现出更优的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 该方法降低了凸松弛技术的使用门槛，同时通过结构分解显著提升了求解效率，为机器人状态估计的全局优化提供了实用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Robust and efficient state estimation is crucial for perception, navigation, and control in robotics. State estimation problems are conveniently modeled using the factor-graph framework as enabled by modern software packages such as GTSAM or g2o. However, the standard solvers included in such frameworks are local and may converge to poor local minima, posing significant safety concerns. Conversely, techniques based on convex relaxations have been shown to provide a means of globally solving or certifying many state estimation problems. However, these relaxations 1) often require substantial effort to formulate, and 2) may incur significantly higher cost compared to efficient local solvers, as they require solving a large semidefinite program (SDP). In this work, we address both shortcomings by 1) creating a new procedure within the GTSAM framework for automatically constructing convex SDP relaxations for any factor graphs with common factor and variable types, and by 2) exploiting the Bayes tree constructions native to GTSAM to decompose the SDP problem, leading to significant speedup in solver time for chordally sparse problems. We demonstrate the favorable scaling of this structure-exploiting global estimator compared to standard local solvers for two case studies: A 3D pose-graph SLAM problem with a ring factor graph and a 2D localization problem with a chain factor graph. The software framework is available at https://github.com/borglab/gtsam.

</details>


### [138] [Bidirectional Incremental Generalized Hybrid A*](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30647)
*Sidharth Talia,Oren Salzman,Siddhartha Srinivasa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出双向增量广义混合A*（Bi-IGHA*）算法，通过双向搜索缓解冻结顶点隐藏解的问题，在保持单调成本改进和终止保证的同时，显著减少高维复杂动力学规划中的节点扩展量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非结构化环境中，具有复杂动力学的系统难以预计算运动基元，而传统A*受维数灾难影响计算不可行。Hybrid A*通过离散化状态空间减轻了负担，但造成了树搜索与离散化分辨率之间的耦合。IGHA*通过层次化分辨率上的增量搜索打破了这一耦合，但其冻结顶点机制会隐藏解支持节点。本文旨在通过双向搜索从根本上缓解这一问题。

Method: 将IGHA*扩展到双向搜索范式，提出Bi-IGHA*算法。该方法从起点和终点同时进行层次化分辨率搜索，在每次迭代中冻结顶点供后续搜索复用，通过双向扩展从根本上缓解单向搜索中冻结顶点隐藏解支持节点的问题，同时保持IGHA*的单调成本改进和终止保证。

Result: 在R3、R4和R6规划问题上的实验表明，Bi-IGHA*显著减少了节点扩展量。在高速越野自主驾驶的动力学规划任务中，Bi-IGHA*在实现等效闭环性能的同时，所需扩展量大幅减少。

Conclusion: Bi-IGHA*通过双向搜索有效缓解了冻结顶点隐藏解的问题，在高维复杂动力学规划中比IGHA*更高效，保持了理论保证，并在实际越野自主驾驶任务中验证了实用性。

Abstract: We focus on the problem of efficient anytime kinodynamic planning for systems with complex dynamics in unstructured environments that make precomputing motion primitives infeasible. Directly applying A* to such problems is computationally infeasible due to the curse of dimensionality. Methods such as Hybrid A* addressed this burden by discretizing the state space, but in turn creating a coupling between tree discovery and the discretization resolution. The Incremental Generalized Hybrid A* (IGHA*) performs search over a hierarchy of resolutions in an anytime fashion to break this coupling, by freezing vertices to use in later search iterations rather than pruning them. However, the frozen vertices can hide solution-supporting vertices from the search at a particular iteration. While classical bidirectional search is motivated by the reduction of search depth, extending IGHA* into the bidirectional setting (termed Bi-IGHA*) obtains additional benefit by fundamentally mitigating the behaviour induced by frozen vertices hiding solutions. We show that Bi-IGHA* preserves IGHA*'s guarantees on monotonic cost improvement and termination. We empirically show that Bi-IGHA* substantially reduces expansions on R3, R4, and R6 planning problems, and achieves equivalent closed-loop performance with kinodynamic planning for high-speed off-road autonomy while requiring significantly fewer expansions. Website: https://personalrobotics.github.io/IGHAStar/biighastar.html

</details>


### [139] [Primitive Subspaces Mediate Few-Shot Transfer in VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30695)
*Anya Singh,Cabrel Happi,Jai Relan,Varun Nair,Vidyut Baradwaj*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于原始技能感知训练（primitive-aware training）的方法，使视觉-语言-行动（VLA）策略能够在无需微调的情况下，通过少量演示（仅3次）即可完成新任务的零样本或少样本迁移，样本效率比传统平面策略训练提升3倍。通过消融实验证明原始技能表征是迁移能力的因果必要条件，并纠正了评估分块策略时的单步动作范围门控膨胀导致的高误报率问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业环境中部署VLA策略需要低成本的任务教学能力，但现有VLA模型每个新任务都需要微调，成本高昂。作者希望探索是否可以通过原始技能感知训练产生可迁移的技能库，使得策略在推理时仅需少量演示即可组合完成未训练过的任务。

Method: 在REASSEMBLE接触丰富装配数据集上，对OpenVLA和π_{0.5}两种VLA架构采用匹配的LoRA微调配方和锁定超参数进行训练。对比平面轨迹训练和原始技能分割的训练方式（使用原始技能特定的语言提示）。在6个保留的对象-任务组合上进行少样本迁移评估（0,1,3,5,10个演示），不更新权重。在LIBERO-Long数据集上验证。通过消融原始技能可解码隐状态子空间来建立因果关系。

Result: 原始技能训练模型仅需3个演示即可达到微调上界性能的78%，而平面训练模型需要10个演示才能达到同等水平——样本效率差距达3倍，且在多个种子、架构和数据集上一致复现。消融原始技能子空间导致少样本迁移性能下降32个百分点，而消融等维度随机子空间无影响。识别并纠正了分块策略评估中单步动作范围门控膨胀导致误报率数量级升高的问题。

Conclusion: 原始技能感知训练是一种有效的策略，可以显著提升VLA策略的少样本迁移能力，使工业场景中低成本的任务扩展成为可能。原始技能表征是迁移能力的因果必要条件而非偶然相关。评估分块策略时需注意动作范围门控膨胀导致的评估偏差。

Abstract: Deploying vision-language-action (VLA) policies in industrial environments requires the ability to teach new tasks at low cost, a property current VLAs lack, since each new task requires fine-tuning. We investigate whether primitive-aware training produces a transferable artifact: a learned library of sub-skills that can be composed at inference time, conditioned on a small number of demonstrations, to perform tasks the policy was never trained on. We train two VLA architectures with different inductive biases, OpenVLA and $π_{0.5}$, on the REASSEMBLE contact-rich assembly dataset under matched LoRA fine-tuning recipes and locked hyperparameters, varying training between flat trajectories and primitive-segmented episodes with primitive-specific language prompts. We hold out 6 object-task combinations from training and evaluate few-shot transfer: models receive $m \in \{0, 1, 3, 5, 10\}$ demonstrations of a held-out task and attempt execution without weight updates. We replicate across three training seeds and validate on a second dataset (LIBERO-Long). Primitive-trained models reach 78% of fine-tuned upper-bound performance with only m=3 demonstrations, while flat-trained models require m=10 demonstrations to reach the same level -- a $3\times$ sample efficiency gap that replicates across seeds, architectures, and datasets. To establish causation, we ablate the primitive-decodable subspace of hidden states and show few-shot transfer degrades by 32 percentage points while ablating a random subspace of equal dimensionality has no effect, indicating primitive representations are causally necessary rather than incidentally correlated with transfer. We identify and correct a methodological pitfall in evaluating chunked policies: family-wise inflation of single-step action-range gates produces order-of-magnitude higher false-failure rates against ground-truth human demonstrations.

</details>


### [140] [GSAM: A Generalizable and Safe Robotic Framework for Articulated Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30740)
*Beichen Shao,Mengying Xie,Heng Su,Wanyi Zhang,Mingyan Li,Yan Ding,Fausto Giunchiglia,Chao Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GSAM是一个通用且安全的机器人操作框架，通过视觉感知器生成运动学参数、VLM精炼器进行常识推理校正、交互约束函数生成器结合LLM进行轨迹规划，在50个铰链物体任务中相比最佳基线标准差降低3.1%，操作成功率提升36.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理铰接物体时忽略了物体多样性和末端执行器与手柄交互的复杂性，导致泛化能力有限且容易发生破坏性碰撞。

Method: 提出GSAM框架：(1) 视觉感知器生成运动学参数；(2) 微调VLM精炼器利用COT常识推理校正感知偏差；(3) 交互约束函数生成器整合铰接物体、交互姿态和避障知识；(4) LLM将约束函数化并应用于轨迹与姿态规划；(5) 运动学感知操作规划器验证可到达性。

Result: 在5类物体的50个铰链任务和50个随机初始化的末端执行器-手柄配置上，GSAM相比最佳基线标准差降低3.1%，操作成功率提升36.0%。

Conclusion: GSAM在物体泛化和交互安全方面优于现有方法，有效解决了铰接物体操作中的多样性和碰撞问题。

Abstract: Articulated object manipulation is a unique challenge for service robots. Existing methods employ end-to-end policy learning, visionmotion planning, and large-language/visual-language model (LLM/VLM), but often overlook the diversity of articulated objects and the complexity of interactions between end-effector and handle, leading to limited generalization and destructive collisions. To address this, we propose GSAM, a generalizable and safe robotic framework for articulated object manipulation. Specifically, a vision-based perceiver generates the kinematic parameters. Considering that pre-trained markers in perceiver yield raw estimations that may deviate from commonsense, we present a f ine-tuned VLM-based refiner, using chain-of-thought (COT) commonsense reasoning to refine perception. To prevent destructive collisions, we design an interaction constraint function generator, integrating articulated object, interaction pose, and obstacle avoidance knowledge into a base. LLM then functionalize these constraints and apply them to trajectory and posture planning. A kinematic-aware manipulation planner verifies reachability for trajectory and posture. Experiments on 50 hinge tasks across 5 object categories and 50 randomly initialized end-effectorhandle configurations show that GSAM reduces standard deviation by 3.1% and improves manipulation success rate by 36.0% compared to the best baseline, respectively demonstrating the superior object generalization and interaction safety of GSAM in practical scenarios.

</details>


### [141] [SSR: Scaling Surefooted and Symmetric Humanoid Traversal to the Open World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30770)
*Ruiqi Yu,Yiwen Wang,Yuan Hao,Jun WU,Qiuguo Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SSR是一个基于第一人称视觉的人形机器人开放世界穿越框架，通过想象足端引导、等变潜空间对称性增强和地形多判别器运动先验，实现了在多样化真实地形上的安全、稳定、高质量穿越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将人形机器人的穿越能力扩展到开放世界是实际部署的关键，但异构地形下的视觉引导足部放置、高动态运动中的安全可靠性以及协调自然的全身行为仍然具有挑战性。

Method: 提出SSR端到端框架：1）想象足端引导机制，建模即将触地的摆动足接触并评估支撑性，引导预触地阶段向稳定区域调整；2）等变潜空间对称增强，在高维视觉观测下高效诱导双侧协调；3）地形特定多判别器运动先验，鼓励跨场景的类人行为。

Result: 在多种真实地形上实现安全、稳定、高质量穿越，包括不同结构的楼梯、宽间隙和高平台等极端挑战，并在开放户外环境中实现可靠的长时程穿越。

Conclusion: SSR通过想象足端引导和等变对称增强等创新设计，有效解决了人形机器人开放世界视觉穿越的关键难题，展现了良好的泛化能力和实际部署潜力。

Abstract: Extending humanoid traversal to the open world is key to practical deployment in human environments, but remains challenging. The robot must use vision to ensure safe and reliable foot placement on heterogeneous terrain under highly dynamic motion, while producing coordinated, natural whole-body behaviors. We propose SSR, an efficient end-to-end framework for egocentric vision-based humanoid traversal that jointly learns these capabilities. SSR introduces imagined foothold guidance, which learns to model forthcoming swing-foot contacts and evaluates their support to guide pre-touchdown swings toward stable regions, reducing edge slips. It further employs equivariant latent-space symmetry augmentation to efficiently induce bilateral coordination under high-dimensional visual observations, and uses terrain-specific multi-discriminator motion priors to encourage human-like behavior across scenes. Extensive experiments show that SSR achieves safe, stable, and high-quality locomotion on diverse real-world terrains, including stairs with varied structures and extreme challenges such as wide gaps and high platforms, while enabling reliable long-horizon traversal in open outdoor environments.

</details>


### [142] [Object-Informed Model Predictive Path Integral Control for Non-Prehensile Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30778)
*Nikola Raicevic,Bharath Raam Radhakrishnan,Chenbin Yu,Ki Myung Brian Lee,Nikolay Atanasov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种分层MPPI控制方法，通过先求解简化的物体级规划作为参考，再引导机器人-物体联合规划，在非抓取操作中实现了40%（仿真）和20%（实际）的任务成功率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非抓取机器人操作中的长时域规划因欠驱动和不连续交互而极具挑战，现有方法难以在长时域预测中兼顾效率与成功率。

Method: 采用分层模型预测路径积分（MPPI）控制：先求解简化的物体独立运动规划（假设物体可被直接驱动），将得到的物体轨迹作为参考，再求解机器人-物体联合规划问题。

Result: 仿真中任务成功率提升40%，控制频率提升26%；实物实验中成功率提升20%，计算开销与标准MPPI相当。

Conclusion: 物体信息引导的分层MPPI方法能有效提升非抓取操作任务的长时域规划性能，且额外计算开销极小。

Abstract: Long-horizon planning for non-prehensile robot manipulation is challenging due to underactuated and discontinuous interactions. We propose a hierarchical formulation of model predictive path integral (MPPI) control that guides robot-level planning with a separately computed object-level plan to achieve efficient long-horizon prediction. We first solve a simplified object-only problem, assuming the object can be actuated directly, and use the planned object trajectory as a reference in solving the joint robot-object planning problem. We evaluate our method in both simulation and hardware using a 6-DoF xArm6 manipulator to perform object pushing tasks in which the target object must reach a goal while avoiding static obstacles, necessitating non-myopic reasoning. Our object-informed MPPI increases task success by 40\% with a 26\% faster control frequency in simulation, and by 20\% in real experiments with similar computation as regular MPPI.

</details>


### [143] [Two Degree-of-Freedom Vibratory Transport in a Grasp](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30780)
*C. L. Yako,Shenli Yuan,Kenneth Salisbury*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文利用非对称振动实现抓取物体的二自由度（平移+旋转）灵巧操作，通过闭环位置控制产生周期性粘滑波形，理论分析并实验验证了粘性加速度和滑动加速度对平均速度的影响，并在平行夹爪配置中成功演示了多种物体的双向平移和旋转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现无需复杂机械结构的抓取物体多自由度灵巧操作，利用非对称振动原理简化驱动方式。

Method: 通过闭环位置控制移动表面产生周期性粘滑波形的非对称振动；理论分析两个波形参数（粘性加速度和滑动加速度）对平均速度的影响；搭建实验装置控制夹紧力并记录物体运动；开发2-DoF振动表面（平移+绕法线旋转），以平行夹爪配置实现多种物体的双向平移和旋转。

Result: 理论预测的粘性加速度和滑动加速度对平均速度的影响趋势得到实验验证；2-DoF振动表面在平行夹爪中成功实现多种抓取物体的双向平移和旋转；平移中的波形趋势同样适用于面内旋转。

Conclusion: 非对称振动可作为一种有效的二自由度灵巧操作方法，其波形参数与运动速度的关系具有理论可预测性和实验可重复性。

Abstract: In this paper, we use asymmetric vibrations to demonstrate two degree-of-freedom (DoF) in-hand manipulation of grasped parts. The asymmetric vibrations are achieved through closed-loop position control of a moving surface, which applies a periodic stick-slip waveform to the part to be manipulated. We show analytically how two vibratory waveform parameters, the sticking acceleration and the slipping acceleration, affect average part velocity when moving against gravity. The theoretical trends are then validated using an experimental setup where the squeeze force is controlled and part motion is recorded by a high-resolution encoder. We also develop a 2-DoF vibratory surface capable of translation in one direction and rotation about the surface normal. Using two of these 2-DoF surfaces in a parallel jaw gripper configuration, we bidirectionally translate and rotate a variety of grasped parts, as well as demonstrate that the same waveform trends for translation also persist for in-plane rotation.

</details>


### [144] [Feat2Go: Visual Feature-Grounded Value Estimation for Embodied Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30795)
*Junyang Shu,Zhiwei Lin,Bingqing Wei,Yongtao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Feat2Go框架，通过预训练视觉世界模型导出连续进度目标，训练价值模型预测进度并重塑终端奖励，从而在无需人工奖励工程的情况下，显著提升VLA模型在单臂和双臂操作任务中的强化学习效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习能提升VLA模型能力并避免模仿学习的高数据需求，但稀疏监督和长程操作中奖励信号设计困难限制了其有效性。

Method: 首先从预训练视觉世界模型中通过补丁级相似度测量导出连续进度目标，并用趋势聚类将情节划分为语义阶段；然后训练价值模型预测该结构进度，并用预测值重塑终端奖励以优化策略。

Result: 在ManiSkill3上，OpenVLA-OFT的平均分布外成功率从17.5%提升至82.9%，同时保留96.9%的分布内性能；在RoboTwin 2.0上，平均成功率88.8%，优于先前强化学习方法。

Conclusion: Feat2Go兼容现有VLA策略强化学习流程（包括PPO和GRPO），无需人工奖励工程，在单臂和双臂操作任务中一致提升了VLA模型性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning is a promising approach for improving the capabilities of vision-language-action (VLA) models while avoiding the heavy data requirements of imitation learning. However, its effectiveness for VLA models is often constrained by sparse supervision and the difficulty of designing informative reward signals for long-horizon manipulation. In this work, we present Feat2Go, a fine-grained value estimation framework for embodied reinforcement learning. Specifically, Feat2Go first derives a continuous progress target from a pretrained visual world model by measuring patch-level similarity to subgoal states and partitioning episodes into semantic stages with trend-based clustering. We then train an embodied value model to predict this structural progress from the current observation and task instruction, and use the predicted value to reshape terminal rewards during policy optimization. The proposed framework is compatible with existing VLA policy reinforcement learning pipelines, including PPO and GRPO, and does not rely on manual reward engineering. Extensive experiments on ManiSkill3 and RoboTwin 2.0 demonstrate that Feat2Go consistently improves the performance of existing VLA models under both single-arm and bimanual manipulation settings. More specifically, on ManiSkill3, Feat2Go improves OpenVLAOFT from 17.5% to 82.9% average out-of-distribution success while retaining 96.9% in-distribution performance. On RoboTwin 2.0, Feat2Go achieves an average success rate of 88.8% in domain-randomized task settings, outperforming prior reinforcement learning methods.

</details>


### [145] [Hide-and-Seek in Trajectories: Discovering Failure Signals for VLA Runtime Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30834)
*Seongheon Park,Wendi Li,Changdae Oh,Samuel Yeh,Zsolt Kira,Michael Hagenow,Sharon Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Hide-and-Seek框架，通过轨迹级监督和对比学习实现VLA模型执行失败检测，无需逐步骤标注，在多个基准和实物平台上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在执行任务时易发生执行失败，现有检测方法要么依赖昂贵的动作重采样或外部模型，要么将轨迹级标签均匀传播到每个时间步，掩盖了局部失败信号。需要一种高效且无需细粒度标注的失败检测方法。

Method: 将VLA失败检测形式化为粗粒度监督学习问题，结合轨迹间和轨迹内对比学习目标，从轨迹级监督中定位指示失败的关键动作，诱导出具有时间结构的失败信号，无需任何步骤级标注。

Result: 在LIBERO、VLABench和真实机器人平台上，针对OpenVLA、π₀和π₀.₅三种代表性VLA策略进行评估，在多任务失败检测中取得SOTA性能，在共形预测下实现了实用的准确率-及时性权衡，并泛化到未见任务。

Conclusion: Hide-and-Seek通过粗粒度监督和对比学习高效解决了VLA模型执行失败检测问题，无需细粒度标注，具有良好的泛化能力和实用价值。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models enable robots to follow natural language instructions and generalize across diverse tasks, but they remain vulnerable to execution failures that compromise reliability in real-world deployment. Detecting such failures during execution is therefore critical for the robust deployment of embodied systems. Existing failure detection methods either rely on expensive action resampling or external models, while alternatives propagate trajectory-level labels uniformly across every timestep, obscuring localized failure signals. In this paper, we propose \textbf{Hide-and-Seek}, a framework that formulates VLA failure detection as a coarsely supervised learning problem. By combining inter-trajectory and intra-trajectory contrastive objectives, Hide-and-Seek localizes failure-indicative actions and induces temporally structured failure signals from trajectory-level supervision alone, without any step-level annotation. We evaluate Hide-and-Seek on LIBERO, VLABench, and a real-world robotic platform across three representative VLA policies: OpenVLA, $π_0$, and $π_{0.5}$.Our method achieves state-of-the-art multi-task failure detection performance with a practical accuracy--timeliness trade-off under conformal prediction, and generalizes well to both seen and unseen tasks.

</details>


### [146] [High-Load-Density Electro-Permanent Magnetic Foot with Controllable Adhesion for Quadruped Wall-Climbing Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30849)
*An Li,Bo Tao,I-Ming Chen,Han Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于四足机器人铁磁表面爬行的电永磁吸附脚，采用环形Halbach电永磁体( CHN-EPM )和力反馈控制，实现了超过1000 N的吸附力、200:1的负载重量比，并在多种铁磁表面上验证了稳定 locomotion。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了实现四足机器人在铁磁表面上的可靠爬行运动，需要解决高负载密度、可控吸附以及非理想接触条件下的稳定吸附问题。

Method: 提出环形Halbach电永磁体( CHN-EPM )，利用三维磁路结构和磁通汇聚效应实现分布式并行磁路；开发磁化驱动器与两级脉冲电流控制策略；集成柔性压力传感器进行接触力反馈，实现吸附状态监测与切换。

Result: 最大吸附力超过1000 N，负载重量比超过200:1；在商用四足机器人GO2上实现了天花板和垂直壁面的高负载吸附，以及在喷漆、穿孔和曲面铁磁表面上的稳定运动。

Conclusion: 所提出的电永磁吸附脚具有高负载密度、抗气隙变化能力强的特点，能够在部分接触等不确定条件下保持有效吸附，为四足机器人在铁磁表面上的爬行应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: To enable reliable climbing locomotion of quadruped robots on ferromagnetic surfaces, this paper presents a high-load-density electro-permanent magnetic foot with controllable adhesion, featuring force-feedback circular Halbach-net electro-permanent magnet (CHN-EPM) adhesion units and a magnetization control system. Due to its three-dimensional magnetic circuit structure and flux-concentration effect, the CHN-EPM enables a distributed parallel magnetic flux path with enhanced flux utilization, resulting in reduced sensitivity to air-gap variations and allowing effective adhesion to be maintained even under partial contact conditions. The proposed CHN-EPM generates a maximum adhesion force exceeding 1000 N with a load-to-weight ratio over 200:1. A magnetization driver and a two-stage pulse current control strategy are developed to regulate the excitation current amplitude and duration, enabling accurate and reliable magnetization. By incorporating a flexible pressure sensor for contact force feedback, the system can effectively monitor attachment and detachment states, ensuring robust adhesion switching under uncertain contact conditions. The proposed system is integrated into a commercial quadruped robot (Unitree GO2), demonstrating high-load adhesion on ceiling and vertical-wall surfaces and stable locomotion on painted, perforated, and curved ferromagnetic surfaces.

</details>


### [147] [Wall-OSS-0.5 Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30877)
*Ryan Yu,Pushi Zhang,Starrick Liu,Brae Liu,Miracle Kang,Shalfun Li,Lights Shi,Ellie Ma,Ping Yang,Chris Pan,Jerry Chen,Dongxiu Liu,Rain Sun,Miles Guo,Byron Zhang,Hugo Zhou,Zach Xu,Vincent Chen,Harrison Huang,James Wang,Dance Kuzi,Andy Zhai,Hang Su,Roy Gan,Lucy Liang,Hao Wang,Qian Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Wall-OSS-0.5，一个4B参数的开源VLA模型，验证了VLA预训练本身即可产生可执行的机器人行为，而不仅仅是更好的微调初始化。模型在20+具身上预训练，采用梯度桥接共训练策略，在零样本场景下即可完成多项真实机器人任务，微调后性能显著优于基线π_0.5（提升17.5%），且动作训练不损害视觉-语言能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答一个基础性问题：VLA预训练本身是否能产生可执行的机器人行为，还是仅仅为下游策略学习提供更好的初始化。

Method: 基于3B VLM骨干网络构建4B VLA模型，增补动作生成组件；采用梯度桥接共训练策略，联合优化三个目标：离散动作预测（提供强VLM梯度）、多模态预测（保持视觉-语言理解）、连续流匹配（部署时动作接口）；在20+具身、超过100万条机器人轨迹上预训练。

Result: 零样本下可在17个任务套件上完成多项真实机器人任务（包括一个保留的变形操作任务）；微调后在15个真实机器人任务上达到60.5%平均任务进度，超越π_0.5达17.5%；同时保持广泛视觉-语言能力，增强具身接地能力。

Conclusion: VLA预训练可被视为直接可测试且已有用的机器人能力来源，而不仅仅是初始化策略；动作训练不会侵蚀固有的视觉-语言能力。

Abstract: Large-scale Vision-Language-Action (VLA) pretraining is increasingly adopted as the foundation for robot policies, yet the evidence for pretrained VLAs is almost invariably reported after task-specific fine-tuning. This leaves a foundational question unanswered: does VLA pretraining itself yield executable robot behavior, or does it merely furnish a better initialization for downstream policy learning? We present Wall-OSS-0.5, an open-source 4B VLA built upon a 3B VLM backbone augmented with action-generation components, designed so that pretrained robotic capability is directly measurable on physical hardware. The model is pretrained across more than 20 embodiments, processing over one million robot trajectories per epoch alongside a grounded multimodal corpus. We adopt a gradient-bridged co-training recipe in which three objectives play distinct and complementary roles: discrete action prediction routes strong VLM-native gradients into the backbone, multimodal prediction preserves grounded vision-language understanding, and continuous flow matching serves as the deployment-time action interface. Before task-specific fine-tuning, the pretrained checkpoint achieves non-trivial zero-shot real-robot behavior, completing several tasks, including a held-out deformable manipulation task, at high task progress on a 17-task suite. After fine-tuning, the same checkpoint serves as a stronger adaptation prior, reaching 60.5% average task progress on 15 real-robot tasks and outperforming π_0.5 by 17.5%. Multimodal evaluations further confirm that action training does not erode grounded vision-language competence: the model preserves broad vision-language ability while strengthening embodied grounding. Together, these results reposition VLA pretraining from an initialization strategy to a directly testable, already useful source of robot capability.

</details>


### [148] [Enhancing Human-Likeness in Reinforcement Learning Agents via Hierarchical Macro Action Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30928)
*Usman Nizamani,M. Shaheer Luqman,Fawad Javed Fateh,Ali Shah Ali,Murad Popattia,M. Zeeshan Zia,Quoc-Huy Tran*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出分层宏动作量化框架（HiMAQ），通过两级向量量化将人类示范编码为宏动作，使强化学习智能体在保持高奖励的同时生成更接近人类的行为序列。在D4RL基准测试中，HiMAQ在人类相似度评分上优于非分层基线（MAQ），且成功率达到或超过以往RL方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习智能体虽然性能强劲，但以奖励驱动为主，其行为模式与人类存在显著差异，限制了可解释性和可靠性。因此需要一种既能最大化奖励又能与人类行为高度一致的类人智能体框架。

Method: 提出HiMAQ框架，采用层次化宏动作量化方法：第一级（低级）量化将输入动作映射到细粒度子动作簇；第二级（高级）量化将这些子动作簇聚合成动作簇，从而将人类示范编码为宏动作，引导RL智能体生成类人行为。

Result: 在D4RL基准测试上，HiMAQ在人类相似度评分上优于非分层基线MAQ，同时保持与先前RL智能体相当或更高的成功率。该改进可泛化至多种RL算法（IQL、SAC、RLPD）。

Conclusion: 分层宏动作量化是提升RL智能体类人行为的有效途径，在不牺牲任务性能的前提下显著增强了行为的人类相似性，具有跨算法的良好泛化能力。

Abstract: Human-like agents are a long-standing goal of artificial intelligence. Despite strong performance, most reinforcement learning (RL) agents remain reward-driven and often exhibit behaviors that differ from humans, limiting interpretability and reliability. In this work, we introduce a novel human-like RL framework that predicts action sequences closely aligned with human behaviors while maximizing rewards. Specifically, we encode human demonstrations into macro actions using a hierarchical macro action quantization approach (termed HiMAQ) consisting of two successive levels of vector quantization. The lower quantization level maps input actions to fine-grained subaction clusters, while the higher quantization level aggregates these subaction clusters into action clusters. Extensive evaluations on the D4RL benchmarks show that our hierarchical approach outperforms the non-hierarchical baseline (MAQ), achieving better human-likeness scores while maintaining comparable or better success rates than previous RL agents. The improvements generalize across integrations with various RL algorithms, namely IQL, SAC, and RLPD.

</details>


### [149] [RDGen: Demonstration Generation for High-Quality Robot Learning via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30957)
*Zijian Zhu,Menglin Zou,Zhuang Li,Yaojie Tu,Xinhai Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RDGen框架，利用强化学习策略作为结构化轨迹生成器，通过Sim-to-Real RL生成高质量机器人演示数据，替代昂贵的人工遥操作，实验表明RL生成的演示轨迹更平滑且下游VLA性能更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型性能受限于高质量机器人轨迹数据的可用性，而当前通过人工遥操作收集数据的方式劳动密集、成本高昂且难以扩展。
