We evaluate AURA on real-user interview transcripts using re-identification attacks carried out by web-search agents, along with a utility evaluation based on interviewee-profile facts, codebook facts, and the joint contextual utility grid. Our results show that AURA improves the privacy-utility frontier by using adaptive privacy scope to strengthen resistance to agentic re-identification and using a mask-reconstruct anonymization method to better preserve contextual utility under fixed privacy scope.

</details>


### [186] [TRACE: Task-Aware Adaptive Self-Evolving Agentic Jailbreaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30883)
*Churui Zeng,Weiwei Qi,Kedong Xiu,Tianhang Zheng,Chaochao Lu,Liang He,Zhan Qin,Kui Ren*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TRACE是一个针对LLM Agent的实用越狱框架，通过将恶意任务分解为无害子任务序列并用场景伪装，结合Q-learning驱动的迭代进化，实现对多种高级LLM Agent的成功越狱，最高达到100%绕过率和0.73平均成功分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM Agent能够规划、编写代码并端到端执行专家级攻击流程，带来了新的安全威胁。然而这一威胁被低估，因为安全对齐阻止LLM直接生成有害指令，且现有越狱方法无法一致地诱导Agent执行恶意操作。

Method: TRACE框架包含三个核心步骤：(1) 将恶意任务分解为多种子任务序列方案，选择显式有害子任务最少的序列；(2) 将剩余有害子任务嵌入任务感知场景（包含角色、环境、指令和启发式规则）进行伪装；(3) 通过Q-learning机制采样明确定义的转换动作，迭代进化场景以诱导Agent执行有害子任务。

Result: 在AgentHarm和AdvCUA基准上，TRACE在多种高级LLM Agent上持续优于现有越狱基线，达到最高100%绕过率和0.73平均成功分数。在受控的网络攻击实例中也验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: TRACE揭示了LLM Agent面临的新型安全威胁，表明现有安全对齐措施不足以防御针对Agent的复杂越狱攻击，强调了加强Agent安全防护的紧迫性。

Abstract: The rise of LLM agents introduces a new threat by enabling planning, coding, and even end-to-end execution of expert-level attack workflows. However, this threat remains underexplored and underestimated since (i) safety alignment prevents LLMs from directly generating harmful instructions, and (ii) most existing jailbreak methods cannot consistently induce agents to execute malicious operations. In this paper, we propose TRACE, a practical agentic jailbreaking framework to further reveal the risks of this threat surface. To conceal the malicious intent, TRACE decomposes a malicious task into multiple subtask sequences under different schemes and selects the sequence with the fewest explicitly harmful subtasks. TRACE then disguises the remaining harmful subtasks as benign-looking instructions by embedding them in task-aware scenarios with related roles, environments, directives, and heuristics. The scenarios are iteratively evolved through well-defined transformation actions, which are sampled by a Q-learning-inspired mechanism, for inducing the agent to execute on the harmful subtasks. Extensive evaluations on AgentHarm and AdvCUA show that TRACE consistently outperforms existing jailbreak baselines across multiple advanced LLM agents, achieving up to 100% bypass rate and 0.73 average success score. We also demonstrate the effectiveness of TRACE in controlled cyberattack instances. Our code and demos are available at https://github.com/ZJU-LLM-Safety/TRACE.git.

</details>


### [187] [A Core-Structure-Based Automated Analysis Tool for Commercial Virtualization Obfuscation Deobfuscation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30902)
*Wanju Kim,Seoksu Lee,Eun-Sun Cho*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 针对虚拟化混淆技术日益被恶意软件利用的问题，本文提出VMPredator工具，通过内存分析和跟踪分析自动提取语义单元，减少对虚拟机内部结构的依赖，实验表明可将混淆程序长度缩减约85%，并能将小规模程序完全恢复至与原程序语义相同的状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚拟化混淆是一种比传统混淆更强大的技术，正被越来越多地应用于恶意软件，导致分析人员需要投入大量精力和时间，现有工具无法处理多种形式的虚拟化混淆。

Method: 提出VMPredator工具，通过内存分析和跟踪分析等多种分析方法自动提取语义单元，同时最小化对虚拟机特定内部结构的依赖，以处理多样化的虚拟化混淆形式。

Result: 混淆程序长度减少约85%，小规模程序能够完全恢复至与原程序语义相同的状态。

Conclusion: VMPredator能有效处理多种形式的虚拟化混淆，显著缩减混淆程序体积并实现语义级还原。

Abstract: Virtualization obfuscation is a more powerful obfuscation technique compared to other obfuscation methods, and as it is increasingly being applied to malware, it demands significant effort and time from analysts. This study analyzes virtualization obfuscation and proposes a tool called VMPredator that automatically extracts semantic units. The proposed tool performs various analyses including memory analysis and trace analysis, while minimizing dependency on the specific internal structure of virtual machines in order to handle diverse forms of virtualization obfuscation that existing tools are unable to process. Experimental results demonstrate that the length of obfuscated programs was reduced by approximately 85%, and it was verified through validation that small-scale programs were fully restored to semantics identical to the original.

</details>


### [188] [Toward Accessible Mobile Money: A Voice-Driven, Biometrically Secured USSD Automation Framework for Visually Impaired Users](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31375)
*Sunday Ajayi,Babatunde Eric Olatunji,Eric Umuhoza*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Android的智能中间件，通过黑屏模式、生物识别PIN注入和无障碍服务，使视障用户能够独立、安全地完成USSD移动支付，将任务成功率提升至90%以上，交易时间缩短至12-15秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非洲地区视障用户在使用基于USSD技术的移动金融时面临可达性和安全性障碍：USSD系统不支持非视觉交互，用户需依赖第三方协助甚至输入PIN，导致欺诈风险增加、交易信心降低；现有屏幕阅读器与USSD不兼容，常造成会话超时。

Method: 提出一种Android智能中间件：利用Android无障碍服务自动化USSD交易，集成基于硬件的密钥库安全实现生物识别PIN注入，引入隐私保护的屏幕变暗机制（黑屏模式），并采用设备端自然语言解析实现独立语音移动金融访问。

Result: 任务成功率从65-75%提升至90%以上，交易完成时间从40-60秒缩短至12-15秒，同时感知安全性得到改善。

Conclusion: 该方案有效解决了视障用户在USSD移动支付中的可达性与安全性困境，为金融普惠提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Financial inclusion has expanded significantly across Africa through mobile money services delivered primarily via USSD technology. However, visually impaired individuals continue to face accessibility and security barriers when conducting financial transactions. Current USSD systems are not designed for non-visual interaction, forcing users to rely on third-party assistance even for PIN entry, thereby increasing fraud exposure and reducing transaction confidence. Although alternative assistive technologies such as screen readers exist, they are not compatible with USSD operations, often causing sessions to time out before the user can complete a transaction. This paper presents an Android-based intelligent middleware that automates USSD transactions, integrates biometric-secured PIN injection, and introduces a privacy-preserving screen-dimming mechanism: Blackout Mode. The system leverages Android Accessibility Services, hardware-backed Keystore security, and on-device natural language parsing to enable independent, secure voice-based mobile money access. We show that the proposed solution improves task success rates from 65-75% to more than 90% and reduces transaction completion time from 40-60 seconds to 12-15 seconds, while also improving perceived security.

</details>


### [189] [Free-Riding in the AI Economy: Demystifying Logic Flaws in x402-Enabled Payment Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30998)
*Shengchen Ling,Yihang Huang,Yuan Chen,Yajin Zhou,Lei Wu,Cong Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次对x402协议生态进行系统性安全分析，发现了签名语义缺口（跨资源替换攻击）和时间同步缺口（并发竞争导致服务重复）两类根本性漏洞，在AI推理场景中可实现最高100%的资源泄漏率，并提出了面向请求的签名和悲观状态锁定等缓解措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自主经济时代到来，x402协议成为机器间支付的事实标准，但同步HTTP请求与异步区块链最终性之间的状态同步问题带来了严重安全隐患，目前缺乏对其安全性的系统分析。

Method: 形式化定义了五项安全不变性（Security Invariants），对x402生态进行系统安全分析；针对官方SDK和线上部署进行漏洞验证；提出架构级缓解方案。

Result: 发现签名设计中存在语义缺口导致跨资源替换攻击（支付证明被移植到未授权上下文）；并发竞态条件导致概率性服务重复；在动态定价模型中攻击者可利用授权方案中的同步缺口迫使商家补贴计算成本，在线上中间件中实现高达100%的资源泄漏率。

Conclusion: x402当前实现未能强制执行事务原子性和密码学上下文绑定，存在系统性漏洞。建议采用面向请求的签名和悲观状态锁定来保障自主代理的金融轨道的安全。所有发现已披露给Coinbase和ThirdWeb。

Abstract: The agentic economy demands programmatic financial rails, positioning the x402 protocol as the de facto standard for machine-to-machine payments. However, bridging synchronous HTTP requests with asynchronous blockchain finality introduces profound state synchronization challenges. In this work, we perform the first comprehensive security analysis of the x402 ecosystem. By formalizing five Security Invariants, we reveal that current implementations fail to enforce transactional atomicity and cryptographic context binding, leading to systemic vulnerabilities. We identify a semantic gap in signature design enabling cross-resource substitution, where payment proofs are transplanted to other unauthorized contexts. Furthermore, we expose a temporal gap where concurrency race conditions allow probabilistic service duplication. In the AI inference domain, we demonstrate how dynamic pricing models are vulnerable to allowance overdrafts and infrastructure rate limits. We validate these vulnerabilities against official SDKs and live deployments. Specifically, we show that attackers can exploit the synchronization gap in dynamic authorization schemes to force merchants to subsidize compute costs, achieving a resource leakage ratio of up to 100% on production middleware. Finally, we propose architectural mitigations, advocating for request-bound signatures and pessimistic state locking to secure the financial rails of autonomous agents. All discovered issues have been disclosed to Coinbase and ThirdWeb.

</details>


### [190] [Thou Shall Not Pass: Gatekeeping Outbound TLS Connections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31020)
*Henrique B. Brum,Matteo Franzil,Riccardo Germenia,Salvatore Manfredi,Domenico Siracusa,Luis A. Dias Knob*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过分析5000万+ TLS握手，发现服务器采用最新TLS技术远快于指南更新，导致安全风险。为此提出TLSGatekeeper，一种实时网络工具，可在100 Gbps下透明监控TLS握手，每包仅增加671-795ns延迟，无需客户端修改即可执行安全策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管TLS广泛使用，但其安全性常因过时版本和错误配置而受损，且客户端因临时性、普遍性和服务器依赖性难以配置，用户面临非标准或不安全连接的风险。

Method: 在Fondazione Bruno Kessler收集两周内5000万+ TLS握手，针对四个TLS指南分析三个服务器选择参数；提出TLSGatekeeper，实时网络工具透明监控握手并基于组织策略报告不合规连接。

Result: 不安全/过时选项使用虽少但持续存在；服务器采用新技术远超指南更新速度；TLSGatekeeper可支持100 Gbps流量，每握手包增加671ns（TLS 1.3）和795ns（TLS 1.2）延迟。

Conclusion: TLSGatekeeper能在大规模部署中有效强制执行安全策略，无需客户端修改，且比下一代防火墙更灵活地定义不安全配置并保护端到端隐私。

Abstract: Despite the widespread use of Transport Layer Security (TLS), its security guarantees are frequently compromised by outdated versions and misconfigurations. To analyze this problem, we collected more than 50 million TLS handshakes over a two-week period at our research institution, Fondazione Bruno Kessler, and analyzed three server-selected parameters against the recommendations of four TLS guidelines. Our analysis shows that while the use of insecure or outdated options is minimal, it remains persistent. More importantly, servers are adopting the latest TLS advancements much faster than official guidelines can be updated to provide directives for them. These findings, combined with the difficulty of configuring TLS clients due to their ephemeral, ubiquitous and server-dependent nature, leave users vulnerable to non-standard or outright insecure connections. To address this, we present TLSGatekeeper, a real-time, network-based tool that transparently monitors handshakes, analyzes server parameters, and, based on organizational policy, reports non-compliant connections without requiring client-side modifications. Unlike Next-Generation Firewalls, TLSGatekeeper preserves end-to-end privacy by validating only handshakes, and offers greater flexibility in defining undesired configurations. Our evaluation shows that TLSGatekeeper sustains traffic rates of up to 100 Gbps while preventing insecure connections, with an average added processing delay of 671 ns (TLS 1.3) and 795 ns (TLS 1.2) per handshake packet, making enforcement feasible at scale.

</details>


### [191] [From Prompt Injection to Persistent Control: Defending Agentic Harness Against Trojan Backdoors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31042)
*Jiejun Tan,Zhicheng Dou,Xinyu Yang,Yuyang Hu,Yiruo Cheng,Xiaoxi Li,Ji-Rong Wen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了LLM智能体在本地工作空间中面临的多步特洛伊攻击威胁，并提出了基准测试ClawTrojan（攻击成功率95.5%）与防御系统DASGuard（通过追踪文本来源并移除不可信控制内容实现动态防御）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体从对话机器人演变为可读写文件、调用工具、跨会话复用工作空间的真实工作工具，这带来了新的攻击面：攻击者可将提示注入嵌入文件或工具输出中，通过多步特洛伊攻击范式实现持久化控制，而现有逐步骤检查的防御无法检测这种累积型威胁。

Method: 构建ClawTrojan基准测试（在OpenClaw风格模拟工作空间中使用GPT-5.4），并设计DASGuard防御系统——扫描敏感本地文件中的控制类文本，追溯其来源，移除非可信来源的控制内容，结合运行时攻击拦截与工作空间消毒提交。

Result: ClawTrojan在GPT-5.4上达到95.5%的攻击成功率，而现有的单轮提示注入攻击在相同模型上接近零成功率；DASGuard实现了强劲的动态防御效果。

Conclusion: 多步特洛伊攻击是LLM智能体面临的新型严重安全威胁，现有逐步骤防御不足，需要如DASGuard这样结合来源追踪与运行时阻断的动态防御方案。

Abstract: LLM agents are evolving from conversational chatbots to operational tools in real-world workspaces. In local agentic harnesses, an LLM can read and write files, call tools, and reuse workspace state across sessions. While such capabilities enhance utility, they also expose a new attack surface for attackers. Attackers can embed a prompt injection within a file or tool output. Agents may read this hidden instruction, store it, and execute it later. In this multi-step trojan attack paradigm, no individual step appears malicious on its own, but these steps can collectively turn untrusted text into persistent control content. However, existing defenses often inspect each step in isolation. As a result, they can block a clear harmful action, but fail to detect the earlier write operation that plants the backdoor. To reveal this threat, we introduce ClawTrojan, a benchmark designed to identify multi-step trojan attacks in local agentic harnesses. In an OpenClaw-style simulated workspace with GPT-5.4, ClawTrojan reaches a 95.5% attack success rate (ASR), while existing single-turn prompt-injection attacks produce near-zero ASR on the same model. To address this threat, we propose DASGuard, which scans control-like text in sensitive local files, traces its origin, and removes control content that does not originate from a trusted source. Our results show that DASGuard achieves strong dynamic defense by combining runtime attack blocking with sanitized commits to the workspace.

</details>


### [192] [EvoDefense: Co-Evolving Black-Box Defense with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31140)
*Yu Li,Yuenan Hou,Yingmei Wei,Yanming Guo,Chaochao Lu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出EvoDefense，一种基于经验引导的共进化黑盒防御范式，通过守卫LLM和经验记忆模块，在持续的攻击-防御进化循环中迭代优化防御策略，无需重新训练即可泛化到未见过的攻击类型和目标模型，在多个基准测试中取得显著防御效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黑盒防御方法依赖预定义的过滤启发式规则，难以泛化到未见过的攻击类型和目标模型架构，亟需一种能够自适应进化、无需重新训练的通用防御范式。

Method: EvoDefense包含三个核心组件：1）守卫LLM用于检测恶意查询；2）经验记忆模块用于积累之前交互中的防御知识；3）攻击-防御共进化循环，其中攻击生成器和守卫模型通过经验引导的优化迭代式地改进各自的攻击策略和防御策略。

Result: 在HarmBench、AdvBench和AlpacaEval上的实验表明，EvoDefense在7个流行模型和5种代表性LLM攻击下均取得一致强防御性能，同时保持有竞争力的通用能力。在HarmBench上，AutoDAN-turbo对Gemini-3-flash和LLaMA-3-8B-Instruct的攻击成功率分别从29.4%和43.4%降至8.4%和6.2%。

Conclusion: EvoDefense通过经验引导的共进化机制有效解决了黑盒防御的泛化性问题，在不需重新训练的前提下实现了对未见攻击和目标模型的鲁棒防御，为LLM黑盒安全防御提供了新范式。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) remain highly vulnerable to diverse attacks, particularly in black-box settings where the internals of target models are inaccessible. Existing black-box defenses typically rely on pre-defined filtering heuristics, which often fail to generalize to unseen attack types and target model architectures. We introduce EvoDefense, an experience-guided co-evolving black-box defense paradigm. EvoDefense employs a guard LLM to detect malicious queries and an experience memory module to accumulate defense knowledge from previous interactions. At the core of EvoDefense is a continuous attack-defense evolution loop, where an attack generator and the guard model iteratively refine their attack strategies and defense policies through experience-guided optimization. This design enables EvoDefense to generalize across unseen attacks and target models without retraining. Experiments on HarmBench, AdvBench, and AlpacaEval show that EvoDefense achieves consistently strong defense performance across seven popular models and five representative LLM attacks, while preserving competitive general capabilities. On HarmBench, EvoDefense reduces the attack success rate (ASR) of AutoDAN-turbo on Gemini-3-flash and LLaMA-3-8B-Instruct from 29.4% and 43.4% to 8.4% and 6.2%, respectively.

</details>


### [193] [MAECO-Lite: Modular Ontology for Dynamic Malware Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31199)
*Zekeri Adams,Peter Švec,Ján Kľuka,Roderik Ploszek,Monday Onoja,Štefan Balogh,Martin Homola*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过本体论分析揭示了MAEC和STIX在动态恶意软件行为表示中的本体论不匹配问题，并提出了轻量级本体MAECO-Lite，其模块化结构清晰区分持久性实体与运行时事件，实验表明该本体显著提升了描述逻辑概念学习的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有标准（MAEC和STIX）在描述动态恶意软件行为时结构复杂且混淆了持久性工件与运行时事件的本体论区分，限制了执行轨迹推理能力。

Method: 基于统一基础本体论（UFO）对MAEC和STIX核心构造进行本体论分析，发现工件、倾向性与运行时事件之间的混淆问题；据此设计MAECO-Lite轻量级本体，采用模块化结构，区分样本、进程、动作、系统工件和ATT&CK技术等实体。

Result: MAECO-Lite在描述逻辑概念学习任务中显著优于原始标准，表明本体论驱动的建模能同时提升语义清晰度和计算可用性。

Conclusion: 本体论分析能够揭示安全标准中的概念混淆问题，基于本体论原则设计的简化表示可在保持语义精度的同时提升机器学习效果。

Abstract: Capturing dynamic malware behavior in a practical but still semantically precise manner remains a significant challenge in cyber threat intelligence. While standards such as MAEC and STIX provide widely adopted vocabularies for describing malware artifacts and observations, they represent data with considerable complexity in structures that often obscure important ontological distinctions. In particular, they tend to conflate enduring malware artifacts with the events generated during execution, thereby flattening distinctions that are central in foundational standards for ontology design. In this paper, we conduct a foundational ontological analysis of core MAEC and STIX constructs relevant to dynamic malware analysis relying on Unified Foundational Ontology (UFO) as a theoretical lens. Our analysis reveals some ontological mismatches arising from the conflation of artifacts, dispositions, and runtime events in MAEC and STIX that complicate coherent representation of dynamic malware behavior and, from a practical perspective, limit the ability to reason about execution traces. Based on these insights, we propose MAECO-Lite, a lightweight ontology designed to represent data and operationalize their processing for dynamic malware analysis. The ontology adopts a modular structure centered on samples, processes, actions, system artifacts, and MITRE ATT&CK Techniques, while maintaining a clear separation between enduring entities and runtime events. An initial evaluation using description logic concept learning algorithms shows that the simplified ontology significantly improves learning performance, demonstrating that ontologically grounded modelling can enhance both semantic clarity and computational usability.

</details>


### [194] [BadBone: Backdoor Attacks Against Backbone Models in Visual Prompt Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31246)
*Ziqing Yang,Rui Wen,Xinlei He,Yun Shen,Michael Backes,Yang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出BadBone，一种针对提示学习的后门攻击方法，通过双层优化在预训练骨干模型中植入后门，使得仅采用提示学习的下游任务继承该漏洞。实验表明该方法在保持模型效用性的同时实现了高攻击性能，且现有六种主流防御方法均无法有效检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提示学习作为一种新的机器学习范式被广泛采用，但其安全漏洞（尤其是后门攻击）尚未被充分探索。现有后门攻击主要针对提示学习过程本身，而本文旨在攻击预训练骨干模型，使得采用提示学习的下游任务继承后门漏洞，从而实现更隐蔽的攻击。

Method: 采用双层优化（bi-level optimization）方法，在预训练骨干模型中植入后门。攻击不直接修改提示学习过程，而是通过优化使骨干模型携带后门，只有当下游任务使用提示学习时才会触发后门行为。

Result: 在三个不同模型和三个不同领域数据集上的实验表明，目标与非目标后门攻击均实现高攻击性能，同时保持了预训练和下游任务的效用性。针对Neural Cleanse、ABS、MNTD、NAD、CLP、D-BR六种防御方法的评估显示，这些防御对BadBone后门模型基本无效。

Conclusion: BadBone揭示了提示学习范式下的新型安全威胁，现有防御手段难以应对。有效的防御策略仍有待未来研究探索。

Abstract: Prompt learning is a new machine learning paradigm that has attracted ample attention due to its simplicity and proven efficacy. Despite its growing adoption, the security vulnerabilities associated with this paradigm remain underexplored. In this work, we take the first step to propose BadBone, a stealthy and adaptive backdoor attack against prompt learning using bi-level optimization. Instead of backdooring the prompt learning process, we aim to compromise a backbone model such that only target downstream tasks employing prompt learning inherit the backdoor vulnerability. Extensive experiments on three different models and three datasets from various domains show that our targeted/untargeted backdoored models achieve high attack performance while maintaining utility on both pre-training and downstream tasks. Moreover, we evaluate our approach against six state-of-the-art model-level defenses, including Neural Cleanse, ABS, MNTD, NAD, CLP, and D-BR. The results demonstrate that these defenses are largely ineffective against our backdoored models and thus leave the effective defense as an important direction for future work.

</details>


### [195] [GETA: Generalized Encrypted Traffic Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31277)
*Ransika Gunasekara,Rahat Masood,Salil Kanhere*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: GETA是一个协议无关的加密流量分析框架，将网络流建模为多变量时间序列，结合元学习、嵌入精炼和自注意力机制，在少量标注数据下实现跨域少样本自适应，在9个公开数据集上超越现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统流量分析（如深度包检测）因加密和隐私保护协议的广泛采用而失效；现有基于机器学习的加密流量分析方法通常依赖协议特定的头部特征、需要大量标注数据，且在不同网络环境中泛化能力差。

Method: GETA将网络流建模为仅使用流量元数据的多变量时间序列，结合元学习（meta-learning）、嵌入精炼（embedding refinement）和自注意力（self-attention）机制，实现协议无关的少样本跨域自适应。

Result: 在9个公开数据集上（涵盖应用识别、VPN流量分类、IoT设备指纹识别和攻击检测），GETA一致性地优于现有的最先进基线方法。

Conclusion: GETA为现代加密网络中的鲁棒流量分析提供了一种实用且可泛化的基础框架。

Abstract: Traditional traffic analysis is being fundamentally challenged by the rapid adoption of encryption, tunnelling, and privacy-preserving protocols, which increasingly obscure packet payloads and limit the usefulness of Deep Packet Inspection (DPI). Although machine learning has advanced encrypted traffic analysis, existing approaches often remain tied to protocol-specific header features, depend on large labelled datasets, and degrade when deployed across heterogeneous network environments. We present GETA, a protocol-agnostic framework for encrypted traffic analysis that models network flows as multivariate time series using only traffic metadata, thereby avoiding reliance on packet payloads or header semantics. GETA combines meta-learning, embedding refinement, and self-attention to support few-shot adaptation to previously unseen domains with minimal labelled data. Across nine public datasets spanning application identification, VPN traffic classification, IoT device fingerprinting, and attack detection, GETA consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. These results show that GETA offers a practical and generalisable foundation for robust traffic analysis in modern encrypted networks.

</details>


### [196] [MeshGuard: MUD-Based Network Access Control for Large-Scale Thread-Powered IoT Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31326)
*Dominik Roy George,Wouter van Hoof,Habib Mostafaei,Savio Sciancalepore*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MeshGuard是一个框架，通过扩展MLE协议和利用SDN技术，在复杂的Thread网络环境中实现基于MUD的访问控制，支持多个异构边界路由器，具备线性可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MUD标准不支持Thread网络中的受限IoT设备，且先前扩展方案仅适用于单一边界路由器的简单拓扑，无法扩展到具有多个异构边界路由器的实际部署场景。

Method: MeshGuard扩展MLE协议以在任意拓扑下将MUD信息从受限设备传递到边界路由器，并利用SDN跨所有路由器同步访问控制列表。

Result: 在nRF5340、nRF52833和Raspberry-Pi 3上的概念验证实验表明，MeshGuard相比现有方法安全性增强、开销极小且具有线性可扩展性。

Conclusion: MeshGuard成功将MUD访问控制扩展到复杂、多边界路由器的Thread网络，解决了现有方案的可扩展性瓶颈。

Abstract: The IETF standard Manufacturer Usage Description (MUD) enables manufacturers to equip IoT devices with certified URLs that provide traffic profiles for those devices, helping administrators enforce network access control. However, MUD assumes devices operate on full IP stacks and therefore does not account for constrained IoT devices running Thread--the dominant low-power mesh networking standard--which lacks complete TCP/IP functionality. While prior work proposes extensions to support MUD in Thread environments, these approaches are limited to simple topologies with a single border router and do not scale to realistic deployments with multiple, heterogeneous border routers. We introduce MeshGuard, a framework enabling MUD-based access control in complex Thread networks, with any number of border routers. MeshGuard extends the Mesh Link Establishment (MLE) protocol to deliver MUD information from constrained devices to border routers regardless of network topology. Moreover, MeshGuard leverages Software-Defined Networking (SDN) to synchronize access control lists across all routers. Experiments on our proof-of-concept with real devices (nRF5340, nRF52833, Raspberry-Pi 3) demonstrate enhanced security, minimal overhead, and linear scalability compared to state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [197] [When Entropy Is Not Enough: Multi-Modal Classification of Encrypted and Compressed Data Fragments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31337)
*Fabio De Gaspari,Dorjan Hitaj,Samuele Salaris,Luigi V. Mancini*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Triumvir，一种多模态、不确定性感知的集成架构，通过融合统计、序列和空间三种表示，显著提升了短字节片段（512-2048字节）的加密与压缩数据识别准确率，在二分类和多分类任务上分别比SOTA方法提升+4.5pp和+6.4pp。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖单模态表示，假设单一数据视图足以准确分类，但在低信息场景（短数据片段）下这一假设成为根本性局限，导致加密与压缩片段的区分效果不佳。

Method: 提出Triumvir，一种多模态、不确定性感知的集成架构，融合原始字节片段的统计、序列和空间三种表示模态，通过集成学习实现分类与不确定性估计。

Result: 在二分类任务上比SOTA提升+4.5pp，多分类任务提升+6.4pp；消融实验证实多模态组合是关键，相比部分配置提升高达+5pp。

Conclusion: 多模态融合策略在短数据片段识别任务中显著优于单模态方法，验证了结合多种表示对提升低信息场景下分类性能的必要性和有效性。

Abstract: Reliable identification of encrypted data fragments is essential in cybersecurity, with applications to ransomware detection, digital forensics, and large-scale data analysis. Distinguishing encrypted from compressed fragments is particularly challenging, as short fragments lack structural data and exhibit low statistical redundancy. Traditional statistical methods based on byte-level distributions show limited effectiveness on this task. Recent machine learning approaches improve performance by learning subtle patterns from raw bytes, but predominantly rely on single-modal representations, implicitly assuming that a single view of the data is sufficient for accurate classification. This paper shows that this assumption becomes a fundamental limitation in low-information settings, when only small fragments of data are available (512--2048 Bytes). We propose Triumvir, a multi-modal, uncertainty-aware ensemble architecture that integrates statistical, sequential, and spatial representations of raw byte fragments. Extensive experimental analysis demonstrates that Triumvir consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods with gains of up to +4.5pp in binary and +6.4pp in multiclass classification. Ablation studies confirm that combining modalities is critical, yielding improvements of up to +5pp over partial configurations.

</details>


### [198] [Stateful Online Monitoring Catches Distributed Agent Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31593)
*Davis Brown,Samarth Bhargav,Arav Santhanam,Kasper Hong,Ivan Zhang,Matan Shtepel,Steffi Chern,Alexander Robey,Eric Wong,Hamed Hassani*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次提出分布式智能体攻击，通过多账户协同隐藏恶意目标以逃避安全监控；同时提出基于实时聚类的在线状态监控器，在跨账户聚合弱可疑信号时有效检测分布式攻击，Pareto优于标准监控器，且对正常用户延迟影响极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全监控器仅对单智能体上下文评分，无法检测跨账户聚合可见的分布式滥用。攻击者可利用多账户分散恶意任务，使每个对话记录看似无害，从而规避检测。

Method: 构建首个分布式智能体攻击框架（多智能体脚手架），将复杂网络安全任务拆分为多个子任务，各子智能体上下文受限，隐蔽恶意目标。防御方面，开发在线状态监控器，利用实时聚类收集跨多智能体对话记录的弱可疑信号，仅在对语言模型标记跨账户滥用时进行升级。

Result: 该分布式攻击使标准监控器仅能捕获传统攻击1/5的频率。在线状态监控器在大型模拟数据中心流量中Pareto优于标准监控器，早30%捕获分布式攻击，在到达最有害阶段前标记网络滥用，且对~99%正常用户流量延迟几乎无增加。红队测试后发现防御还能捕获标准越狱攻击。

Conclusion: 检测优势在大规模正常背景流量下仍存在但会缩小。结果表明需要一类新的安全监控器——从用户组而非孤立对话视角进行推理——以应对分布式滥用威胁。

Abstract: Language models can find thousands of severe software vulnerabilities, and agents are increasingly being misused for cyberattacks. To avoid detection, attackers frequently distribute their misuse, splitting a harmful task across many user accounts so each individual transcript looks benign. Because safety monitors score only one agent context at a time, they are structurally blind to misuse that is only visible in aggregate, across many accounts. We show this gap is real by building, to our knowledge, the first distributed agent attack, a multi-agent scaffold that completes hard cybersecurity tasks while hiding the harmful objective across subagents with limited contexts, evading a standard monitor that catches it only a fifth as often as prior agent attacks. Towards a defense, we develop an online stateful monitor that uses real-time clustering to collect weak suspiciousness signals across many agent transcripts, and escalates only rarely to a language model that flags misuse across user accounts. In evaluations with large-scale simulated datacenter traffic, our monitor Pareto dominates standard monitors, catching distributed attacks 30% earlier and flagging cyber misuse before it reaches the most harmful stages. Crucially, this comes at negligible additional latency for ~99% of user traffic. This detection advantage persists but narrows as the benign background traffic grows very large. After an extensive red-teaming exercise, we improve the defense and surprisingly also find that it catches standard jailbreaks, since adaptive attackers reuse attack variants across accounts. Our results point toward a new class of safety monitors which reason over groups of users rather than isolated transcripts.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [199] [What makes an action sequence enjoyable to watch?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30864)
*Jean-Peïc Chou,Kristine Zheng,Junyi Chu,Maneesh Agrawala,Judith E. Fan*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过生成Flappy Bird风格的游戏视频，发现观众更喜欢观看玩家完成更困难障碍物序列的视频，而危险感（玩家随时可能失败的不确定性）并不能预测 enjoyment 评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究为什么有些复杂动作序列（如体育比赛）比其他序列更令人愉悦观看，即识别影响观看 enjoyment 的关键因素。

Method: 生成了24个Flappy Bird风格游戏视频，操纵难度（玩家平均成功频率）和 moment-to-moment 不确定性（每一步玩家 crash 的可能性）。864名参与者分别从 enjoyment、难度感知或危险性感知三个维度对视频进行评分。

Result: 参与者更偏好玩家完成更困难障碍物序列的视频；危险感（轨迹的危险性）不能预测 enjoyment 评分。

Conclusion: 程序化生成的刺激可以分离影响动作序列观看 enjoyment 的因素，其中难度是关键预测因子，而 moment-to-moment 的不确定性（危险性）作用有限。

Abstract: People often seek out ways to watch others perform complex action sequences (e.g., sports). What makes some sequences more enjoyable to watch than others? We generated 24 video clips of gameplay from a Flappy Bird-style video game. Clips varied in difficulty (how often players succeeded on average) and in moment-to-moment uncertainty (how likely the player was to crash at any given step). Participants (N=864) rated each video on one of three dimensions: how much they enjoyed it, how difficult the level appeared, or how dangerous the player's trajectory appeared. We found that participants preferred videos where the player seemed to be completing more difficult obstacle courses, but dangerousness did not predict enjoyment ratings. These findings show how procedurally generated stimuli can isolate the factors that affect how enjoyable an action sequence is to watch.

</details>


### [200] [TUX: Measuring Human--AI Tacit Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30930)
*Yueshen Li,Hanyi Min,Vedant Das Swain,Koustuv Saha*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过引入“心照不宣的理解指数”（TUX）和基于Wavelength游戏的频谱放置任务，系统研究了人类与LLM之间在没有明确目标、沟通或反馈情况下的隐性对齐能力。实验表明，人类与LLM的隐性对齐受人格特征结构的影响，而非随机相似性，且个体特质、决策风格和信心等丰富预测变量能更有效地解释TUX差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人机对齐评估多聚焦于明确任务成功、准确率或奖励优化，忽略了协作中普遍存在的隐性理解（tacit understanding）——即在没有清晰目标、沟通或反馈的情况下，智能体能否与人类的评价立场或表征先验对齐。本文旨在填补这一空白，系统测量和解释人类与LLM之间的隐性对齐能力。

Method: 受社交派对游戏Wavelength启发，设计频谱放置任务，让人类和基于profile条件化的LLM智能体独立将概念放置于主观频谱上。提出TUX（Tacit Understanding Index）作为成对相似性度量。招募241名人类参与者及200个profile条件化的LLM智能体（涵盖4种模型），通过回归分析探索人格特质、决策风格、信心水平等对TUX的解释力。

Result: 人类-LLM在特质空间中最接近的配对具有显著更高的TUX，表明隐性对齐由个体层面特征结构决定。回归分析显示，随着预测变量集更丰富（人格特质、决策风格、信心等），TUX的可解释性增强，优于仅使用聚合特质距离的基线模型。

Conclusion: 人类与LLM之间的隐性理解是可测量的，但基于profile条件化的方法在捕捉深层表征对齐方面存在局限性。隐性对齐在结构上受人格特征驱动，而非随机相似性，这为设计更具隐性协作能力的人机交互系统提供了新的评估框架。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) increasingly act as collaborative partners, human--AI alignment is often evaluated through explicit task success, accuracy, or reward optimization. Yet many collaborative settings depend on tacit understanding: whether an agent can align with a human's evaluative stance or representational priors without clear objectives, communication, or feedback. To study this capacity, we develop a spectrum-placement task inspired by the social party game Wavelength, in which humans and agents independently place concepts along subjective spectra. We operationalize the Tacit Understanding Index (TUX) as a pairwise measure of similarity between human and agent judgments, and evaluate it with 241 human participants and 200 profile-conditioned LLM agents across four models. We find that nearest human--agent pairs in trait space achieve significantly higher TUX, suggesting that tacit alignment is structured by person-level characteristics rather than random similarity. Regression analyses show that TUX becomes more explainable as predictor sets become richer, with individual traits, decision-making styles, and confidence improving over aggregate trait-distance baselines. These findings suggest that tacit understanding between humans and LLMs is measurable, while revealing the limits of profile-based conditioning for capturing deeper representational alignment.

</details>


### [201] [From Statistics to Individuals: An Exploration of Zoomable Empathic Visualizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31026)
*Edwige Chauvergne,Arnaud Prouzeau,Martin Hachet,Pierre Dragicevic*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了可缩放共情可视化（ZEVs），一种允许用户在抽象统计可视化与聚焦个体的定性表现之间平滑导航的交互式体验。通过三个案例和定性用户研究，ZEVs促进了更深层次的理解和情感参与，揭示了数据背后的个体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据可视化虽然能有效传达统计信息，但在表现人群时往往隐藏了个体。作者希望弥合抽象统计与个体经验之间的鸿沟，让用户既能从宏观视角理解数据，又能共情地接触数据背后的真实个体。

Method: 提出了ZEVs（可缩放共情可视化）这一交互式可视化范式，设计了三个应用案例，并通过定性用户研究评估其效果。

Result: 定性用户研究表明，ZEVs能够促进更深层次的理解和情感参与，同时也指出了需要改进和进一步完善的领域。

Conclusion: ZEVs为揭示数据背后的个体提供了新的方法路径，有望推动可视化设计向更具共情力和人文关怀的方向发展。

Abstract: Data visualization is a powerful tool for conveying statistical information, but when representing populations, it tends to hide individuals. We introduce Zoomable Empathic Visualizations (ZEVs), interactive experiences allowing users to smoothly navigate between abstract statistical visualizations and more qualitative, relatable representations focused on individuals. We present three use cases of ZEVs and report on a qualitative user study that highlights opportunities for deeper understanding and emotional engagement, while pointing to areas for improvement and further refinement. In summary, ZEVs point toward new approaches for revealing the individuals behind the data.

</details>


### [202] [Extending the UXR Point of View Playbook: Triangulating Insights in Complex Developer Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31104)
*Sarah Kianfar*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文针对用户研究(UXR)领域从数据收集到建立有说服力的观点(POV)的转型挑战，提出了UXR POV Playbook的扩展方案，通过云开发者工具领域的多方法研究，引入三张新的"Playbook Cards"来帮助研究者将技术发现转化为商业叙事。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着UXR领域日趋成熟，从业者面临从单纯的数据收集向具有战略影响力的观点(POV)转型的挑战，需要系统化的方法论支持这一跃升。

Method: 基于云开发者工具领域（涵盖AI Agent、命令行界面、错误消息）的广泛多方法研究，采用定性与定量数据三角验证的方式构建高置信度的POV，并提炼出三张新的Playbook Cards。

Result: 提出了三种新的Playbook Cards：The Paradigm Shift（范式转变）、Explainability as Trust（可解释性即信任）、The Cost of Friction（摩擦成本），为研究者提供从复杂技术发现到商业叙事的结构化转化机制。

Conclusion: 本文通过创新性的Playbook Cards扩展了UXR POV Playbook，有效解决了从数据到战略观点转换的痛点，为UXR从业者提供了可操作的工具来提升研究的商业影响力。

Abstract: As User Experience Research (UXR) matures, practitioners face the challenge of moving beyond data collection toward establishing a compelling Point of View (POV) that drives strategic impact. This paper proposes an extension to the UXR POV Playbook, specifically focusing on the transition from the "Insight Generation" layer to the "POV" layer. Drawing on extensive multi-method research in Cloud Developer Tools, spanning AI Agents, Command Line Interfaces (CLI), and Error Messages, we demonstrate how triangulating qualitative and quantitative data facilitates the creation of high-confidence POVs. We introduce three new "Playbook Cards" derived from this research: The Paradigm Shift, Explainability as Trust, and The Cost of Friction. These cards provide a structured mechanism for researchers to translate complex technical findings into irrefutable business narratives.

</details>


### [203] [Generative AI in developing User Experience Research Point of View: A NotebookLM case study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31125)
*Mona Giff,Stephen Giff,Huseyin Dogan*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了一种利用Google NotebookLM增强UXR PoV（观点）框架的形式化方法论，包含五个提示词和四个阶段，在11篇UXR论文上测试表明NotebookLM能够有效协作生成PoV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UXR领域正面临身份危机，缺乏方法论基础且传统方法耗时过长，难以跟上产品决策节奏；同时GenAI在UXR中的应用导致研究人员工作强度增加（提示工程、数据清洗、输出验证耗时）。

Method: 提出一个形式化的五提示四阶段方法论：(1)利用框架、(2)建立路线图、(3)应用最佳实践、(4)构建PoV叙事；在11篇UXR论文上测试Google NotebookLM的有效性。

Result: NotebookLM成功地在所有PoV创建阶段利用了UXR PoV框架，证明了其在提供充分上下文和特定提示时可作为有效的协作伙伴。

Conclusion: 只要提供足够的上下文和具体的提示词，NotebookLM可以作为UXR中有效的协作工具，帮助研究人员从原始数据过渡到证据驱动的战略性产品观点。

Abstract: User Experience Research (UXR) is currently undergoing a transition from traditional usability testing towards design-led and data-driven approaches, yet it faces an identity crisis due to a lack of methodological grounding in UXR and time-intensive methodologies which often lag behind product decision cycles. To address this, the UXR Point of View (PoV) framework formalises the UXR process by transitioning from raw data collection to forming an evidence-based PoV which drives strategic product impact. Furthermore, the use of GenAI in UXR has been investigated, but researchers often face increased work intensity when using GenAI, attributed to time spent on prompt engineering, data cleaning, and verification of AI outputs. This paper proposes and evaluates a formalised methodology for leveraging GenAI, specifically Google's NotebookLM, to augment the UXR PoV process. The methodology consists of five prompts across four stages: (1) leveraging the framework, (2) establishing roadmaps, (3) applying best-practices, and (4) crafting PoV narratives; and was tested on eleven UXR papers. Results showed that by using the proposed methodology, NotebookLM successfully leveraged the UXR PoV framework across all stages of PoV creation. These findings demonstrate that NotebookLM can serve as an effective collaborative partner in UXR, so long as it is provided with sufficient context and specific prompting.

</details>


### [204] [UXR PoV for Neuroinclusive Emotion Regulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31131)
*Melike Akca,Mona Giff,Deniz Cetinkaya,Huseyin Dogan,Stephen Giff*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种生成式AI增强的用户体验研究方法（UXR），结合DBT、SDT和COM-B理论框架，通过四阶段流程和十张UXR Play Cards，为成人ADHD患者设计神经包容性、情绪智能的数字情绪调节干预系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数字和AI情绪调节干预系统存在理论整合薄弱、对神经多样性包容不足、缺乏结构化用户体验研究方法论等问题，无法有效弥合心理学洞察与设计实践之间的鸿沟。

Method: 基于UXR Point of View (PoV) Playbook，提出生成式AI辅助的四阶段UXR方法：AI支持假设生成、基础规划、通过构建模块进行洞察生成、构建利益相关者特定的PoV叙事。整合DBT、SDT和COM-B理论，利用生成式AI作为协同分析工具支持综合、假设形成和设计表达。

Result: 生成十张理论驱动的UXR Play Cards，将心理学机制和实证发现转化为可操作的设计指南，形成可复制的、具有偏误意识的生成式AI与UXR实践整合框架。

Conclusion: 本研究的主要贡献是提供了一个可复制的、具有偏误意识的框架，用于将生成式AI整合到UXR实践中，推动以人为中心和神经包容性的数字心理健康设计方法发展。

Abstract: Attention-deficit/hyperactivity disorder (ADHD) is a psychiatric disorder which presents itself in individuals through patterns of developmentally inappropriate levels of inattentiveness, hyperactivity, and impulsivity, with difficulties in decision making and emotional regulation (ER). Although digital and AI-based interventions have expanded access to ER support, many existing systems remain limited by weak theoretical integration, insufficient accommodation of neurodiversity, and a lack of structured user experience research (UXR) methodologies, that bridge psychological insight with design practice. This paper introduces a Generative AI-augmented UXR methodology, grounded in the UXR Point of View (PoV) Playbook, to support the design of emotionally intelligent and Neuroinclusive digital ER interventions for adults with ADHD. The approach integrates empirical evidence with established psychological frameworks Dialectical Behaviour Therapy (DBT), Self-Determination Theory (SDT), and the COM-B behavioural model and leverages Generative AI as a co-analytic tool to support synthesis, hypothesis formation, and design articulation. The methodology is operationalized through a four-stage UXR process encompassing AI-supported hypothesis generation, foundational planning, insight generation via Building Blocks, and the construction of stakeholder-specific PoV narratives. This process results in a set of ten theory informed UXR Play Cards that translate psychological mechanisms and empirical findings into actionable design guidance. The primary contribution of this work is a replicable, bias-aware framework for integrating Generative AI into UXR practice, advancing human-centred and Neuroinclusive approaches to digital mental health design.

</details>


### [205] [Developing an AI-Powered UX Research Point of View for Digital Health in A Regulatory Context: An Exemplar Case from MSM and Transgender HIV Care in Nigeria](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31138)
*Emmanuel Oluwatosin Oluokun,Festus Fatai Adedoyin,Huseyin Dogan,Nan Jiang,Melike Akca,Abiodun Adedeji,Olumuyiwa Ayorinde,Fatima Ahmad Muazu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文针对尼日利亚MSM和跨性别HIV/AIDS感染者这一弱势群体，提出了一种生成式AI增强的用户体验研究方法论，通过四阶段UXR流程和十张理论驱动的Play Cards，将心理学机制与实证发现转化为可操作的设计指南，形成可复制的、 stigma感知的、以隐私为中心的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字健康平台在服务弱势群体时，缺乏能够充分考量其心理社会状况的理论驱动的用户体验研究方法论，导致实际效果受限。

Method: 基于UXR PoV Playbook，结合协同设计工作坊、主题分析和需求工程，构建了包含AI支持的假设生成、基础规划、Building Blocks洞察生成和利益相关者PoV叙事构建的四阶段流程，产出十张理论驱动的UXR Play Cards。

Result: 产出了十张理论驱动的UXR Play Cards，将心理机制和实证发现转化为可操作的设计指导，每张卡片包含具体任务、AI增强方法和针对边缘人群的伦理保障。

Conclusion: 核心贡献是提供了一个可复制的、stigma感知的、以隐私为中心的负责任GenAI使用框架，推动面向边缘人群的人本数字健康设计。

Abstract: User Experience Research (UXR) in a legal and regulatory contexts presents unique challenges that require specialised approaches to protect vulnerable populations whilst generating actionable insights. Digital consultation, appointment booking, and medication delivery platforms show promise for extending care access; however, their real-world effectiveness is curtailed by an absence of theoretically grounded user experience research (UXR) methodologies that adequately account for the psychosocial conditions of these populations. This paper introduces a Generative AI-augmented UXR methodology, grounded in the UXR Point of View (PoV) Playbook, to guide the design of psychologically safe, low-cognitive-load digital health interventions for MSM and transgender individuals living with HIV/AIDS in Nigeria. Drawing from empirical research involving co-design workshops, thematic analysis, and requirements engineering, the methodology is operationalised through a four-stage UXR process encompassing AI-supported hypothesis generation, foundational planning, insight generation via Building Blocks, and the construction of stakeholder-specific PoV narratives. This process results in ten theory-informed UXR Play Cards that translate psychological mechanisms and empirical findings into actionable design guidance. Each play contains actionable tasks, AI-augmented approaches, and ethical guardrails tailored for research with marginalised populations. The output is a set of ten theory-informed UXR Play Cards translating psychological insight and empirical evidence into actionable design guidance. The core contribution is a replicable, stigma-aware, and privacy-centred framework for responsible GenAI use in UXR practice, advancing human-centred digital health design for marginalised communities.

</details>


### [206] [Extending the UXR Point of View Pyramid: A Generative AI-Augmented Methodology for Human-Centred AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31143)
*Festus Fatai Adedoyin,Huseyin Dogan,Melike Akca,Abiodun Adedeji*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文针对英国债务管理场景，将传统的用户体验研究（UXR）观点金字塔扩展为AI增强的方法论框架，通过形式化AI增强的观点金字塔、结构化提示架构和AI驱动的手牌卡系统，在保持可追溯性和伦理监督的前提下，将生成式AI嵌入UXR工作流程，用于高风险的金融AI系统设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 英国家庭债务和生活成本压力上升，AI金融技术在信贷评估、还款结构和债务支持服务中扮演越来越重要的角色，但这些系统面临监管约束、算法不透明和高脆弱性风险。现有UXR观点框架并非为AI中介的金融系统设计，无法充分处理可解释性、公平性和问责制等核心问题。

Method: 本文扩展了UXR观点金字塔，构建了AI增强的方法论框架，具体包括：(1) AI增强的观点金字塔（AI-Augmented PoV Pyramid），(2) 用于综合分析和假设生成的结构化提示架构（structured prompt architecture），(3) AI驱动的手牌卡系统（AI-enabled Playbook Card system），将生成式AI嵌入UXR工作流，同时保持可追溯性和伦理监督。生成式AI被定位为认识论支持机制，而非分析权威，需经过人工验证和监管意识审视。

Result: 提出了一个针对高风险的金融AI环境的UXR方法论框架，以债务管理技术（包括可负担性评估、还款规划和财务压力预测系统）为应用场景进行验证，推动了负责任的、AI驱动的UXR实践发展。

Conclusion: 本文为CHI社区贡献了面向高风险的金融AI环境的UXR方法论演进，将生成式AI作为受人类验证和监管约束的认识论支持工具，而非替代人类分析权威，为负责任的人本AI债务管理技术提供了方法论基础。

Abstract: Rising household debt and cost-of-living pressures in the United Kingdom have intensified the role of AI-driven financial technologies in mediating credit assessment, repayment structuring, and debt support services. These systems increasingly shape consequential financial decisions, yet they operate within complex socio-technical environments characterised by regulatory constraint, algorithmic opacity, and heightened vulnerability risk. User Experience Research (UXR) Points of View (PoVs) are critical in translating heterogeneous research evidence into strategic direction for product and governance decisions. However, the existing UXR PoV framework was not designed for AI-mediated financial systems where interpretability, fairness, and accountability are central. This paper extends the UXR PoV pyramid into an AI-augmented methodological framework for Human-Centred AI debt management technologies in the UK financial services context. We formalise (1) an AI-Augmented PoV Pyramid, (2) a structured prompt architecture for synthesis and hypothesis generation, and (3) an AI-enabled Playbook Card system that embeds Generative AI into UXR workflows while preserving traceability and ethical oversight. Generative AI is positioned not as an analytic authority, but as an epistemic support mechanism subject to human validation and regulatory awareness. By grounding the framework in debt management technologies, including affordability assessment, repayment planning, and financial stress prediction systems, this work advances UXR methodology for high-stakes financial AI environments and contributes to the evolution of responsible, AI-powered UXR practice within the CHI community.

</details>


### [207] [From Evidence to Design: Developing an AI-Augmented UX Research Point of View for Digital Wellbeing in Emergency and Public Safety Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31146)
*Olumuyiwa Ayorinde,Huseyin Dogan,Festus Adedoyin,Nan Jiang,Emmanuel Oluokun,Abiodun Adedeji,Melike Akca*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究探讨如何将用户体验研究方法与AI支持的分析相结合，为急救和公共安全人员的数字健康干预提供更清晰的设计方向。通过UXR PoV框架和AI辅助文献分析，识别了心理、行为和设计模式，并整合行为改变技术与说服技术原则，最终产出了UXR PoV金字塔、九张UXR游戏卡和以利益相关者为中心的PoV叙事。研究发现，有效的健康系统必须最小化认知负担、适应运营环境并优先考虑心理安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 急救和公共安全人员在高压、轮班制环境中工作，认知疲劳和不可预测的日程导致他们难以参与传统的健康工具，因此亟需针对这一特殊群体的数字健康干预设计方法。

Method: 采用UXR Point-of-View（PoV）框架，结合AI支持的文献分析流程，识别反复出现的心理、行为和设计模式，并整合行为改变技术（BCT）和说服技术原则进行解释。

Result: 产出了UXR PoV金字塔、九张UXR游戏卡以及以利益相关者为中心的PoV叙事。

Conclusion: 针对EPSP的有效健康系统必须最小化认知负担、适应运营环境并优先考虑心理安全；AI可以辅助大规模证据解读，但人类研究者仍需负责情境判断和设计方向决策。

Abstract: This paper investigates how User Experience Research (UXR) methods can be combined with AI-supported analysis to develop clearer design direction for digital wellbeing interventions targeting Emergency and Public Safety Personnel (EPSP). EPSP work in high-stress, shift-based environments where cognitive fatigue and unpredictable schedules reduce engagement with conventional wellbeing tools. Using the UXR Point-of-View (PoV) framework, this study applied an AI-supported literature analysis process to identify recurring psychological, behavioural, and design patterns. Behaviour Change Techniques and Persuasive Technology principles were integrated throughout interpretation to connect evidence with practical design reasoning. The process resulted in a UXR PoV Pyramid, nine UXR Play Cards, and stakeholder focused PoV narratives. Findings show that effective wellbeing systems for EPSP must minimise cognitive effort, adapt to operational context, and prioritise psychological safety. The work demonstrates how AI can assist large-scale evidence interpretation while human researchers maintain responsibility for contextual judgement and design direction.

</details>


### [208] [Developing a Culturally Grounded, AI-Augmented UX Research Point of View (POV): An Exemplar Case Study from Telemedicine Dementia Care](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31147)
*Abiodun Adedeji,Huseyin Dogan,Festus Adedoyin,Michelle Heward,Melike Akca,Emmanuel Oluwatosin Oluokun,Fatima Ahmad Muhazu,Olumuyiwa Ayorinde*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过尼日利亚家庭照护者远程医疗痴呆症护理框架 TeleDeCa 的案例，展示了一种结合混合方法研究、假设生成、本体建模和生成式AI的文化敏感型UXR观点构建方法，并提炼了可复用的Play Cards以扩展UXR POV Playbook。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管UXR POV在行业实践中广泛使用，但缺乏明确记录其构建过程的公开示例，尤其是在文化敏感和低资源场景下。

Method: 基于UXR POV Playbook和金字塔框架，结合混合方法研究、假设生成、本体建模，并引入生成式AI作为有边界的研究协作者，支持综合、假设探索和叙事构建，同时保留人类判断、伦理责任和文化敏感性。

Result: 成功构建了TeleDeCa的文化 grounded、AI增强的UXR POV，并提炼出可复用的Play Cards，可作为CHI 2026工作坊的示范材料。

Conclusion: 本文展示了在无需完全成熟的系统或验证结果的情况下，如何构建具有说服力的、文化敏感且AI增强的UXR POV，扩展了现有方法论框架。

Abstract: User Experience Research (UXR) Points of View (POVs) distil complex and often fragmented research evidence into actionable perspectives that guide how teams interpret user needs, frame design decisions, and align stakeholders. Although POVs are widely used in industry practice, there are few published examples that explicitly document how POVs are constructed, particularly in culturally sensitive and low-resource contexts. This paper presents an exemplar case study demonstrating how a culturally grounded, AI-augmented UXR POV was developed to inform TeleDeCa, a telemedicine dementia care framework for family caregivers in Nigeria. Building on the UXR POV Playbook and pyramid framework, we illustrate how mixed-methods research, hypothesis generation, and ontology-based modelling can be combined to form a defensible POV without requiring a fully finalised system or validated outcomes. Generative AI (GenAI) is integrated across the UXR POV framework as a bounded research collaborator, supporting synthesis, hypothesis exploration, and narrative construction while preserving human judgment, ethical accountability, and cultural sensitivity. The contribution of this paper lies in the extraction of reusable Play Cards and a Play that extend the UXR POV Playbook and serve as exemplar material for the CHI 2026 workshop on developing AI-powered UXR POVs.

</details>


### [209] [Developing a UXR Point of View for Cognitive Accessibility in Mobile Learning with Generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31149)
*Fatima Ahmad Muazu,Festus Adedoyin,Huseyin Dogan,Abiodun Adedeji,Melike Akca,Olumuyiwa Ayorinde*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种结合用户体验研究（UXR）原则与大语言模型（LLM）支持分析的方法，通过四阶段流程（基础结构构建、结构化验证、洞察整合、视角表达）来改善面向认知障碍学习者的移动学习系统需求质量，最终形成了认知可访问性UXR剧本，将可用性和参与度挑战归因于需求模糊而非界面设计问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认知障碍学习者在移动学习系统中面临诸多可用性和参与度挑战，而这些挑战往往源于需求定义不明确或不充分，而非界面设计本身。现有方法缺乏将认知可访问性原则嵌入可测量、可技术追踪的需求的系统化路径。

Method: 以UXR PoV金字塔为方法论框架，分为四个阶段：1) 基础结构构建（心理学、行为学、设计层面）；2) 结构化验证（使用DeLone和McLean信息系统成功模型与质量功能展开QFD）；3) 洞察整合（开发9张认知可访问性UXR游戏卡）；4) 利益相关者特定视角表达。全程集成LLM辅助综合（主题聚类、需求精炼、假设形成），但保持人工监督。

Result: 发现许多可用性和参与度挑战源于模糊或规定不足的需求，而非界面设计问题；提出了认知可访问性UXR剧本，为理论、系统架构和利益相关者策略的对齐提供了结构化路径。

Conclusion: 通过将认知可访问性原则嵌入可测量且可技术追踪的需求中，能够有效改善面向认知障碍学习者的移动学习系统质量，弥合理论与系统架构、策略之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: This study investigates how UX research (UXR) principles, combined with Large Language Model (LLM)-supported analysis, can be used to improve the quality of requirements for mobile learning systems designed for learners with cognitive disabilities. Using the UXR Point-of-View (PoV) pyramid as a methodological framework, the study progressed through four stages: foundational structuring of psychological, behavioral, and design layers; structured validation using the DeLone and McLean Information Systems Success Model and Quality Function Deployment (QFD); insight consolidation through the development of nine Cognitive Accessibility UXR Play Cards; and stakeholder-specific PoV articulation to support interdisciplinary communication. LLM-supported synthesis was integrated to assist in theme clustering, requirement refinement, and hypothesis formulation under human oversight. Findings suggest that many usability and engagement challenges in mobile learning originate from ambiguous or under-specified requirements rather than interface design alone. By embedding cognitive accessibility principles into measurable and technically traceable requirements, the proposed Cognitive Accessibility UXR Playbook provides a structured pathway for aligning theory, system architecture, and stakeholder strategy.

</details>


### [210] [Personalized to Persuade: The Effects of Contextualization and Warmth on Trust and Reliance in Conversational AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31275)
*Mert Yazan,Suzan Verberne,Frederik Bungaran Ishak Situmeang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过2x2被试间实验（N=380）发现，AI助手的个性化解释（情境化）会降低其说服力，但与对话温暖感结合时可通过交叉交互恢复说服力；用户对AI的依赖普遍存在且不受对话设计影响；AI素养高的用户虽信任度更低，但反而更易被说服并更依赖AI建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在日常任务中（用户常缺乏先验知识），AI的个性化解释（情境化）与对话温暖感如何影响用户对AI的说服力感知和依赖程度，尤其是在AI与人类专家建议相悖的情境下。

Method: 采用2x2被试间实验设计（N=380），操纵情境化（有/无）和对话温暖感（高/低）两个变量，测量AI助手的说服力、用户依赖程度及信任等指标。

Result: 情境化降低了AI的说服力，但与温暖感结合后通过交叉交互恢复说服力；用户对AI的依赖在所有条件下均存在且不受对话设计影响；信任强烈预测说服和依赖，但情境化和温暖感并非通过信任起作用；AI素养高的用户报告更低信任，但更易被说服且更依赖AI建议。

Conclusion: 用户倾向于服从AI代理而非人类专家判断，但界面层面的对话设计选择对行为的影响有限。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) agents personalize their responses by tailoring explanations to users' backgrounds, interests, and prior interactions, referred to as contextualization. Personalization has been identified as a persuasive strategy in politics or in marketing. However, the persuasive effect of contextualization in everyday tasks, where users often lack prior knowledge, remains unclear. We conducted a $2\times2$ between-subjects experiment ($N = 380$) examining how contextualization, combined with conversational warmth, shapes reliance and persuasiveness of an AI assistant arguing against expert recommendations. Our findings reveal that contextualization reduces the persuasive power of AI, but its combination with warmth restores persuasiveness through a crossover interaction. Reliance on AI is present across conditions and is invariant to the conversational design. Trust strongly predicts both persuasion and reliance, yet neither contextualization nor warmth operates through trust. AI literacy decouples trust from behavior: more literate users report lower trust in the assistant, yet are more persuaded and more reliant on its advice. These results suggest that users are prone to deferring to AI agents over human expert judgment; however, interface-level conversational design choices have a limited role in shaping the behavior.

</details>


### [211] [A Focus of Attention-Based Virtual Training Platform for Pre-Prosthetic Myoelectric Skill Acquisition: A Proof-of-Concept Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31332)
*Xiaochen Zhang,Sigrid Dupan*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于虚拟现实的预假肢训练平台，结合EMG驱动光标与动画手势，通过内外部注意力焦点协议进行训练。实验表明两种注意力焦点均有助于肌电技能习得与保持，其中外部注意力焦点与技能保留正相关，为设计更有效的假肢训练方案提供了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有肌电假肢训练范式过度强调内部肌肉激活，忽视了真实世界中目标导向的外部结果反馈存在不足，导致训练与现实使用之间存在鸿沟。

Method: 开发了一个虚拟训练平台，集成EMG驱动光标与动画手势，提供肌肉级和功能级双重反馈。采用两种注意力焦点协议（内部FoA vs 外部FoA），进行概念验证研究。

Result: 两种协议下参与者均成功习得并保留了肌电技能，但表现出不同的性能特征和学习策略。外部FoA与技能保留呈正相关。

Conclusion: 基于注意力焦点的虚拟训练平台在预假肢应用中是可行的，两种FoA均对学习有实质贡献，且其时机安排可能至关重要。外部FoA有助于强化训练与技能习得之间的联系。

Abstract: Advances in myoelectric prosthetic technology have substantially increased the functional potential of modern devices. Accordingly, heightened control demands have led to the acknowledgement of pre-prosthetic training as a key stage in the acquisition of myoelectric skills. Existing training paradigms largely emphasize internal muscle activation while external, goal-directed outcomes required for effective real-world use are often neglected. We address this gap by introducing a virtual pre-prosthetic training platform that integrates EMG-driven cursor with animated hand gestures, enabling the delivery of both muscle-level and functional-level feedback. In this proof-of-concept study, participants were assigned to one of two focus of attention (FoA) protocols, each incorporating both feedback types but differing in whether internal or external FoA was emphasised. Participants successfully acquired and retained myoelectric skill across both protocols, but distinct performance characteristics and learning strategies emerged, indicating that both FoAs contribute meaningfully to learning and that their timing may play an important role. External FoA was positively associated with retention, suggesting that it may strengthen the link between training and skill acquisition. Together, the results demonstrate the feasibility of an FoA-based virtual training platform for pre-prosthetic applications and indicate that it can provide a foundation for designing training protocols that better prepare users for prosthetic use.

</details>


### [212] [Appropriateness of Empathy in AI: A Signal-Cost Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31340)
*Chi-Ching Juan,Tao Wang,Harold Lee*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文从经济学视角引入信号理论，提出基于信号成本代理（情感丰富度、观点采择、情境定制）的多维框架，用于评估AI共情的适度性，而非仅关注共情是否存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多聚焦于量化AI中的共情，但极少探讨共情是否在语境中适度——过度共情显得操控，不足则显得冷漠，亟需一个评估共情适度性的系统性框架。

Method: 将信号理论应用于人机对话，提出信号成本代理（情感丰富度、观点采择、情境定制），分别映射到情感共情、认知共情和联想共情三个维度，构建多维评估框架。

Result: 提出了一个能够系统性评估AI共情适度性的多维框架，评估标准从“是否存在共情”转向“共情是否相对于用户需求适度”。

Conclusion: 信号成本代理框架为评估AI共情的语境适度性提供了新的经济学视角，有助于避免共情不足或过度的问题。

Abstract: The appropriateness of empathy in AI has emerged as a critical concern, as excessive empathy risks seeming manipulative while insufficient empathy appears dismissive. While prior research has explored how to quantify empathy in AI, few studies examine whether such empathy is contextually appropriate. This paper introduces an economic perspective by applying signaling theory to human-AI conversations. We propose Signal Cost Proxies (emotional richness, perspective-taking, and contextual tailoring) mapped to affective, cognitive, and associative empathy. This multidimensional framework enables systematic evaluation of empathy not just by presence, but by its appropriateness relative to user demand.

</details>


### [213] [Can Generative AI help people navigate Radical Moral Disagreements? The CONSIDER prototype](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31574)
*William Hohnen-Ford,Sarah Chen,Kathryn B. Francis,Madeline G. Reinecke,Ilina Singh,David Lyreskog*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出CONSIDER原型工具，利用LLM生成对立观点，通过结构化分歧帮助用户在激进道德分歧（RMDs）中澄清价值观，以应对社会极化及公众心理健康问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激进道德分歧（RMDs）导致社会极化日益严重，对公众心理健康造成可测量的成本；现有工具难以帮助人们有效应对这类高强度、分裂性的分歧。

Method: 基于Mill关于分歧的认识论价值理论，设计CONSIDER工具，通过LLM生成对立观点，引导用户进行结构化分歧互动，实现价值澄清。

Result: 提出了CONSIDER的原型设计逻辑，并分析了此类工具可能带来的潜在风险。

Conclusion: CONSIDER为使用AI工具导航RMDs提供了初步方案，但需谨慎评估其风险，以指导未来开发方向。

Abstract: Radical Moral Disagreements (RMDs) are highly polarising topics that are increasingly censored in everyday life, with growing evidence suggesting that this polarisation carries measurable costs to public mental health. To address these challenges, some researchers have proposed Large Language Models (LLMs) as a means to support more democratic deliberation and better moral reasoning. Yet existing tools are poorly calibrated to help people navigate RMDs, because of their intense and divisive characteristics. This paper introduces CONSIDER, a prototype for a one-to-one AI tool for RMD navigation. Drawing on Mill's account of the epistemic value of disagreement, CONSIDER aims at value clarification through structured disagreement with an opposing LLM-generated opinion. We describe CONSIDER's design logic and analyse potential risks posed by such tools to guide future development.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [214] [SpecDB: LLM-Generated Customized Databases via Feature-Oriented Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31097)
*Yunkai Lou,Longbin Lai,Shunyang Li,Zhengping Qian,Ying Zhang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出 SpecDB，一个利用大语言模型按需合成定制化关系数据库的系统。通过将生产系统分解为10个功能模块，采用扩展的 FODA 特征模型（含 cooperate 边）构建依赖图 DBGraph，并利用多层模块构建流水线（含专门子代理和精炼代理）生成数据库。在 TPC-C 测试中，生成的数据库（23,779行 Rust 代码）在10个仓库下 tpmC=130，与 PostgreSQL（128）和 MySQL（127）相当，但代码量仅为它们的约3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流关系数据库在所有部署中提供统一的功能集，但单个工作负载仅使用其中一小部分子系统。本文旨在探索是否可以根据目标工作负载按需生成具有匹配功能集的数据库，以避免冗余功能带来的资源浪费。

Method: SpecDB 包含三个核心组件：(1) DBGraph——基于 FODA 特征模型扩展 cooperate 边构建的依赖图，捕获10个功能模块及其实现变体间的跨模块依赖；(2) 分层模块构建流水线——每个模块由专门子代理（含 Main、Tester、Architect 三个内部代理）生成、验证和集成，并由精炼代理迭代修复和调优；(3) 选择组件——将自然语言工作负载描述翻译为实现变体集合，形成端到端流水线。

Result: 在 TPC-C/BenchmarkSQL 测试中，生成数据库（23,779行 Rust）在1仓库和10仓库下的60分钟 TPC-C 测试中零错误。10仓库下 tpmC=130（PostgreSQL 为128，MySQL 为127），延迟相当，代码量仅为其约3%。

Conclusion: 由于代理在模块规范层面而非产品源码层面操作，原则上可以跨系统边界组合技术。伴随 LLM 成本下降，为目标工作负载生成定制数据库正变得简便可行。

Abstract: Mainstream relational databases ship a uniform feature set across deployments, although individual workloads exercise only a fraction of the available subsystems. We investigate whether a database can instead be generated on demand with a feature set matched to the target workload. We present SpecDB, a system that uses large language models (LLMs) to synthesize customized relational databases. We survey 9 production systems and decompose them into 10 functional modules, each further divided into implementation variants. To capture cross-module dependencies, including cases where implementations in disjoint subtrees must be co-designed, we adopt the FODA feature model and extend it with a cooperate edge, yielding a dependency graph DBGraph. SpecDB operationalizes DBGraph through a layered module-construction pipeline in which each module is generated, validated, and integrated by a dedicated subagent (driven by three inner agents: Main, Tester, Architect), and a Refining Agent that iteratively repairs and tunes the assembled database against a user-supplied refining harness with read-only access to existing database source code. A companion selection component translates a natural-language workload description into a set of implementation variants, providing an end-to-end pipeline from workload description to deployable database. We evaluate SpecDB on TPC-C with BenchmarkSQL. The generated database (23,779 lines of Rust) completes 60-minute TPC-C at 1 and 10 warehouses with zero errors. At 10 warehouses it reaches tpmC=130, compared to 128 for PostgreSQL and 127 for MySQL, with comparable latency at ~3% of their code size. Because the agent operates at module-specification level rather than product source, it can in principle combine techniques across system boundaries. Paired with falling LLM costs, generating a purpose-built database for a target workload is becoming straightforward.

</details>


### [215] [AIM: A practical approach to automated index management for SQL databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31406)
*Ritwik Yadav,Satyanarayana R. Valluri,Mohamed Zaït*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文介绍AIM（自动索引管理器），一个可配置的索引管理系统，能够为SQL数据库识别有影响力的二级索引以高效利用CPU、I/O和存储资源，已在数千个生产数据库上验证，具备快速收敛、减少对查询优化器依赖以及"无回归"保证等特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据库索引管理依赖人工或缺乏自动化、高效的工业级解决方案，需要一种能够自动识别有影响力的二级索引、适应工作负载变化并高效利用系统资源的索引管理系统。

Method: AIM采用端到端设计，具备快速收敛算法（即使对宽复合索引也有效）、减少对查询优化器的依赖、提供"无回归"保证，并为每个索引推荐提供基于度量的可解释性说明。

Result: 在数千个生产数据库上部署验证，实验表明AIM能够快速识别最有效的索引，生成的物理设计接近最优。

Conclusion: AIM是少数大规模部署在生产数据库上的工业级索引推荐引擎之一，能够自动适应工作负载变化，有效利用资源，并提供了可靠的无回归保障。

Abstract: This paper describes AIM (Automatic Index Manager), a configurable index management system, which identifies impactful secondary indexes for SQL databases to efficiently use available resources such as CPU, I/O and storage. It has been validated on thousands of databases which support production systems. With AIM, the physical design of the database adapts itself to the changes in the workload.We lay out the end to end design of AIM while calling out the guarantees and tradeoffs associated with our design choices. Some of the salient features of AIM include fast convergence even while recommending wide composite indexes, reduced reliance on the query optimizer and a "no regression" guarantee for production workloads. Each index recommendation from AIM is accompanied with a metrics driven explanation, making it easier to verify machine driven changes.AIM is one of the few industrial strength index recommendation engines that is deployed on production databases at a large scale. The experimental results show that AIM is quick in identifying the most effective indexes and the resulting physical design is close to optimal.

</details>


### [216] [A Theoretical Study of DBLog: Certified Virtual Cuts for a Snapshot-Equivalent Replay of Live Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31475)
*Andreas Andreakis*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文是2019年DBLog论文的理论后续，形式化了DBLog的"快照等价重放证书"概念，证明其无需物理快照即可构建快照等价的重放，并提供了Isabelle/HOL机器检查的证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2019年DBLog论文描述了一种无需表锁、无需暂停写入、无需全局读事务的CDC机制，但未形式化其正确性。本文旨在填补这一理论空白，给出DBLog正确性的严格形式化证明。

Method: 提出了"认证虚拟切割"（certified virtual cut）作为核心形式化对象：一个有限证据包，其认证重放在选定分界线和键范围上达到与源数据库相同的每键状态。在Isabelle/HOL中完成了机器检查的证明。

Result: 证明了每个良构的DBLog运行在其分界线和作用域上满足每键重放等价性；接受的证书配合忠实源观测可验证此类运行并产生虚拟切割；还证明了源端延续结果：在同一作用域上，已建立的切割可通过追加作用域过滤的忠实CDC段推进到更后的分界线。

Conclusion: 本文形式化了DBLog的理论基础，证明了其构建的虚拟切割在给定前提条件下等价于物理快照重放，为CDC系统的正确性提供了严格的理论保障。

Abstract: This paper is a theoretical follow-up to the 2019 DBLog paper, which introduced a change-data-capture (CDC) mechanism for both backfilling a downstream system from a live source database and streaming its ongoing changes, while the source keeps accepting writes. DBLog reads the table in primary-key range scans (chunks) interleaved with the source change log: watermarks locate each chunk in log order, chunk rows become refresh events, and CDC events repair stale chunk observations. The mechanism requires no table lock, no pause in writes, and no global read transaction, and has since been adopted by Debezium and Apache Flink CDC.
  The 2019 paper described the mechanism operationally but did not formalize its correctness object. This paper formalizes that object: DBLog constructs a snapshot-equivalent replay certificate without a single physical snapshot read.
  The central formal object is a certified virtual cut: a finite evidence bundle whose certified replay reaches the same per-key state as the source at a chosen frontier on a chosen key scope. A virtual cut is extensional: replay equality at a frontier, not a physical snapshot read, and does not assert one source timestamp across chunk rows. The paper proves per-key replay equality for every wellformed DBLog run at its frontier and scope, and that an accepted certificate, evaluated against faithful source observation, witnesses such a run and yields a virtual cut. It also proves a source-side continuation result: on the same scope, an established cut advances to later frontiers by appending the scope-filtered faithful CDC segment committed in between. Each result is Conditional on premises stated where the result appears. Whole-table correctness, exactly-once delivery, sink-state convergence, and transfer to named deployments are not consequences of certificate acceptance alone. All proofs are machine-checked in Isabelle/HOL.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [217] [Kernel Foundry: A Diagnosis-driven Evolutionary Kernel Optimizer with Multi-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30359)
*Zixuan Huang,Da Chen,Kecheng Huang,Lihao Yin,Xing Li,Huiling Zhen,Mingxuan Yuan,Zili Shao*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 提出Kernel Foundry，一个基于诊断驱动的进化框架，用于自动GPU内核优化。该方法结合专家引导的检索增强初始化与多岛屿进化搜索，通过结构化诊断反馈迭代优化内核，并在KernelBench上实现高达100%的正确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成高性能GPU内核需要同时保证正确性和硬件感知优化，现有LLM虽能生成代码但往往无法同时兼顾正确性和效率。

Method: 提出Kernel Foundry框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 专家引导的检索增强初始化；(2) 多岛屿进化搜索，利用结构化诊断反馈迭代优化候选内核；(3) 集中式经验库积累可复用的优化知识，并包含防作弊机制。

Result: 在KernelBench上，该方法在正确性和性能方面均持续优于强基线，在Level 2任务上达到100%的正确率。

Conclusion: 诊断驱动的进化框架能有效结合LLM的代码生成能力与结构化优化反馈，显著提升GPU内核的正确性与性能。

Abstract: Generating high-performance GPU kernels remains challenging due to the need for both correctness and hardware-aware optimization. While large language models (LLMs) show promise in code generation, they often fail to produce kernels that are both correct and efficient.
  We propose Kernel Foundry, a diagnosis-driven evolutionary framework for automatic GPU kernel optimization. Our method combines expert-guided, retrieval-augmented initialization with a multi-island evolutionary search, where candidate kernels are iteratively refined using structured diagnostic feedback. A centralized experience library accumulates reusable optimization knowledge to guide subsequent evolution, while explicit mechanisms prevent cheating behaviors that bypass kernel-level computation.
  Experiments on KernelBench show that our method consistently improves both correctness and performance over strong baselines, achieving up to 100% correctness on Level~2.

</details>


### [218] [Gradient-Free Training of Spiking Neural Networks via Low-Rank Evolution Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30361)
*Dhruv Patankar,Sachit Ramesha Gowda*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出EGGROLL方法，通过低秩分解进化策略扰动，将SNN训练每代内存从O(mn)降至O(r(m+n))，在N-MNIST上实现79.21%准确率，每代加速2.23倍，实现了无梯度、适合神经形态硬件的SNN训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SNN在神经形态硬件上具有能效优势，但其训练面临困难：替代梯度方法不兼容片上学习，而进化策略(ES)的计算成本随参数数量线性增长，对大权重矩阵不实用。

Method: 提出EGGROLL，一种低秩分解ES扰动方法，将每代内存复杂度从O(mn)降低到O(r(m+n))，结合LIF SNN在N-MNIST数据集上进行无梯度训练。

Result: N-MNIST测试准确率79.21%，每代墙钟时间相比全秩ES加速2.23倍，存在明确的准确率-速度权衡。

Conclusion: EGGROLL可用于SNN训练且性能可行，无需替代梯度即可兼容神经形态硬件上的片上学习。

Abstract: Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) offer compelling energy efficiency on neuromorphic hardware, yet their training remains challenging because the discrete spike threshold is non-differentiable. Surrogate-gradient methods sidestep this by approximating the derivative, but they impose backpropagation infrastructure that is incompatible with on-chip learning. Evolution Strategies (\es) are a natural gradient-free alternative, yet their computational cost scales with the number of parameters, making them impractical for large weight matrices.
  We present a method for training SNNs using EGGROLL, a low-rank factorisation of ES perturbations that reduces per-generation memory from $\mathcal{O}(mn)$ to $\mathcal{O}(r(m{+}n))$. Combining EGGROLL with a Leaky Integrate-and-Fire SNN on N-MNIST, we demonstrate that gradient-free training achieves 79.21% test accuracy while reducing per-generation wall-clock time by 2.23$\times$ relative to full-rank ES. Our results demonstrate EGGROLL is viable for SNN training, with a clear accuracy-speed tradeoff, compatible with training on neuromorphic hardware without surrogate gradients.

</details>


### [219] [Reinterpreting Safety Thresholds as Neuron Spiking Thresholds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30368)
*Enrico Del Re,Mohamed Sabry,Cristina Olaverri-Monreal*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出基于漏积分点火（LIF）神经元的脉冲神经网络（SNN）来重新解释替代安全措施（SSM）的阈值，将多个SSM输入融合并训练使其脉冲与人类刹车时刻对齐，实验表明该模型能捕捉阈值交叉无法解释的人类反应，且脉冲动力学可桥接客观SSM与主观安全感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SSM评估使用固定阈值，无法捕捉人类对持续临界状态或短暂高风险峰值的反应，需要一种能更好对齐人类安全感知的阈值模型。

Method: 将SSM阈值重新建模为LIF神经元的脉冲阈值，多个SSM输入组合成SNN，通过3D-CoAutoSim平台（CARLA/Unreal + 6自由度运动平台）采集的跟车实验数据训练网络，使其脉冲发射与人类刹车起始时刻对齐。

Result: 学习到的脉冲活动在多种场景下与刹车行为定性对齐，能捕捉阈值交叉无法一致解释的反应；参与者间学习到的输入阈值相对一致，而学习到的衰减因子编码了不同的时间敏感性。

Conclusion: 脉冲动力学可作为桥接客观SSM与主观人类安全感知的机制。

Abstract: Surrogate Safety Measures (SSMs) are extensively utilised in the evaluation of traffic risk in automated driving contexts. However, the majority of SSM-based evaluations employ fixed thresholds that fail to capture the human response to sustained borderline conditions or the reaction to brief, high-risk peaks. The present work proposes a biologically inspired reinterpretation of SSM thresholds. This is modelled as spiking thresholds of leaky integrate-and-fire (LIF) neurons, with multiple SSM inputs combined into a spiking neural network (SNN). The SNN is trained to emit spikes that are aligned with human braking onsets. The training data was recorded in a controlled car-following experiment using the 3D-CoAutoSim platform with CARLA/Unreal and a 6-DOF motion platform, where induced critical events were generated. The results demonstrate that the learned spiking activity qualitatively aligns with braking behaviour across scenarios and captures reactions that are not consistently explained by threshold crossings alone. Analysis across participants further indicates that learned input thresholds remain relatively consistent, while learned decay factors encode different temporal sensitivities for the SSMs. The findings of this study indicate that spiking dynamics may serve as a mechanism to facilitate the convergence of objective SSMs with subjective human safety perception.

</details>


### [220] [Updating the standard neuron model in artificial neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30370)
*Raul Mohedano,Thomas Batard,Erik Velasco-Salido,Ramsses De Los Santos Mendoza,Jorge H. Martínez,Stacey Levine,Marcelo Bertalmío*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 该论文提出用更真实的皮层细胞模型替代人工神经网络中传统的点神经元模型，在不增加参数数量的情况下，显著提升了网络的表达能力、鲁棒性、学习速度，同时减少了记忆化和训练数据需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人工神经网络自1950年代起一直沿用神经科学中过于简化的点神经元模型，而神经科学已证明该模型无法充分表征许多基本的神经过程，因此需要引入更真实的神经元模型来改善网络性能。

Method: 用最新的皮层细胞模型替换人工神经网络中的标准点神经元模型，在不增加参数数量的前提下，通过理论分析和实验验证其效果。

Result: 更真实的神经元模型使人工神经网络在表达能力、鲁棒性、学习速度方面得到提升，同时减少了过记忆化和对训练数据量的需求。

Conclusion: 采用更符合生物神经科学的神经元模型能显著改善人工神经网络的性能，为神经网络架构设计提供了新的方向。

Abstract: From their inception in the 1950s, artificial neural networks (ANNs) started using the so-called point neuron model then prevalent in neuroscience, hoping that this analogy would allow for a better emulation of brain function. Over the years the neuroscience literature has shown that the point neuron model is too simplistic to properly represent many fundamental neural processes; however, the standard neuron model in ANNs still remains the same. Here we substitute it by a very recent model of cortical cells and demonstrate through theoretical analyses and experimental results how, simply by using a more realistic neural unit element without augmenting the number of parameters, the resulting ANNs offer a number of important advantages that include increases in expressivity, robustness and learning speed, and a reduction in memorization and the amount of training data needed.

</details>


### [221] [From Mean-Field Limits to Semiclassical Concentration: Global Convergence of the Canonical Evolutionary Strategy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30371)
*Matías Neto,Nicolás Garay,Luis Martí,Nayat Sanchez-Pi*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出了规范进化策略（CES）作为受控数学框架，通过薛定谔型复制子-突变子方程的半经典极限分析进化算法的全局收敛性，建立了从离散个体动力学到确定性均场极限的严格层级，证明全局收敛由主算子的本征函数控制，并定义了“几何选择”机制，优先收敛到鲁棒平坦最优解，实现了无需额外数值启发式的全局收敛理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决随机连续优化中全局收敛性问题，为进化策略的全局收敛提供严格的理论基础，解释“最平坦者生存”现象，并克服共识驱动方法在全局最小值位于初始支持域外时过早方差坍塌的缺陷。

Method: 将规范进化策略（CES）建模为受控数学框架，通过薛定谔型复制子-突变子方程的半经典极限分析全局收敛；建立从离散个体动力学到确定性均场极限的严格层级；利用几何选择（Geometric Selection）机制，通过主算子本征函数优先选择鲁棒平坦最优解；复制子-突变子动力学实现内在质量传输。

Result: 在d=30的高维基准测试中，CES在偏移初始化场景下实现了更低的残差误差，而标准共识驱动和基于梯度的方法无法有效迁移。CES无需额外数值启发式即可实现全局收敛。

Conclusion: 通过从点态共识转向谱集中，该框架为进化策略的全局收敛提供了稳健的理论基础，揭示了全局收敛由本征函数主导的几何选择机制，为理解进化算法的全局优化行为提供了新的数学视角。

Abstract: We address the issue of global convergence in stochastic continuous optimization. For that purpose, we formulate the Canonical Evolutionary Strategy (CES) as a controlled mathematical framework to analyze global convergence in evolutionary algorithms via the semiclassical limit of a Schr{ö}dinger-type replicator-mutator equation. We provide a rigorous hierarchy from a discrete individual-based dynamics to a deterministic mean-field limit, demonstrating that global convergence is governed by the principal eigenfunction of the underlying operator. This property, defined as Geometric Selection, naturally prioritizes robust, flat optima over narrow local traps, offering a mathematical justification for the ''survival of the flattest'' phenomenon. Moreover, unlike consensus-driven methods that are prone to premature variance collapse when the global minimizer resides outside the initial support, the replicator-mutator dynamics of CES facilitate intrinsic mass transport. High-dimensional benchmarks (d = 30) confirm this advantage, showing that CES achieves lower residual errors in shifted initialization scenarios where standard consensus-driven and gradient-based methods fail to migrate effectively. By shifting the focus from point-wise consensus to spectral concentration, our framework provides a robust theoretical foundation for global convergence in Evolution Strategies (ES) without the need for additional numerical heuristics.

</details>


### [222] [Evolutionary Algorithm for Reservoir Learning and Yielding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30372)
*Julien Testu,Pierrick Legrand,Xavier Hinaut*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出EARLY框架，利用进化算法自动演化多储层回声状态网络的拓扑结构和超参数，在时序学习任务上优于随机搜索，且不同难度任务演化出不同的模块化架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典回声状态网络（ESN）需要针对特定任务手动调整架构和超参数，缺乏通用性和自动化能力，且单一储层结构在复杂时序任务上表达能力有限。

Method: 提出EARLY（Evolutionary Algorithm for Reservoir Learning and Yielding）框架，将多储层ESN架构编码为图结构基因组，通过交叉、变异和选择操作进化拓扑结构与超参数，受大脑模块化组织启发。

Result: 在CogScale数据集上，演化出的架构在多个时序任务上优于随机搜索；简单任务产生轻量级架构，复杂任务产生更丰富的模块化组织；跨情境学习数据集上验证了泛化能力。

Conclusion: 进化搜索能够自动发现可复用的储层结构，为更广泛的时序问题提供通用解决方案，且模块化程度与任务难度正相关。

Abstract: Reservoir computing, a type of recurrent neural network, is a promising approach for temporal learning as it separates dynamic processing from the trained readout layer. However, classical Echo State Networks (ESNs) often require task-specific tuning of their architecture and hyperparameters to achieve good performance. This paper introduces EARLY (Evolutionary Algorithm for Reservoir Learning and Yielding), a framework designed to evolve both the topology and hyperparameters of multi-reservoir ESNs. Inspired by the modular organisation of the brain, EARLY encodes architectures as graph-based genomes and applies crossover, mutation, and selection to discover effective configurations. Our goal is to create both generic architectures and tasks inducing generalization. The method is evaluated on temporal learning tasks from the CogScale dataset. Results show that evolved architectures outperform those obtained with random search on several tasks and exhibit structural differences depending on task difficulty: simpler tasks yield lightweight architectures, while more complex tasks favour richer modular organisations. These findings suggest that evolutionary search can help identify reusable reservoir structures for a broader range of temporal problems. The evolved architectures are further evaluated on a cross-situational learning dataset to assess their ability to adapt to new environments.

</details>


### [223] [GP-GOMEA with GPU-Based Fitness Evaluations: Design and Performance Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30954)
*Jasper Post,Johannes Koch,Anton Bouter,Tanja Alderliesten,Peter A. N. Bosman*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文首次提出了GP-GOMEA的完全GPU加速适应度评估方案，通过设计GPU友好的个体表示和评估策略，大幅提升了评估吞吐量。在四个符号回归基准测试中，该方法显著提高了性能，特别是在大数据集和大种群规模下。此外，该方案使得之前不可行的分析成为可能，首次实现了问题无关的进化算法在四小时内可靠回归最大的费曼方程之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GP-GOMEA作为符号回归的先进进化算法，虽然能发现小型可解释模型，但其计算成本过高，限制了在大数据集和复杂目标表达式上的应用。与此同时，深度学习的成功很大程度上归功于GPU的大规模并行能力。因此，本文旨在通过GPU加速解决GP-GOMEA的计算瓶颈问题。

Method: 设计了GPU友好的模板个体表示方法及相应的评估策略，利用种群搜索固有的并行性，将适应度评估完全迁移到GPU上执行。

Result: 在四个标准符号回归基准上，评估吞吐量提升数个数量级；在更大数据集和更大种群规模下性能显著提升；首次实现问题无关进化算法在四小时内可靠回归最大的费曼方程之一。

Conclusion: GPU加速使GP-GOMEA能够处理之前不可行的分析任务，揭示了表达式结构如何影响搜索难度，并显著扩展了进化算法在大规模符号回归问题上的应用能力。
